### [45] [EVFormer: An Egocentric Vision-EMG Bidirectional Attention Model for Bimanual Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06970)
*JiaCheng Ge,SiYu Zhang,ShengJie Li,XinTong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVFormer融合RGB帧与前置200ms双侧腕部sEMG，经跨模态双向交叉注意力与门控融合估计44个手部关节角，单被试初步研究中MAE为11.482°，优于单模态与晚融合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称双手姿态估计对虚拟交互、可穿戴控制与康复意义重大，但自遮挡、手-手接触和物体操作会严重降低纯视觉观测质量，因此需要引入视觉之外的信息源以提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出EVFormer多模态框架：输入当前RGB帧与前置200 ms双侧腕部sEMG；分别用独立编码器提取视觉空间特征与sEMG时序特征；通过顺序双向交叉注意力实现跨模态信息交换；采用特征级门控融合；回归44个手指与腕部关节角度。在单个公开EgoEMG同步录制数据上做单被试可行性验证，训练/验证/测试按时间顺序划分。

Result: 在296个测试样本上，EVFormer平均绝对误差11.482°，而纯视觉、纯sEMG、晚融合及训练均值基线为13.228–13.610°；相对纯视觉降低13.20%，相对晚融合降低14.23%；在5类手势中的4类取得最低误差。

Conclusion: 初步表明第一人称视觉与sEMG在特征级的交互可提升双手姿态估计精度；但结论受限于单被试单录制，需跨被试、跨会话、跨传感器位置及真实交互条件下的进一步验证以确认泛化性。

Abstract: Egocentric bimanual hand pose estimation is important for virtual interaction, wearable control, and rehabilitation, but visual observations are often degraded by self-occlusion, hand-hand contact, and object manipulation. We propose EVFormer, a multimodal framework that combines the current RGB frame with the preceding 200 ms of bilateral wrist surface electromyography (sEMG) to estimate 44 finger and wrist joint angles. EVFormer separately encodes visual spatial features and sEMG temporal features, enables cross-modal information exchange through sequential bidirectional cross-attention, and integrates the two modalities using feature-wise gated fusion. We evaluate EVFormer in a single-participant feasibility study using one synchronized public EgoEMG recording with chronologically separated training, validation, and test splits. On 296 test samples, EVFormer achieves a mean absolute error of 11.482 degrees, compared with 13.228-13.610 degrees for vision-only, sEMG-only, late-fusion, and training-mean baselines. This corresponds to relative error reductions of 13.20% compared with the vision-only model and 14.23% compared with late fusion. EVFormer also achieves the lowest error in four of the five evaluated gesture classes. These results provide preliminary evidence that feature-level interaction between egocentric vision and sEMG can improve bimanual hand pose estimation. Further evaluation across participants, recording sessions, sensor placements, and real-world interaction conditions is required to establish the generalizability of the approach.

</details>


### [46] [Uncertainty in Representation Learning on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06974)
*Yuqicheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究知识图谱嵌入（KGE）中的三类不确定性：知识不确定性、算法不确定性、预测不确定性，并提出模型无关、具理论保证的方法加以量化与缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管KGE在基准测试中表现优异，但其预测缺乏有原则的可靠性保证，限制了在高风险场景中的应用；且不确定性贯穿整个KGE流程（输入不完整/含噪、训练随机性、预测可靠性）。

Method: 针对算法不确定性：证明相同设置下训练的模型预测差异显著，提出基于投票的聚合框架以缓解不稳定；针对预测不确定性：将共形预测（conformal prediction）适配到KGE，构建具无分布覆盖保证的答案集，并扩展为谓词条件可靠性保证；针对知识不确定性：为带置信度评分的三元组开发统计有效的预测区间，并提出基于嵌入的方法近似统计本体上的概率推理，具备形式化可靠性保证。

Result: 提出一组互补、模型无关的方法，可提供无分布覆盖保证、谓词条件可靠性保证以及统计有效的预测区间与形式化可靠性保证，从而量化并缓解KGE中的不确定性。

Conclusion: 这些方法为KGE中的不确定性提供了实用且具理论基础的解决方案，推动知识图谱推理从单纯追求预测精度迈向可靠且不确定性感知的推理。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) methods represent entities and predicates in continuous vector spaces to infer missing knowledge. Despite strong benchmark performance, their predictions often lack principled reliability guarantees, limiting their use in high-stakes applications. Moreover, uncertainty arises throughout the KGE pipeline, from incomplete or probabilistic input knowledge to stochastic training and prediction. This thesis systematically investigates three sources of uncertainty in KGE: knowledge uncertainty, arising from incomplete, noisy, or probabilistic input knowledge; algorithmic uncertainty, induced by randomness in model training; and predictive uncertainty, concerning the reliability of model outputs. To address algorithmic uncertainty, the thesis demonstrates that models trained under identical settings can produce substantially different predictions and introduces a voting-based aggregation framework to mitigate this instability. To quantify predictive uncertainty, it adapts conformal prediction to KGE, constructing answer sets with distribution-free coverage guarantees and extending them to provide predicate-conditional reliability guarantees. To support reasoning under knowledge uncertainty, it develops statistically valid prediction intervals for confidence-scored triples and an embedding-based approach to approximate probabilistic reasoning over statistical ontologies with formal soundness guarantees. Together, these complementary, model-agnostic methods provide a practical and theoretically grounded approach to uncertainty in KGE, advancing beyond predictive accuracy toward reliable and uncertainty-aware knowledge graph reasoning.

</details>


### [47] [A Data-Driven Framework for Unsupervised Monitoring of Transmission Systems Using End-of-Line Testing Data: A Case Study at Ford Motor Company](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06980)
*Mohammad N. Bisheh,Mehrdad Moradi,Parinaz Farajiparvar,Colin Brady,Rajesh Gupta,Xueling Li,Javad Navaei,Milad Parvaneh,Kamran Paynabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段高维多元监测框架（预处理+非线性降维+控制图Phase I监测），在福特汽车制造真实产线上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感技术产生的高维数据具有复杂非线性模式和强时间依赖性。传统统计监测难以捕捉非线性结构；终端检测（EoL）中的方法依赖预设阈值和启发式规则，难以发现高维时序中的异常特征；而深度学习/生成式AI虽预测能力强，却因数据采集成本高、需可解释、低延迟、计算高效且便于非技术人员使用而不适用。

Method: 两阶段多元监测框架：第一阶段预处理，剔除不完整与非信息样本并对时间序列做时间对齐；第二阶段进行非线性降维，随后通过控制图Phase I监测流程实现异常检测。框架支持无监督与有监督两种设置（取决于训练时是否有真值标签），模块化结构便于跨领域与按运营需求适配。

Result: 在福特汽车公司真实生产数据上评估，准确率、召回率、F1从既有模型的0.50、0.30、0.429提升至0.625、1.00、0.769。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持可解释、低延迟、计算高效且易用的前提下，有效应对高维时序数据的非线性与强时序依赖，检测性能显著优于企业现有模型，具备良好的模块化可迁移性。

Abstract: Sensing technologies have advanced rapidly across industries ranging from energy to automotive manufacturing. These systems generate high-dimensional (HD) data characterized by complex nonlinear patterns and strong temporal dependencies. Traditional statistical monitoring methods are often limited in their ability to capture such nonlinear structure. Likewise, many analytical approaches used in End-of-Line testing rely on predefined thresholds and heuristic rules, which restrict their ability to detect informative anomaly signatures in HD temporal data. In contrast, while modern deep learning and generative AI models offer strong predictive capabilities, they are often unsuitable in applications where data are costly to collect and where the monitoring system must remain interpretable, low-latency, computationally efficient, and usable by non-technical practitioners. To overcome these limitations, we propose an advanced multivariate monitoring framework for HD data. The framework operates in two stages. In the first stage, the data are preprocessed to remove incomplete and non-informative samples and to temporally align time series data. In the second stage, nonlinear dimensionality reduction is performed, followed by anomaly detection through a control chart based phase I monitoring procedure. The framework can be used in both unsupervised and supervised settings, depending on the availability of ground truth labels during training. Moreover, its flexible and modular structure allows practitioners to adapt its components to different domains and operational requirements. We evaluate the proposed framework on real production data from an automotive manufacturing environment at Ford Motor Company. The proposed method achieves higher accuracy, recall, and F1 score than the company's existing model, improving these metrics from 0.50, 0.30, and 0.429 to 0.625, 1.00, and 0.769, respectively.

</details>


### [48] [An Information-Theoretic Evaluation Framework for Benchmark and Model Diagnosis in Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06988)
*Houru Jiang,Zixi Wang,Tengteng Cheng,Xueyi Li,Mingliang Hou,Jiaqi Zheng,Renqiang Luo,Teng Guo,Zitao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息论的KT基准诊断框架，用CTW构建因果不确定性坐标，将预测投影到该坐标上分熵带评估模型表现，揭示模型改进的局部非均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识追踪（KT）模型主要用AUC、准确率等聚合指标评估，这类全局分数掩盖了剩余错误的来源，也无法判断基准是否接近饱和。在真实KT场景中估计全局理论上限很困难，但局部可预测性是可以量化的，因此需要一个能揭示局部建模失败与真实增益的诊断工具。

Method: 提出信息论评估框架：在项目-作答历史和当前项目查询上使用上下文树加权（CTW），作为可操作的因果不确定性坐标；并将其与完整KT信息集下不可观测的局部不可约不确定性（LIU）区分开。把模型预测投影到这一共享的不确定性坐标上，从而在不同熵带而非仅在全局层面评估模型性能增益。辅以项目感知参照、log-loss和等频分析。

Result: 在NIPS Task 3/4和Algebra 2005上的综合评估显示，模型改进高度非均匀：现代KT模型在高熵区域获得显著增益；项目感知参照、log-loss和等频分析支持这种局部化结论；框架还标出表观增益需检查噪声敏感行为的区域。

Conclusion: 该框架通过呈现局部建模失败与真实增益，为研究残差预测结构以及当前KT基准和模型局限提供了诊断工具，弥补了聚合指标无法定位错误来源和判断基准饱和的不足。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) models are predominantly evaluated using aggregate metrics such as area under the curve (AUC) and accuracy. However, these global scores obscure where the remaining errors originate and fail to indicate whether a benchmark is approaching saturation. While estimating a global theoretical performance limit is challenging in realistic KT settings, it is possible to quantify local predictability. To address this, we propose an information-theoretic evaluation framework for KT benchmark diagnosis. We use Context Tree Weighting (CTW) on item-response histories and current-item queries as an operational causal uncertainty coordinate, while distinguishing it from the unobserved Local Irreducible Uncertainty (LIU) under the full KT information set. By projecting predictions onto this shared uncertainty coordinate, we evaluate model performance gains across distinct entropy bands rather than only at the global level. Comprehensive evaluations on NIPS Task 3/4 and Algebra 2005 reveal that model improvements are highly non-uniform. Modern KT models show substantial gains in high-entropy regions, and additional item-aware references, log-loss, and equal-frequency analyses support this localization. The framework also flags regions where apparent gains require checks for noise-sensitive behavior. By surfacing these local modeling failures alongside genuine gains, this approach provides a diagnostic tool for studying both residual predictive structure and the limitations of current KT benchmarks and models.

</details>


### [49] [Repair Lot Skyline: A Weighted Constraint Satisfaction Approach to Pavement Repair Optimization from Geospatial Hazard Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06989)
*Takato Yasuno,Keita Kobayashi,Ryuta Sakaguchi,Takuya Okamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将路面病害修复计划形式化为"修复批次天际线"（Repair Lot Skyline）问题——一个定义在里程（chainage）而非时间轴上的加权约束满足问题，实现单期病害调查即可求解；案例中最终方案含106个合并批次、总长1997.4 m，覆盖100%坑槽与91.6%裂缝，并给出最小批次长度与历史上下文半径的联合四目标超参搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路面管理机构需要把空间分布式的病害清单转化为有界、可执行的修复批次计划：坑槽等事故关键缺陷必须处理，裂缝等低风险缺陷仅在收益抵偿成本时纳入，而历史修补位置只作为再退化风险信号、本身不触发修复。现有做法缺乏对"必选—可选"异质约束与成本效益权衡的统一形式化，因此需要一个不依赖未来退化预测的单期决策模型。

Method: 形式化为WCSP（加权约束满足问题），决策变量定义在里程维度而非时间维度，仅需单期病害调查、不对未来退化作任何断言；识别143个候选危险簇（61个硬约束、82个软约束）；以成本—收益为准则决定软候选的纳入，并用天际线前沿刻画权衡；进一步将最小批次长度L_min与历史上下文半径κ的确定建模为针对该WCSP的联合四目标超参数搜索。

Result: 106个候选合并为最终修复计划，总长1997.4 m，为全部软候选无条件纳入时长度（2385.9 m）的83.7%；覆盖100%观测坑槽（138/138）、91.6%观测裂缝（404/441），捕获全部候选集总危害收益的93.6%（542/579）。天际线显示明显边际收益递减：其余37个被排除软候选仅增加6.8%收益，却使修复长度增加19.4%。推荐配置L_min=14.7 m、κ=10 m相比未调优默认值，使修复班组出动次数减少7.1%，代价是预算增大12.9%、裂缝覆盖率降低1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 该形式化方法可在不预测未来退化的前提下，从单期病害调查生成兼顾安全性（坑槽全覆盖）与成本效益的修复批次计划，并通过天际线前沿显式暴露收益—长度权衡；同时表明L_min与κ的联合调优能有效降低班组出动次数，但需在预算与裂缝覆盖率之间作明确取舍。

Abstract: Pavement agencies must translate a spatially distributed distress inventory into a bounded, actionable repair-lot plan: accident-critical defects (potholes) must always be addressed, lower-risk defects (cracks) should be included only when their benefit justifies the repair cost, and historical patch locations signal re-degradation risk without themselves triggering repair. We formalize this as a Repair Lot Skyline problem: a Weighted Constraint Satisfaction Problem (WCSP) defined over chainage (distance along the road) rather than over time, so that it requires only a single-epoch distress survey and makes no claim about future deterioration. The WCSP identifies 143 candidate hazard clusters (61 hard, 82 soft), of which 106 are merged into a final repair plan totaling 1,997.4 m---83.7% of the 2,385.9 m that would be required if every soft candidate were included regardless of cost. This plan covers 100% of observed potholes (138/138) and 91.6% of observed cracks (404/441), capturing 93.6% (542/579) of the total hazard benefit available in the full candidate set. The skyline frontier shows pronounced diminishing returns beyond this point: the remaining 37 excluded soft candidates would add only 6.8% additional benefit for a 19.4% increase in repair length. We further formalize the minimum-lot-length $L_{\min}$ and historical-context radius $κ$ as a joint, four-objective hyperparameter search over this WCSP; on the same case study, the recommended configuration ($L_{\min} = 14.7$ m, $κ= 10$ m) reduces repair-crew mobilizations by 7.1% relative to an untuned default, at the cost of a 12.9% larger budget and a 1.1-percentage-point lower crack coverage.

</details>


### [50] [DART-ES: Difficulty-Aware Reweighting and Targeted Replay for Fine-Tuning LLMs with Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06993)
*Zhishen Sun,Hongzhan Wang,Sizhe Dang,Guang Dai,Haishan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DART-ES，通过基于通过率的动态难度状态联合引导难度重加权与稀有可解样本回放，提升ES微调LLM的效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ES将问题级种群反馈压缩为单一标量并对各问题均匀平均奖励，无法刻画每个问题的学习价值随模型能力的变化，限制了扰动方向评估与训练数据分配的效果。

Method: DART-ES从扰动种群在各问题上的通过率估计局部可解性，并聚合历史观测构建动态难度状态；该共享状态同时引导连续难度重加权与稀有可解样本回放，且不引入额外难度模型或反向传播。

Result: 在五个基座模型上均优于ES，平均准确率由72.07%提升至73.53%，超过GRPO在GSM8K上的73.26%；在五个数学推理基准上平均49.20%对比ES的48.34%，与强7B RL模型相当；在指令微调、代码生成及14B模型Countdown任务上均有一致增益。系统效率上，相比GRPO每步运行时间降低15.2%--50.2%，单GPU峰值显存降低21.1%--51.1%，且全参数微调下比GRPO+LoRA更省时间与显存。

Conclusion: DART-ES以无需额外模型与反向传播的方式改进ES的奖励聚合与数据分配，在多种任务与模型规模上兼具性能与效率优势，具有较强泛化性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) enable memory efficient full parameter fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) using only forward computation. However, standard ES uniformly averages rewards across problems and compresses problem level population feedback into a single scalar, making it difficult to capture how the learning value of each problem changes with model capability. To address this limitation, we propose Difficulty-Aware Reweighting and Targeted Replay for Evolution Strategies (DART-ES). DART-ES estimates the local solvability of each problem from its pass rate across the perturbation population and aggregates historical observations to construct a dynamic difficulty state. This shared state jointly guides continuous difficulty reweighting and rare solvable sample replay, thereby improving perturbation direction evaluation and training data allocation without introducing an additional difficulty model or backpropagation. Extensive experiments show that DART-ES achieves good fine-tuning performance. DART-ES outperforms ES on all five base models and improves the average accuracy from 72.07\% to 73.53\%, exceeding the 73.26\% achieved by GRPO on GSM8K. Across five challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DART-ES achieves an average accuracy of 49.20\%, compared with 48.34\% for ES and remains competitive with strong 7B models trained with RL. Further experiments show consistent gains in instruction tuning, code generation and the Countdown task with a 14B model, demonstrating strong generalization across tasks and scalability to larger models. Beyond performance gains, DART-ES also shows clear advantages in system efficiency. It reduces runtime per step by 15.2\%--50.2\% and peak memory usage per GPU by 21.1\%--51.1\% compared with GRPO. Despite performing full parameter fine-tuning, DART-ES also requires less runtime and GPU memory than GRPO+LoRA.

</details>


### [51] [Mask-Guided KV Cache Eviction in Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06996)
*Gleb Molodtsov,Ekaterina Alimaskina,Evgeny Uskov,Artur Zagitov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练方法MaskAhead，用统一的掩码查询排序机制同时解决块扩散语言模型KV缓存的“选择”与“淘汰”问题，并给出低比特量化变体Q-MaskAhead，在多项长序列任务中大幅降低KV内存且保持较高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散语言模型在生成过程中需保留完整KV缓存并在每个去噪步骤中对其做注意力，导致内存占用大、生成速度慢。降低开销需同时解决两个问题：当前块去噪应使用哪些历史token（选择），以及哪些KV应保留在内存供后续块使用（淘汰），现有方法难以统一且高效地处理。

Method: 提出MaskAhead，一种免训练方法，通过单一的基于mask-query的排序机制同时完成选择与淘汰：利用当前块掩码引导选择，利用对未来被掩码块的探测（probes）引导淘汰，两者均按KV条目对注意力输出的估计贡献进行排序。进一步提出量化变体Q-MaskAhead，直接从低比特KV计算选择与注意力，基本保持被选条目的一致性。

Result: 在Fast-dLLM-v2、DreamReasoner、LLaDA2.0-mini上覆盖长生成推理、长提示问答与“大海捞针”检索任务。长提示问答中，MaskAhead相对稠密推理平均降低KV内存9.5倍，仅有1.2个点平均F1损失；Q-MaskAhead将内存降幅提升至20.1倍，平均F1损失为2.3个点。在batch-32系统评测中，MaskAhead相对稠密推理取得1.23倍端到端与1.68倍解码阶段加速。

Conclusion: 基于掩码查询的统一排序机制可同时有效完成KV选择与淘汰，在无需训练的前提下显著压缩块扩散语言模型的KV缓存并提升生成速度，量化变体进一步放大内存收益且精度损失可控，适用于长上下文与长生成场景。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models keep a large key-value (KV) cache throughout generation and attend to it at every denoising step, limiting both memory capacity and generation speed. Reducing these costs requires deciding which past tokens to use for denoising the current block (selection) and which to keep in memory for future blocks (eviction). We propose MaskAhead, a training-free method that solves both tasks with a single mask-query-based ranking mechanism. Current-block masks guide selection, while probes of upcoming masked blocks guide eviction. Both rank KV entries by their estimated contribution to the attention output. Our quantized variant, Q-MaskAhead, computes selection and attention directly from low-bit KV, largely preserving the selected entries. Experiments on Fast-dLLM-v2, DreamReasoner, and LLaDA2.0-mini cover long-generation reasoning, long-prompt question answering, and needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. On long-prompt QA, MaskAhead reduces KV memory by $9.5\times$ on average with a 1.2-point mean F1 loss relative to dense inference. Q-MaskAhead increases the reduction to $20.1\times$ with a 2.3-point mean F1 loss. In a batch-32 systems profile, MaskAhead achieves $1.23\times$ end-to-end and $1.68\times$ decode-stage speedups over dense inference.

</details>


### [52] [Should We Skip Diffusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07002)
*Yiping Ji,James Martens,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DDT-RFE，移除编码器块中 Self-Attention 和 MLP 周围的残差连接以实现逐层抽象，同时融合 patch embedding 与中间及最终编码器特征以保留细节信息；在多项视觉理解任务和 ImageNet 图像生成上均优于 DDT，且使用更少编码器块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成图像的同时学习语义表征。DDT 中条件编码器为速度解码器提供去噪引导特征，这些特征需同时捕获高层抽象结构与低层细节。但编码器中的 skip/残差连接使浅层特征绕过后续变换，可能限制渐进式抽象，或至少难以解耦不同抽象层次。作者希望增强编码器的表示抽象能力，同时不丢失解码器所需的细节信息。

Method: DDT-RFE 移除每个编码器块中 Self-Attention 与 MLP 操作周围的残差连接，并保持训练稳定；为保留被抽象丢弃但解码器仍需要的信息，将输入 patch embedding 与中间及最终编码器特征融合，构成编码器输出，使解码器可访问多个编码深度的信息，而每个编码器块能学习更抽象的表征。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割、目标发现和语义对应等视觉理解任务上整体优于 DDT，且使用更少编码器块；在 ImageNet 图像生成上取得更低的 FID。

Conclusion: 去除编码器块内残差连接并融合多深度特征，可在不牺牲细节的前提下提升表征收敛至更高抽象层次，全面改善扩散 Transformer 的理解与生成性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models learn semantic representations while generating images. In the Decoupled Diffusion Transformer (DDT), a condition encoder provides features that guide a velocity decoder in denoising. To enable effective denoising at all noise levels, these features must capture both high-level abstract structures and low-level details. However, skip/residual connections in the encoder allow shallow features to bypass successive transformations, which may limit progressive abstraction, or at least make it difficult to disentangle different levels of abstraction. We propose DDT-RFE, which removes the residual connections around the Self-Attention and MLP operations in each encoder block while maintaining stable training. To retain the information that abstraction discards but that the decoder still needs, we fuse the input patch embedding with intermediate and final encoder features to form the encoder output. The decoder thus has access to information from multiple encoder depths, while each encoder block is able to learn more abstract representations. DDT-RFE achieves overall improvements over DDT across visual understanding tasks, including image classification, semantic segmentation, object discovery, and semantic correspondence, while using fewer encoder blocks. It also achieves a lower FID for image generation on ImageNet.

</details>


### [53] [STOCK-JEPA: Prior-Anchored Latent Revision Representation Learning in Equity Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07006)
*Yizhi Luo,Jiahe Yi,Jianhui Zhang,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Stock-JEPA，一种联合嵌入预测框架，在时点金融先验基础上学习可预测的增量修正，兼顾深度模型的表达能力与线性模型的可解释性，并在中美股市大幅超越 13 个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票市场数据信噪比低：黑箱深度模型能捕捉复杂模式但易过拟合样本噪声且缺乏显式经济结构；经典线性金融模型虽可解释，但其过度简化假设无法刻画非线性信号。因此需要结合两者优势的表征学习方法。

Method: 三部分框架：(1) 用低复杂度金融模型生成刻画多期收益与风险的固定统计量，经先验投影器映射到目标编码器隐空间作为锚点；(2) 设计上下文条件修正预测器，估计未来表征相对锚点的可预测位移，两条分支分别用独立损失更新——锚点学习先验统计，修正项从历史上下文捕捉额外可预测信息；(3) 冻结全部表征模块，训练下游读出层，通过横截面排序与组合表现评估预测。

Result: 理论上证明最优修正对同一未来表征可将先验锚点的期望平方误差精确降低 E[||Δ||²₂]，该非负增益即历史上下文可预测的额外信号的期望平方幅度。实验上在中国和美国大规模股票池、5 项关键评价指标上优于 13 个强基线；消融与表征分析进一步验证所学修正对股票市场表征学习的价值。

Conclusion: 以“线性先验锚点 + 可预测增量修正”的联合嵌入预测范式，可有效融合经济结构与非线性信号，既提升表征质量又保留可解释锚定，为股票市场表征学习提供理论保证与实证支持。

Abstract: Learning effective representations helps characterize the structure and dynamics of equity markets from financial data with a low signal-to-noise ratio. Black-box deep models can capture complex patterns but may overfit sample noise and lack explicit economic structure. Meanwhile, classic linear financial models provide interpretable references, but their oversimplified assumptions leave non-linear signals uncaptured. To combine the strengths of these two directions, we propose Stock-JEPA, a joint-embedding predictive framework that learns predictable incremental revisions relative to a point-in-time financial prior. First, we leverage a low-complexity financial model to produce fixed statistics summarizing multi-horizon return and risk. A prior projector then maps these statistics into the target encoder's latent space as an anchor. Second, we design a context-conditioned revision predictor to estimate the future representation's predictable displacement from the anchor. Separate losses update the two branches: the anchor learns from prior statistics, while the revision captures additional predictable information from historical context. Third, we freeze all representation modules and train a downstream readout, evaluating its forecasts through cross-sectional ranking and portfolio performance. Theoretically, we prove that optimal revision reduces the prior anchor's expected squared error for the same future representation by exactly $\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbolΔ\|_2^2]$. This non-negative gain is the expected squared magnitude of the additional signal predictable from historical context. Experimentally, Stock-JEPA outperforms 13 strong baselines across large-scale China and U.S. equity universes on 5 key evaluation metrics. Ablation studies and representation analysis further demonstrate the value of the learned revisions for representation learning in equity markets.

</details>


### [54] [Identifiable World Models from Pretrained Diffusion Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07028)
*Ruchi Sandilya,Conor Liston,Logan Grosenick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 冻结扩散世界模型可通过轻量对齐映射(ConDA)获得可辨识潜坐标,保持潜在SCM并支持滞后图恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散世界模型虽能预测高维动力系统轨迹,但预测精度不等于潜坐标恢复真实状态变量或因果交互;如何在无需重训生成主干的前提下获得可辨识坐标是关键问题。

Method: 提出Contrastive Diffusion Alignment (ConDA):在冻结扩散潜表示上仅学习轻量对齐映射,将辅助变量非线性ICA保证迁移至该框架;在TCL/GCL假设下,对齐表示可辨识至置换与逐分量可逆变换,保持潜在动态结构因果模型,并将滞后图恢复归结为转移Jacobian稀疏性。对比TCL/GCL/CEBRA三种ConDA与TDRL、CaRiNG、IDOL、temporal SuaVE、iVAE。

Result: 在物理与机器人视频系统上,TCL与GCL实现近乎完美的分块状态恢复和有竞争力的滞后图恢复,仿真落体系统中实现精确恢复;仿真双足机器人中恢复的控制扰动响应符号与时间结构正确。

Conclusion: 冻结生成扩散模型可被赋予可辨识、结构可解释且对干预相关动态分析有用的坐标。

Abstract: Diffusion-based world models can generate and predict trajectories in high-dimensional dynamical systems, but predictive accuracy does not imply that their latent coordinates recover the underlying state variables or causal interactions. We ask whether a frozen pretrained diffusion model can be equipped with identifiable coordinates without retraining its generative backbone. We show that auxiliary-variable nonlinear ICA guarantees can be transferred to Contrastive Diffusion Alignment (ConDA), which learns only a lightweight alignment map on top of frozen diffusion latents. Under standard TCL/GCL assumptions, the aligned representation identifies latent dynamical states up to permutation and componentwise invertible transformations, preserves the latent dynamic structural causal model, and reduces lagged graph recovery to transition-Jacobian sparsity. We evaluate TCL-, GCL-, and CEBRA-based ConDA against TDRL, CaRiNG, IDOL, temporal SuaVE, and iVAE across physical and robotic video systems. TCL and GCL achieve near-perfect blockwise state recovery and competitive lagged graph recovery, including exact recovery in a simulated falling-body system. In a simulated bipedal robot, learned dynamics recover the sign and temporal structure of responses to held-out control perturbations. These results show that a frozen generative diffusion model can be equipped with coordinates that are identifiable, structurally interpretable, and useful for analyzing intervention-relevant dynamics.

</details>


### [55] [Shaping the Wind: Nested Potentials for Kinematically Admissible Urban Wind Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07033)
*Yidi Wang,Yunhe Zhang,Jiawei Gu,Ziyue Qiao,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sculpt嵌套势框架，将几何相关的无散度与不可穿透约束内嵌于神经代理参数化中，并发布UrbanWindFlow数据集用于联合评估精度与运动学可容许性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬态城市风场预测对城市微气候理解与气候韧性城市设计至关重要。建筑解析大涡模拟（LES）精度高但每个布局计算成本巨大，神经代理模型虽快，却仅以速度预测误差为目标，无法保证局部质量守恒与壁面不可穿透性（二者共同构成运动学可容许性）。该问题的根源在于无约束输出表示：几何条件仅引导预测而不限制其落在可容许速度场内；事后修正边界违例会改变相邻流体单元的通量平衡，进而破坏局部质量守恒。

Method: 提出Sculpt——一种嵌套势（nested potential）框架，将耦合的、依赖几何的约束直接构建进参数化中：在原生三维交错网格上，通过体矢量势（volume vector potential）的离散旋度生成无散度速度更新；共享的标量势约束矢量势的边界值，使同一算子同时施加不可穿透性，且无需逐步压力投影。针对旋度反向传播衰减大尺度梯度信号的问题，在多分辨率上参数化体矢量势以更好捕捉大尺度流动结构。同时构建UrbanWindFlow数据集，覆盖多种城市形态与入流条件，用于联合评估精度与运动学可容许性。

Result: 提出Sculpt嵌套势神经代理框架，在三维交错网格上通过离散旋度保证无散度、通过共享标量势保证壁面不可穿透，无需每步压力投影；采用多分辨率体矢量势缓解旋度反向传播的大尺度梯度衰减；并构建UrbanWindFlow LES数据集，覆盖多种城市形态与入流条件，支持精度与运动学可容许性的联合评测。

Conclusion: Sculpt通过将无散度与不可穿透约束内嵌于参数化，在保持神经代理效率的同时实现运动学可容许的瞬态城市风场预测，为城市微气候模拟与气候韧性设计提供更可靠的快速替代方案。

Abstract: Predicting transient urban winds is fundamental to understanding urban microclimates and designing climate-resilient cities. Building-resolving large-eddy simulation produces detailed incompressible urban wind fields at substantial computational cost for each layout. Neural surrogates offer a faster alternative by learning to predict the evolution of velocity fields. However, minimizing velocity prediction error does not guarantee local mass conservation and wall impermeability, which together define kinematic admissibility. This limitation stems from an unconstrained output representation: geometry conditioning guides predictions but does not restrict them to admissible velocity fields. Correcting boundary violations in these outputs changes the flux balance in adjacent fluid cells and may consequently compromise local mass conservation. To address the challenge, we propose Sculpt, a nested potential framework that builds the coupled, geometry-dependent constraints directly into its parameterization. This nested parameterization generates divergence-free velocity updates through the discrete curl of a volume vector potential on the native three-dimensional staggered grid. A shared scalar potential constrains the vector potential's boundary values so that the same operator also enforces impermeability, without a per-step pressure projection. Because backpropagation through this curl attenuates large-scale gradient signals, we parameterize the volume potential at multiple resolutions to better capture large-scale flow structures. We introduce UrbanWindFlow, an LES dataset spanning urban morphologies and inflow conditions, to evaluate accuracy and kinematic admissibility together.

</details>


### [56] [The Premise Is the Problem: Exchangeability Failure in Self-Monitored Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07038)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu,Zhenda Li,Liying Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多步时间序列预测中"自监控自适应"问题：当监控与模型更新使用同一预测误差反馈时，监控器的统计保证会因重叠目标与误差相关性而失效，导致误报、掩盖持续变化并损害预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后的预测模型常需在线更新以适应数据分布变化，但更新也可能使预测变差，因此需要可靠监控来检测有害变化并触发保护。一个自然设计是让监控器监控那些同时驱动模型更新的预测误差；然而这种"自监控"是否仍满足统计保证，此前缺乏系统考察。

Method: 在多步时间序列预测设定下，理论分析监控与自适应共用同一误差反馈的情形，重点考察多步预测中重叠目标（overlapping targets）与预测误差的时间相关性对监控保证所需关键假设的破坏；并通过实验对比自适应模型与冻结模型在实际反馈下的监控信号表现。

Result: 重叠目标与误差相关性破坏了统计保证所依赖的关键假设；监控器在无有害变化时也会发出警报，其触发的保护响应反而进一步降低预测质量；自适应过程会把持续变化"隐藏"于自身监控之下，而原始冻结模型反而保留了更清晰的变化信号。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了自监控自适应的基本失效模式。可靠部署需检验监控器的假设前提、在真实反馈下将自适应模型与冻结模型对照评估，并限制每一次保护响应的影响范围。

Abstract: Modern forecasting models are often updated after deployment so they can respond to changing data. These updates can also make predictions worse, so practical systems need a reliable monitor that can detect harmful changes and trigger protection. A natural design is to monitor the same prediction errors that guide the updates. This paper asks whether the statistical guarantee behind such a monitor remains valid when monitoring and adaptation use the same feedback. We study this question in multi-step time-series forecasting. We show that overlapping targets and dependence in forecast errors can break a key assumption required by the guarantee. The monitor may then raise alarms even when no harmful change has occurred, and its response can further damage prediction quality. We also find that adaptation can hide sustained changes from its own monitor, while the original frozen model retains a clearer signal. These results expose a basic failure mode in self-monitored adaptation. They show why reliable deployment requires checking the monitor's assumptions, comparing adaptation with the frozen model under realistic feedback, and limiting the effect of every protective response.

</details>


### [57] [TRIAGE: Direction-Aware Mismatch Stabilization of Native NVFP4 Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07043)
*Zhen Li,Shuai Zhang,Yanggan Gu,Yiming Zhang,Yang Yu,Mingfa Feng,Congkai Xie,Shuang Yu,Junjie Lai,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对低精度（NVFP4）强化学习训练中学习器与采样器执行不一致导致的策略优化不稳定问题，提出了方向感知的稳定化方法 TRIAGE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度执行可显著加速大语言模型的强化学习，但学习器与采样器之间的执行差异会破坏策略优化稳定性。仅凭失配幅度无法识别哪些更新贡献是局部放大还是收缩的。

Method: 论文刻画了失配与策略梯度方向之间的相互作用，区分了局部放大与收缩的更新贡献。发现在原生 NVFP4 运行中，两类放大区域出现早期失衡，偏向负优势、负差距的更新，其尾部 token 在失配全局扩散前集中于少量响应片段。据此提出 TRIAGE：一种方向感知的稳定化方法，利用片段级诊断有选择地重新平衡策略梯度更新，并对残余严重失配施加有界修复。TRIAGE 修改优化目标，同时在采样器和学习器上保留原生 NVFP4 的 W4A4 前向执行。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-30B-A3B 上的实验表明，在整个评估训练周期内优化稳定，并在五个数学推理基准上达到全精度水平性能；原生 NVFP4 配合 TRIAGE 相比 BF16 可提供高达 2.3 倍的 rollout 吞吐量。

Conclusion: TRIAGE 通过方向感知的片段级诊断与有界修复，在保持原生 NVFP4 W4A4 执行的同时实现了稳定优化与全精度相当的推理性能，并显著提升 rollout 吞吐量。

Abstract: Low-precision execution can substantially accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models, but discrepancies between learner and sampler execution can destabilize policy optimization. In this paper, we characterize the interaction between mismatch and the policy-gradient direction, distinguishing locally amplifying from contracting update contributions that mismatch magnitude alone cannot identify. In native NVFP4 runs, we observe an early imbalance between the two amplifying regions, favoring negative-advantage, negative-gap updates. Their tail tokens become concentrated in a small fraction of response segments before mismatch spreads globally. Motivated by these findings, we introduce TRIAGE, a direction-aware stabilization method that uses segment-level diagnosis to selectively rebalance policy-gradient updates and applies bounded repair to residual severe mismatch. TRIAGE modifies the optimization objective while retaining native NVFP4 weight-and activation 4-bit (W4A4) forward execution on both the sampler and learner. Experiments on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B show stable optimization throughout the evaluated training horizon and achieve full precision level performance across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, while native NVFP4 with TRIAGE provides up to 2.3x higher rollout throughput than BF16.

</details>


### [58] [Adversarial Training for Deep Hedging in Nonstationary Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07162)
*Philipp J. Schneider,Lukas Looser,Antoine Garin,Shuhan Liu,Daniel Kuhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WRAP——一种基于双预算分布鲁棒优化(DRO)的漂移感知对抗训练框架，用于深度对冲：在φ-散度重加权与最优传输路径扰动下推导联合一阶展开，得到可处理的有限维对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度对冲从历史或模拟轨迹中学习交易策略，但在市场非平稳条件下，训练路径未必能代表未来市场状况；需同时应对采样不确定性与时间漂移，以及两类分布误设。

Method: 以加权经验参考分布为锚，基线权重在采样不确定性与时间漂移之间取平衡；在其周围构造模糊集，允许对手在φ-散度约束下对轨迹重加权、在最优传输(OT)约束下扰动路径；对名义期望损失的领先阶增量做联合一阶展开，分解为取决于对冲损失跨轨迹离散度的重加权项和取决于损失对路径扰动敏感性的传输项，从而将分布内层上确界替换为有限维可处理的一阶对抗攻击。

Result: 在平稳与非平稳Heston动力学及广义仿射扩散(GAD)上，实验显示重加权与传输两类机制带来互补收益，联合对抗训练在非平稳情形下增益最大。

Conclusion: WRAP将漂移感知的分布鲁棒对冲转化为可求解的一阶对抗训练问题，重加权与路径扰动互补，联合训练在非平稳市场中最为有效。

Abstract: Deep hedging learns trading policies from historical or simulated market trajectories, yet under nonstationarity these training paths may not represent future market conditions. We propose WRAP (Wasserstein-Reweighting Adversarial Perturbation), a drift-aware adversarial training framework derived from a two-budget distributionally robust optimization (DRO) formulation. The formulation is anchored to a weighted empirical reference distribution whose fixed baseline weights are chosen to balance sampling uncertainty against temporal drift. Around this reference distribution, the ambiguity set addresses two complementary forms of distributional misspecification by allowing an adversary to reweight the observed trajectories subject to a $φ$-divergence constraint and perturb their paths subject to an optimal-transport (OT) constraint. We derive a joint first-order expansion in which the leading-order increase over the nominal expected loss decomposes into a reweighting contribution determined by the dispersion of hedging losses across trajectories and a transport contribution determined by the sensitivity of the loss to path perturbations. This expansion yields an explicit finite-dimensional adversarial attack that replaces the distributional inner supremum with a tractable first-order approximation. Across stationary and nonstationary Heston dynamics and a generalized affine diffusion (GAD), the experiments show complementary benefits from reweighting and transport, with joint adversarial training providing the largest gains under nonstationarity.

</details>


### [59] [Skillful Data-Driven Subseasonal Soil Moisture Forecasting: Prospects and Limits for Flash Drought Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07060)
*Noelia Otero,Atahan Özer,Miguel-Ángel Fernández-Torres,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Vision Transformer与双路径时空注意力的S2S土壤湿度预报模型，发现预报技巧既取决于模型，也取决于问题形式化方式（残差学习、真实物理单位目标），在2021–2022年欧洲基准测试中优于深度学习与ECMWF S2S业务基线，但骤发干旱的起报仍是所有S2S系统的共同难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短中期天气预报已有显著进展，但骤发干旱等高影响事件的预测仍是早期预警与次季节至季节（S2S）物理预报系统的关键挑战，需要提升S2S土壤湿度预报能力。

Method: 采用基于Vision Transformer的架构，结合双路径时间与空间注意力；引入残差学习以超越持续性预报；以真实物理单位（而非标准化距平）作为根区土壤湿度预报目标；并通过分位数头微调实现概率化预报。在2021–2022年欧洲区域与深度学习及ECMWF S2S业务基线进行对比评估。

Result: 模型在所有预见期上取得最高的确定性技巧与概率技巧，概率分布校准良好，并能可靠识别根区异常干燥状态（低于第20百分位）；但以多候强化标准定义的骤发干旱起报，仍是当前所有S2S系统共有的根本性挑战。

Conclusion: 预报技巧不仅取决于预报模型本身，也取决于预测问题的形式化方式；数据驱动的S2S土壤湿度预报因此得到推进，但快速干旱发展过程的预测仍是有待攻克的难题。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in short-to-medium-range weather forecasting, predicting high-impact events such as flash droughts remains a key challenge for both early warning operations and physically-based subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) prediction systems. Here we demonstrate that, for S2S soil-moisture forecasting over Europe, forecast skill depends as much on how the prediction problem is formulated as on the forecasting model itself. Using a Vision Transformer-based architecture with dual-pathway temporal and spatial attention, we show that residual learning is essential to outperform persistence. This advantage is realized only when forecasting root-zone soil moisture in physical units rather than standardized anomalies, revealing that the target representation itself constrains predictability. A probabilistic extension via quantile-head fine-tuning further provides well-calibrated predictive distributions. Benchmarked against deep-learning and operational ECMWF S2S baselines over 2021-2022, our model achieves the highest deterministic and probabilistic skill at all lead times and reliably detects anomalously dry root-zone states (below the 20th percentile). Yet flash drought onset, defined by multi-pentad intensification criteria, remains a fundamental challenge shared across all current S2S systems. These findings advance data-driven S2S soil-moisture forecasting while highlighting the remaining challenge of predicting rapid drought development.

</details>


### [60] [Learning PDE solution operators with variable initial conditions via Latent Dynamics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08475)
*Stefano Maria Pizzamiglio,Stefano Pagani,Francesco Regazzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作扩展了Latent Dynamics Network (LDNet)，使其能够处理变化的初始条件：通过少量早期观测推断初始隐状态，提出自解码与元学习两种策略，在保持无编码器、端到端训练和分辨率无关特性的同时实现高效代理建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDNet虽在时空系统预测中表现优异，但原始公式假定初始条件固定，限制了其在系统从不同初态演化的大量现实场景中的适用性。因此需要解除该限制，同时保留原LDNet的端到端训练流程与无编码器特性（即对空间分辨率和网格拓扑的独立性）。

Method: 将隐状态初始化视为适应问题，从少量早期时刻观测直接推断初始隐状态。研究两种策略：(1) 自解码(auto-decoding)形式；(2) 元学习方法，将初始隐状态作为任务特定的上下文变量。方法基于坐标式解码器(coordinate-based decoder)，允许从空间下采样数据训练。

Result: 在平流-扩散、流体动力学和固体力学等多类物理现象上验证了所提方法的准确性。元学习显著加速隐状态推断，产生更平滑、条件更好的优化景观，并自发地将隐空间组织为反映潜在动力学物理特征的结构化表示。坐标式解码器支持从空间下采样数据训练，并在推断时恢复高分辨率解场。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有变化初始条件的时间相关PDE的多查询仿真提供了一种高效、分辨率无关的代理建模框架，在保持LDNet核心优势的同时克服了固定初始条件的局限。

Abstract: In many-query scenarios, data-driven surrogate models provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for simulating physical systems governed by Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). In this context, the Latent Dynamics Network (LDNet) has recently demonstrated remarkable performance in predicting the response of spatio-temporal systems, combining Neural Ordinary Differential Equations with nonlinear dimensionality reduction. However, the original formulation assumes a fixed initial condition, limiting its applicability to many real-world applications where a system evolves from varying starting states. In this work, we overcome this limitation while keeping the end-to-end training procedure of the original LDNet and its encoder-free nature, which preserves its intrinsic independence from spatial resolution and grid topology. We infer the initial latent state directly from a small set of early-time observations, treating latent-state initialization as an adaptation problem, and investigate two strategies: an auto-decoding formulation and a meta-learning approach in which the initial latent state acts as a task-specific context variable. We demonstrate the accuracy of the proposed methods across diverse physical phenomena, spanning advection-diffusion, fluid dynamics, and solid mechanics. Meta-learning markedly accelerates latent-state inference and induces smoother, better-conditioned optimization landscapes, and spontaneously organizes the latent space into a structured representation that reflects physically meaningful features of the underlying dynamics. The coordinate-based decoder enables training from spatially subsampled data while recovering high-resolution solution fields at inference. The resulting approach provides an efficient and resolution-independent surrogate modeling framework for many-query simulations of time-dependent PDEs with varying initial conditions.

</details>


### [61] [Learning to Simulate Individuals from Macro Social Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07062)
*Yining Zhao,Bushi Liu,Haofei Yu,Zhengyang Qi,Shanyong Wang,Chuyue Li,Yuxiang Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 macro2mind，利用预测市场价格轨迹的群体响应信号，通过 GRPO 训练语言模型学习"行为推理"，并将该推理零样本迁移至用户模拟任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用大模型模拟个体对新情境反应的方法，其行为推理要么继承自预训练，要么来自个体级标注；后者行为多样性有限，且对推理过程本身缺乏监督。

Method: 以预测市场为监督来源：价格轨迹大规模记录了群体对真实事件的响应。采用 GRPO 结合市场信号训练。核心是"社会行为分解"，将行为推理显式化为预测步骤——推断代表性市场参与者群体、预测各群体如何解读新闻并更新信念、推理其互动、聚合成价格。并设计"后见之悔课程"配合难度感知采样，聚焦于后见识别的群体能显著改善预测的可学习样本。

Result: 在 SWM-Bench 上，macro2mind 在 Polymarket 上取得最优方向准确率与相关性。以市场数据训练后可零样本迁移至四个用户模拟基准（Humanual、OvertonBench、PRISM、CAD），在零样本方法中具竞争力。作为数据生成器，可将下游模拟器在未见用户上的准确率提升 15.5 个百分点，比其骨干模型生成的数据高 13.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: 预测市场可作为学习行为推理的可扩展监督信号，所学的显式群体行为分解推理可零样本迁移至用户模拟，并有效充当提升下游模拟器的数据生成器。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate how individuals respond to new situations, yet the behavioral reasoning behind these responses is either inherited from pretraining or learned from individual-level annotations, which offer limited behavioral diversity and little supervision of the reasoning itself. We propose to learn behavioral reasoning from prediction markets, whose price trajectories record how populations respond to real-world events at scale. We introduce macro2mind, which trains a language model with GRPO using market signals. A social behavioral decomposition makes behavioral reasoning an explicit step of forecasting: the model infers representative groups of market participants, predicts how each interprets the news and updates its beliefs, reasons about their interactions, and aggregates these responses into a price. A hindsight-regret curriculum with difficulty-aware sampling focuses training on transitions where hindsight-identified groups substantially improve the forecast while prioritizing examples that remain learnable for the current policy. The learned reasoning applies to user simulation without further training. On SWM-Bench, macro2mind achieves state-of-the-art directional accuracy and correlation on Polymarket. Trained on market data, it transfers zero-shot to four user-simulation benchmarks (Humanual, OvertonBench, PRISM, and CAD) and has competitive performance among zero-shot methods. Used as a data generator, macro2mind also raises a downstream simulator's accuracy on unseen users by 15.5 points, outperforming data generated by its backbone by 13.2 points.

</details>


### [62] [Preserving Unstable Modes Through Inverse Dynamics in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07540)
*Leonardo F. Toso,Yann LeCun,James Anderson,Oumayma Bounou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出，JEPA 的下一步预测+反坍缩正则无法保证保留可控不稳定模态，并提出加入动作重构（逆动力学）损失以学习控制感知表征，理论上证明该损失使编码器在有限时域可达子空间上单射，并保留可控不稳定子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统存在不稳定模态，微小扰动若不经反馈校正会无界增长。从高维视觉观测控制这类系统，需要能保留这些模态的表征。JEPA 被认为是学习此类表征及动力学的自然框架，但作者发现常规训练目标不足以保证保留可控不稳定模态。

Method: 作者首先证明：仅用下一步预测加上反坍缩正则，训练损失可以最小化，但可控不稳定模态仍可能被坍缩，导致无法从学到的表征中实现稳定化。为解决该问题，他们在世界模型训练中加入动作重构目标（逆动力学损失），以鼓励控制感知的视觉表征。理论分析针对线性系统：证明精确动作重构使编码器在有限时域可达子空间上单射，即编码器不能丢弃 H 步内可由动作序列到达的状态方向；并证明随 H 增大，有限时域可控性 Gramian 的主特征子空间收敛到可控不稳定子空间。

Result: 在线性系统上建立了上述理论结果；在非线性视觉控制任务（CartPole、Walker2D、PointMaze）上进行了实证，表明结论可从线性系统推广到非线性视觉控制任务，并展示了控制感知表征学习的优势。

Conclusion: 仅靠下一步预测+反坍缩正则不足以保留可控不稳定模态；引入动作重构（逆动力学）损失可获得控制感知表征，理论保证编码器在有限时域可达子空间上单射并保留可控不稳定方向。线性和非线性视觉控制实验均支持该结论，凸显控制感知表征学习的价值。

Abstract: Robotic systems often exhibit unstable modes, along which small perturbations and disturbances can cause unbounded growth unless corrected through feedback. Controlling such systems from high-dimensional visual observations requires representations that preserve these modes. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) provide a natural framework for learning such representations and their dynamics from visual data. However, we demonstrate that next step prediction combined with anti-collapse regularization does not guarantee that controllable unstable modes are preserved: the training loss can be minimized while these modes are collapsed, making stabilization from the learned representation impossible. To address this, we augment world-model training with an action reconstruction objective (i.e., an inverse dynamics loss) that encourages control-aware representations, namely, visual representations that preserve crucial features for control. We prove that exact action reconstruction makes the encoder injective on the finite-horizon reachable subspace. Thus, the encoder cannot discard any state direction reachable by an action sequence within $H$ steps. Moreover, we show that, as $H$ grows, the dominant eigenspace of the finite-horizon controllability Gramian converges to the controllable unstable subspace. We establish our theoretical results for linear systems and demonstrate empirically that our findings extend to nonlinear visual control tasks (CartPole, Walker2D, and PointMaze), highlighting the benefits of control-aware representation learning.

</details>


### [63] [What Must Replay Preserve? Separating Correctable Bias from Class Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07077)
*BoRen Deng,Xiangyue Ma,Chenglong Li,Xiaoting Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一个诊断框架，将缓存预测视为时间异质监督，通过分组编辑与任务级偏置校正，区分logit replay中“存储分数本身”与“分类器近期类偏置”的作用。在CIFAR-100上，删除后期类匹配的14.9点损失经偏置校正后降至1.8点，而破坏类对应的损失（约4.0点）持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习需在无任务标签下识别所有已见类，DER/DER++等logit replay方法通过匹配存储样本的历史预测来缓解遗忘。但直接删除这种匹配虽能显示其收益，所得精度损失无法判断：究竟是存储分数本身必需，还是损失主要源于分类器对近期类的偏置，以及该损失能否通过偏置校正消除。

Method: 提出诊断框架，把缓存预测视为时间异质监督：将类分为“存储时已知类”与“之后学到类”两组并分别编辑；在任务级偏移前后评估每个模型，该偏移不改变任务内预测（fixed-head设定）。实验以CIFAR-100 + DER++为主，并对比图像蒸馏。

Result: CIFAR-100 + DER++上，用合适的固定常数替换后期学到类的未刷新存储分数，可在1个百分点等价范围内成立；任务级偏移将删除后期类匹配的损失从14.9点降至1.8点。对存储时已知类的非金标分数重分配（保持数值与各任务目标概率不变）造成4.3点损失，偏移后为4.0点；图像蒸馏中亦存在类似损失。

Conclusion: 在测试的fixed-head设定下，删除后期类匹配的巨大损失主要可由任务级偏移校正，说明其很大程度源于分类器对近期类的偏置而非存储分数本身；但破坏类对应关系带来的较小损失无法被该偏移消除，表明存储分数所承载的类对应信息确实必要。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning must recognize all classes seen so far without task labels. Logit replay methods such as DER and DER++ mitigate forgetting by matching the model's past predictions on stored examples. Deleting this matching reveals its benefit, but the resulting accuracy cost cannot show whether the stored scores themselves are needed, or whether the cost survives correction of the classifier's bias toward recent classes. We propose a diagnostic framework that treats a cached prediction as temporally heterogeneous supervision: it separates classes known when an example was stored from classes learned afterward, edits each group, and evaluates every model before and after a task-level offset that leaves within-task predictions unchanged. On CIFAR-100 with DER++, suitable fixed constants replace the unrefreshed stored scores of later-learned classes within an equivalence margin of 1 percentage point, and the offset reduces the cost of deleting their matching from 14.9 to 1.8 points. Reassigning the non-gold scores of classes known at storage, which preserves their values and each task's target probability, costs 4.3 points before and 4.0 after the offset, and a parallel cost persists in image distillation. In the tested fixed-head setting, the large cost of deleting later-class matching is thus mostly correctable by this offset, whereas the smaller cost of disrupting class correspondence persists. Code and data are available at anonymous.4open.science/r/replay-preserve-E22B.

</details>


### [64] [Few-Shot Bioactivity Prediction with Meta-Learning under Assay Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07079)
*Michal Kmicikiewicz,Tommy Rochussen,Vincent Fortuin,Ewa Szczurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MetaHeta框架，通过条件化相关测定（assay）的辅助数据来缓解元学习中测定异质性问题，提升少样本生物活性预测与化合物优选效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期药物发现中单个测定测量数据过少，难以独立训练可靠模型；元学习虽适合少样本场景，但其效果可能受测定异质性限制，作者旨在验证并解决这一问题。

Method: 提出MetaHeta元学习框架：将预测条件化于来自相关测定的辅助数据，相关性依据可用测定信息灵活定义。架构上结合对大规模辅助数据的线性注意力与对稀缺任务特定上下文的精确注意力，从而在高效扩展前者同时保持对后者的精确注意力。

Result: 在ChEMBL与BindingDB的测定数据上验证，MetaHeta改善了少样本生物活性预测，并在回顾性贝叶斯优化中提升了化合物优选（compound prioritization）表现。

Conclusion: 测定异质性会削弱元学习性能；通过显式建模并利用相关测定的辅助信息，MetaHeta可有效缓解该问题，为少样本生物活性预测及下游化合物优选提供更优方案。

Abstract: Accurate bioactivity prediction is a central challenge in early-stage drug discovery, as individual assays often contain too few measurements to train reliable models independently. Meta-learning offers a principled approach to this few-shot setting, but assay heterogeneity may limit its effectiveness. Here, we test this hypothesis and show that meta-learning performance degrades as meta-training tasks become more heterogeneous. To address this, we introduce MetaHeta, a meta-learning framework that accounts for assay heterogeneity by conditioning predictions on auxiliary data from related assays, with relatedness defined flexibly from available assay information. The architecture of MetaHeta combines linear attention over large auxiliary datasets with exact attention over scarce task-specific context, enabling efficient scaling to the former without compromising exact attention over the latter. We demonstrate the benefits of our approach on assays from ChEMBL and BindingDB, improving few-shot bioactivity prediction and downstream compound prioritization in retrospective Bayesian optimization.

</details>


### [65] [Sample-Optimal Estimation of the Fréchet Inception Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07114)
*Ziyun Chen,Jerry Li,Kevin Tian,Yusong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了已知一个分布时，$d$ 维高斯分布间 FID 估计的样本复杂度：经验 plug-in 估计存在有限样本偏差，需 $\gtrsim d^2$ 样本；提出任意阶外推去偏框架及 RTD 算法，达到最优 $O(d/\epsilon^2)$ 样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID 广泛用于评估生成模型，但其经验 plug-in 估计器存在有限样本偏差，导致小样本下评估不可靠。作者希望从理论上刻画 FID 估计的样本复杂度，并设计去偏估计器以降低所需样本量。

Method: 在已知一个分布、两分布为有界均值距离与协方差的 $d$ 维高斯设定下：(1) 建立经验 plug-in 估计器的紧致偏差与方差界；(2) 将 FID$_\infty$ 估计器推广为任意阶 $k$ 的外推方法，并给出紧致的偏差与方差界；(3) 提出相对泰勒去偏（RTD）算法，借鉴 U-统计量思想进行高效去偏。

Result: 经验 plug-in 估计器偏差为 $\Theta(d^2/n)$、方差为 $\Theta(d/n + d^2/n^2)$，样本复杂度 $\gtrsim d^2$；任意阶 $k$ 外推的偏差为 $\Theta(d^{k+2}/n^{k+1})$、方差为 $\Theta(d/n + d^2/n^2)$；RTD 达到 $O(d/\epsilon^2)$ 样本复杂度并证明其最优。合成高斯实验验证了预测的残差偏差与界的紧致性；ImageNet 上 RTD 在标准 50K 样本预算下估计误差最低，VALE$_2$ 仅用 10K 样本即达到 FID$_\infty$ 在 50K 样本下的精度。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上给出了 FID 估计的紧致样本复杂度，并通过外推去偏和 RTD 算法显著降低所需样本量，实验验证了理论界的紧致性与方法的有效性，为小样本下可靠的生成模型评估提供了理论与实用工具。

Abstract: The Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) is widely used to evaluate generative models, but its empirical plug-in estimator suffers from finite-sample bias [BSAG18, CF20]. We study the sample complexity $n$ of estimating FID to error $ε$ between $d$-dimensional Gaussians with bounded mean distance and covariances, when one distribution is known. Our contributions are threefold. (1) We establish tight finite-sample $Θ(\frac{d^2}{n})$ bias and $Θ(\frac{d}{n} + \frac {d^2} {n^2})$ variance bounds for the empirical plug-in estimator, establishing a $\gtrsim d^2$ sample complexity. (2) To debias the empirical plug-in estimator, we generalize the ${\rm FID}_\infty$ estimator of [CF20] to extrapolation methods of arbitrary order $k$. We further prove tight bias and variance bounds of $Θ(\frac{d^{k + 2}}{n^{k + 1}})$ and $Θ(\frac d n + \frac{d^2}{n^2})$ for any order-$k$ extrapolation under our framework. (3) We introduce Relative Taylor Debiasing (RTD), a new, computationally efficient FID estimation algorithm using debiasing techniques inspired by U-statistics. We show that RTD achieves an $O(\frac d {ε^2})$ sample complexity, and prove that this is optimal. We provide a complementary empirical evaluation of our new estimators. Our experiments on synthetic Gaussians validate the predicted residual bias and support the tightness of our bounds. On ImageNet with Inception embeddings, RTD achieves the lowest mean estimation error at the standard 50K sample budget, while our second-order variance-aware extrapolation estimator (VALE$_2$) uses only 10K samples to achieve accuracy comparable to FID$_\infty$ at 50K samples.

</details>


### [66] [CNet: A Complex-Valued Deep Learning Framework with Wirtinger Autodifferentiation and FFT--Hadamard Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08592)
*Marcel Crasmaru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CNet是一个C++/CUDA复数神经网络框架，支持Wirtinger导数、物理原生Born规则测量与CUDA/CPU双实现对拍，并在因果序列建模、射频调制分类和相干衍射成像三任务中验证复数网络能学到物理正确结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习框架以实数运算与softmax分类为主，缺乏对复数神经网络（CVNNs）及复数函数梯度优化（Wirtinger/CR-calculus）的原生支持；同时物理信号处理任务天然是复数域运算，用实数网络建模需人为拆分实虚部，难以保证物理一致性。

Method: 构建CNet框架：网络为作用于幅度向量的复数（常为酉算子，如DFT）级联，分类采用Born规则 p_k=|z_k|²/‖z‖²；每层提供CPU参考实现与CUDA内核并用有限差分校验，计算图按batch克隆以适配GPU；基于恒等式 conv(x,k)=IFFT(FFT(x)·FFT(k)) 加入可学习复数卷积等信号处理原语，并实现真正的Adam优化器与降内存推理模式。提出O(N log N)因果傅里叶混合器（三角掩码DFT，用Bluestein/chirp-z分解求值）构建全复数FNet式因果序列模型。

Result: 三项研究：(1) 全复数、FNet式因果序列模型在字符级语言建模上，经调优后匹配或超过参数量相当的实值因果FNet，并以不到一半训练步数达到实值模型的收敛质量；(2) 射频调制分类（RML2016.10a）瓶颈分析；(3) 相干衍射成像傅里叶相位问题的瓶颈分析，二者精确指出复数网络仍需新算子的位置。三任务中复数表述均可证明学到物理正确结构。

Conclusion: CNet表明物理原生、复数域梯度优化的网络在效率与物理一致性上具有优势：因果复数傅里叶混合器能以更少训练步数达到或超越实值基线，同时瓶颈分析明确了复数网络在射频调制分类与相位恢复中仍缺乏的算子方向。

Abstract: CNet is a C++/CUDA framework for building and training deep complex-valued neural networks (CVNNs) and, more generally, for optimizing complex-valued functions by gradient descent with Wirtinger (CR-calculus) derivatives. It takes a physics-native stance: a network is a cascade of complex -- and often unitary (the DFT) -- operations acting on an amplitude vector, and classification is a Born-rule measurement $p_k = |z_k|^2 / \|z\|^2$ rather than a softmax over real logits. Every layer ships a CPU reference and a CUDA kernel checked against finite differences, and the computation graph is cloned across the batch for GPU execution. On top of the base layers we add signal-processing primitives that turn the identity conv(x,k) = IFFT(FFT(x) . FFT(k)) into a learnable complex convolutional network, together with a true-Adam optimizer and a reduced-memory inference mode.
  We report three studies. First, a fully complex-valued, FNet-style causal sequence model built on a new $O(N \log N)$ causal Fourier mixer -- a triangular-masked DFT evaluated by a Bluestein / chirp-z factorization: once properly tuned it matches or exceeds a parameter-matched real-valued causal FNet on character-level language modeling, reaching the real model's converged quality in under half the training steps. Second and third, bottleneck analyses on radio-modulation classification (RML2016.10a) and the Fourier phase problem of coherent-diffraction imaging, which isolate exactly where complex-valued networks still need new operators. Across all three the complex formulation provably learns the physically correct structure.
  Code: https://github.com/crasmarum/CNet

</details>


### [67] [When Attention Does Not Explain the Peak: Temporal Reference vs. Forecast Output in Attention-Based Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07080)
*Yuji Akamatsu,Takao Yamanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过负载预测模型的 horizon-level 交叉注意力描述子 Ψ_out 检验"注意力即解释"的假设，发现结构化、对输入敏感且可复现的注意力模式并不能有效解释预测峰值的时序行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力图常被解读为预测模型决策依据的证据。本文质疑在负载预测中，高度被关注的未来时段是否真的能解释预测峰值出现的时间，即注意力的"峰值选择性解释"是否成立。

Method: 设计 horizon-level 交叉注意力描述子 Ψ_out：由历史需求的 CLS 表示通过交叉注意力查询 24 个未来外生变量 horizon token。在 Panama 负载数据集 31 个 day-aligned 窗口上，比较预测峰值时间误差与 Ψ_out argmax 误差；通过 ±1 h 范围内的注意力相对均匀基线的倍率考察选择性；用跨窗口一致性评估结构；用替换 12 个未来天气特征为训练集均值做消融；并跨 3 个随机种子重复实验。

Result: 预测峰值时间中位误差 0 h、精确匹配率 51.6%，而 Ψ_out argmax 中位误差 5 h、精确匹配率 0%；31 个窗口中有 27 个预测峰值更接近观测峰值。±1 h 内注意力仅达均匀基线的 1.16×、1.11×、1.14×（分别围绕观测、预测、周幼稚峰值），显示弱且非选择性的集中。但注意力剖面具有结构（跨窗口一致性 0.83），替换未来天气特征为均值后近乎均匀，说明对未来天气变化敏感；结果跨 3 个随机种子可复现。

Conclusion: 结构化、输入敏感且可复现的 horizon-level 交叉注意力并不必然构成对预测行为的有效峰值选择性解释。观测行为更符合注意力承担"内部 horizon 参考"角色以整合未来外生信息，但该功能角色尚未被因果证实。

Abstract: Attention maps are often interpreted as evidence of what a forecasting model uses when making predictions. In our load-forecasting model, a CLS representation of historical demand queries 24 future exogenous horizon tokens through cross-attention, inviting a temporal interpretation in which highly attended horizons may appear to explain forecast peak timing. We test this interpretation using a horizon-level attention descriptor, $Ψ_{\mathrm{out}}$. Across 31 day-aligned windows of the Panama load dataset, the forecast achieves a median peak-time error of 0 h and a 51.6% exact-match rate, whereas the argmax of $Ψ_{\mathrm{out}}$ has a median error of 5 h and 0% exact match. The forecast peak is closer to the observed peak in 27 of 31 windows. This dissociation is not merely an argmax artifact: within $\pm1$ h, attention reaches only $1.16\times$, $1.11\times$, and $1.14\times$ the uniform baseline around observed, predicted, and weekly-naive peaks, respectively, indicating weak and non-selective concentration. Yet the attention profile is structured, with cross-window consistency of 0.83. Replacing 12 future weather features with their training-set means makes the profile nearly uniform, showing sensitivity to future weather variation rather than fixed horizon position alone. The dissociation is also reproduced across three random-seed runs. These results show that structured, input-sensitive, and reproducible horizon-level cross-attention need not provide a valid peak-selective explanation of forecast behavior. The observed behavior is instead consistent with an internal horizon-reference role for integrating future exogenous information, although this functional role is not causally established.

</details>


### [68] [A theory of platonic representations in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07168)
*Darshil Doshi,Wenjie Zhou,Corinna Elena Wegner,Daniel J. Korchinski,Santiago Acevedo,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上解释了多语言模型中间层跨语言表示相似的现象：在具有跨语言共享抽象层级、语言特定表层的数据层级结构假设下，贝叶斯最优预测器等价于信念传播（BP），其消息逐层编码可解析预测Transformer行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型内部层中翻译句子的表示高度相似（与柏拉图表示假说相关），但该现象缺乏理论解释，本文旨在给出一个可验证的理论框架。

Method: 假设数据存在隐藏层级结构，其抽象层级跨语言共享、表层为语言或模态特定；构造由概率上下文无关文法生成的合成语言，各语言共享上层产生式规则但不共享下层规则；在此设定下推导贝叶斯最优下一词预测器为信念传播，并将BP消息逐层编码，与在相同数据上训练的Transformer进行对比验证。

Result: BP逐层消息编码的解析预测与Transformer训练结果吻合良好；框架解释了跨语言相似度在中间层达到峰值、与语言特定结构共存、并随语言接近度、模型质量与数据暴露量增强的原因；区分了相似性（共享邻域几何）与对齐（共享坐标），指出后者在代码混合数据充足时出现；并预测从每一层减去可由前一层线性预测的分量会提升跨语言相似度，在预训练LLM中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为多语言表示相似性提供了基于层级数据生成过程与信念传播的理论解释，统一说明了中间层峰值、相似与对齐的区别及线性可预测分量削减效应。

Abstract: Representations of translated sentences are similar in the inner layers of multilingual language models -- an observation connected to the platonic representation hypothesis, yet unexplained theoretically. We provide an explanation based on the assumption that data have a hidden hierarchical structure whose abstract levels are shared across languages while surface levels are modality- or language-specific. Concretely, we generate synthetic languages from probabilistic context-free grammars sharing upper-level but not lower-level production rules. In this setting the Bayes-optimal next-token predictor is belief propagation (BP); encoding its messages in successive layers yields analytical predictions that agree well with transformers trained on the same data. The framework explains why cross-lingual similarity peaks in middle layers, coexists with language-specific structure, and strengthens with language proximity, model quality and data exposure. It distinguishes similarity (shared neighborhood geometry) from alignment (shared coordinates), showing that the latter occurs when code-switched data, i.e. mixed-language sentences, are abundant enough. It further predicts that subtracting from each layer the component linearly predictable from the preceding one increases cross-lingual similarity, which we confirm in pretrained LLMs.

</details>


### [69] [SchemaFill: Efficient LLM Tool Calling via Slot-Parallel Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07086)
*Zhi-Kai Chen,Song-Yan Li,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SchemaFill框架，通过槽位并行推测解码加速LLM工具调用的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过生成结构化工具调用与外部系统交互。给定用户请求、对话上下文和工具模式目录，工具调用模型需选择工具并生成参数，且可能在一次响应中产生多个调用。标准自回归解码逐token生成，在多调用或多参数字段时延迟很高。虽然显式参数结构提供了并行生成的机会，但后续参数值可能依赖前面的字段和调用，独立生成的值会与目标模型输出不一致。

Method: SchemaFill是一种槽位并行推测解码框架。它无需预先知道实际调用序列或参数值，即可并发生成未来槽位值作为候选；将跨多个字段和调用的候选拼接，在实际输出前缀下由目标模型验证；只提交通过验证的token，当候选不一致时由目标模型提供纠正。

Result: 在Glaive和BFCL数据集上，SchemaFill相比自回归解码在端到端吞吐量上最高提升4.05倍。

Conclusion: SchemaFill在保证目标模型验证的前提下，利用跨槽位和跨调用的并行性，显著提升工具调用生成的端到端吞吐量。

Abstract: LLM agents interact with external systems by generating structured tool calls. Given a user request, conversational context, and a catalog of tool schemas, a tool-calling model must select tools and generate their arguments, potentially producing multiple calls in a single response. Standard autoregressive decoding generates these calls token by token, incurring substantial latency for requests involving multiple calls or many argument fields. The explicit argument structure offers opportunities for parallel generation, but later argument values may depend on preceding fields and calls, so independently generated values can differ from the target model's output. We present SchemaFill, a framework for efficient LLM tool calling through slot-parallel speculative decoding. SchemaFill generates future slot values concurrently as candidates, without requiring advance knowledge of the actual call sequence or argument values. Candidates spanning multiple fields and calls are concatenated for verification by the target model under the actual output prefix. Only verified tokens are committed, and the target supplies corrections when candidates disagree. This applies target verification while exploiting parallelism across slots and calls. On Glaive and BFCL, SchemaFill achieves up to a 4.05$\times$ improvement in end-to-end throughput over autoregressive decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/Czzzk/SchemaFill.

</details>


### [70] [Conditional Flow Matching for Transport Between Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07229)
*Syamantak Kumar,Dheeraj Nagaraj,Saptarshi Roy,Purnamrita Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫过程轨迹之间的传输问题，提出基于流匹配的算法学习源到目标轨迹分布的最优传输映射并保持马尔可夫结构，给出了总体一致性与有限样本误差界及下界，并在EEG图像检索任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受时间序列领域自适应中的序列到序列传输问题启发，需要在仅有有限源和目标轨迹样本的条件下，学习源分布到目标分布的轨迹传输映射，同时保持过程固有的马尔可夫结构，以应对如EEG跨被试高变异性等实际迁移需求。

Method: 提出基于流匹配（flow matching）的算法，从源与目标轨迹分布中学习传输映射并保持马尔可夫结构；在混合时间假设下分析其总体一致性并推导有限样本误差界；同时构造下界论证混合时间相关的样本复杂度不可避免；应用中在ENIGMA解码器的被试特异性时间映射之前加入条件流模块。

Result: 理论上证明了算法在总体极限下的一致性，给出依赖混合时间的有限样本误差界与匹配的下界。实验中在THINGS-EEG2的EEG图像检索任务上，将ENIGMA解码器的平均Top-5检索准确率从43.87%提升至49.05%，相对提升11.82%。

Conclusion: 轨迹传输可在保持马尔可夫结构的条件下用流匹配有效学习，理论上混合时间决定的样本复杂度是本质的，实践中该条件流增强显著缓解了跨被试差异并提升了EEG图像检索性能。

Abstract: Motivated by sequence-to-sequence transport in the context time-series domain adaptation, we study the problem of transportation between trajectories of Markov processes. Given a limited number of trajectories from source distribution and the target distribution, we formulate a flow matching based algorithm which learns a transport map from the source to target trajectory distribution, while preserving the Markov structure. We show that this is consistent in the population limit and derive finite-sample error bounds under mixing time assumptions, following the analysis of classical statistical problems including regression (Nagaraj et al., 2020), principal component analysis (Kumar and Sarkar, 2023), and matrix concentration (Neeman et al., 2024) in the Markov setting. We complement that with a lower-bound construction showing that a mixing-time dependent sample complexity is unavoidable even with regular Gaussian conditional transitions. We evaluate on synthetic and real-world data. For image retrieval from electroencephalography (EEG) on THINGS-EEG2 (Gifford et al., 2022), the task is to identify the viewed image from EEG signals captured from human subjects, which suffers from high inter subject variability. We augment the ENIGMA decoder (Kneeland et al., 2026) with a conditional flow before its subject-specific temporal map. This improves mean top-5 retrieval accuracy from 43.87% to 49.05%, an 11.82% relative improvement.

</details>


### [71] [Muon Is Theoretically Wrong For Convolutions, But Empirically Effective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07103)
*Thibaut Boissin,Thomas Massena,Mathieu Serrurier,Franck Mamalet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Muon优化器在卷积核上的理论解释问题，提出在卷积算子几何中直接形式化优化目标并引入Conv-NS（卷积Newton-Schulz）方法，实验发现其与reshape-based Muon精度相当，进而探讨理论与实践不匹配的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器对矩阵值更新有清晰的理论解释，但卷积核以四维张量存储，标准实现将其reshape为矩阵，这一捷径破坏了Muon背后的理论理解。因此需要直接在卷积算子几何中研究其优化目标。

Method: 在卷积算子几何中直接形式化相应的优化目标，提出Convolutional Newton-Schulz（Conv-NS），在该几何中近似极因子（polar factor）同时保持核支撑（kernel support）。

Result: 在快速训练实验中，Conv-NS与reshape-based Muon均计算高效，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet分类任务上取得相当精度。与预期中理论对齐的Conv-NS应优于reshape-based Muon不符，作者假设精确的卷积正交化可能过度约束更新。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了Muon的强实践性能，同时为其在卷积上的进一步发展开辟了方向，代码已公开。

Abstract: Muon, an optimizer known for its efficiency, has a clear interpretation for matrix-valued updates, but convolutional kernels are stored as four-dimensional tensors. Standard implementations reshape these tensors into matrices, a shortcut which breaks the theoretical understanding behind Muon. To investigate this, we formalize the corresponding optimization objective directly in convolutional operator geometry and introduce Convolutional Newton-Schulz (Conv-NS), which approximates the polar factor in this geometry while preserving kernel support. When applied in fast training experiments, Conv-NS and reshape-based Muon are both computationally efficient and achieve comparable accuracy on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet classification tasks. However, as one could expect a theoretically aligned Conv-NS to outperform reshape-based Muon, we investigate this mismatch between practice and theoretical understanding, with the hypothesis that exact convolutional orthogonalization may overconstrain updates. These findings highlight Muon's strong practical performance while opening directions for its further development on convolutions. Our code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/thib-s/muonconv-cifar10-airbench}{github conv-muon}.

</details>


### [72] [Active Feature Acquisition for Cost-Efficient Temporal Prediction with Reduced Participant Burden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07452)
*Yunni Qu,Bing Cai Kok,Whitney Ringwald,Grant King,Aidan Wright,Kathleen Gates,Junier Oliva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种树蒸馏方法，从基于神经网络的纵向主动特征获取（LAFA）模型中学习可解释策略，在模拟数据与实证EMA数据上均能以极小精度损失显著减少每次采集的条目数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学预测依赖密集纵向数据，但增加每次采集的变量数会加剧被试负担，导致无应答与流失。需要在保证预测精度的前提下减少采集条目。已有的LAFA方法虽能优化动态条目子集选择，但多基于难以解释的神经网络，实践可用性受限。

Method: 提出一种树蒸馏（tree distillation）方法，从基于神经网络的LAFA网络中学习可解释的策略。通过将NN策略蒸馏为决策树形式，在预测目标结果的同时，学习在各时间点动态选择条目的可解释规则。采用模拟数据与真实EMA数据集（预测每日饮酒量）进行验证。

Result: 在模拟实验与实证EMA数据（每日饮酒量预测）中，均能显著减少每次采集的条目数量，且预测精度损失极小。

Conclusion: 树蒸馏可在保持预测性能的同时，将NN-based LAFA策略转化为可解释策略，为缓解密集纵向研究中的被试负担与预测精度矛盾提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of pathological outcomes is a central problem in psychology. To do so, psychologists often collect intensive longitudinal data. However, in such studies, the desire to acquire a large number of variables for the sake of accurate prediction is often counteracted by the need to minimize participant burden. Acquiring more variables per occasion can yield better predictions, but having too many acquisitions increase the risk of non-response and attrition. Longitudinal Active Feature Acquisition (LAFA) is a principled approach to resolve this conundrum. Instead of requiring responses to every item at every acquisition occasion, LAFA produces a policy that seeks to optimally select dynamic subsets of items to be acquired at each timepoint while preserving our ability to forecast a specific outcome. However, existing LAFA methods are mostly based on Neural Networks (NN) that are difficult to interpret in practice. In this work, we introduce a tree distillation method for learning an interpretable policy from NN-based LAFA networks. We validated our method through both a simulation and an empirical EMA dataset on forecasting daily alcohol consumption. In both cases, we find that we can meaningfully reduce the number of items acquired at each occasion with minimal loss in accuracy. Networks (NN) that are difficult to interpret in practice. In this work, we introduce a tree distillation method for learning an interpretable policy from NN-based LAFA networks. We validated our method through both a simulation and an empirical EMA dataset on forecasting daily alcohol consumption. In both cases, we find that we can meaningfully reduce the number of items acquired at each occasion with minimal loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [73] [LiLib: Lifelong Air-to-Ground Path-Loss Prediction on UAVs via a Drift-Triggered Model Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07111)
*Minh Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiLib：一种轻量级持续学习方案，无人机维护小型RLS专家库，通过窗口残差检测漂移并复用/新建专家，在四种城市化场景中显著降低空对地路径损耗预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机作为中继或基站需要精确的空对地路径损耗预测以支持速率自适应与部署，但传播条件随飞行区域（郊区、城区、高层建筑区）变化，且同一区域常被重复访问。在线回归器需遗忘重新学习，单一模型则平均了不兼容的传播机制，均不理想。

Method: 提出LiLib：无人机维护一个由递归最小二乘（RLS）专家组成的小型库；使用带窗残差检验检测漂移；触发短探测阶段后，要么复用最佳已存专家，要么创建新专家。支持专家库在同侪无人机间传递初始化。

Result: 基于四种标准城市化剖面的仿真：预测RMSE从最优滑窗基线的5.89 dB降至4.03 dB（p < 0.001）；返回已知环境后的短期误差从12.3 dB降至5.7 dB；速率自适应中恢复制度感知oracle吞吐量的99%；专家库存储4个专家占用不足0.5 KB；无标签条件下制度识别纯度达92%；第二架无人机用同侪专家库初始化后，环境变化后的误差减半。

Conclusion: LiLib未达到oracle水平，且阴影强烈时相近制度可能被合并。结果表明，对于反复出现的漂移，记忆比重新自适应更有效。

Abstract: UAVs that act as relays or base stations need accurate air-to-ground path-loss predictions for rate adaptation and placement, but propagation conditions change as a UAV moves between suburban, urban and high-rise areas, and the same areas are often revisited. Online regressors that adapt by forgetting must relearn each environment from scratch, whereas a single model trained on all data averages incompatible regimes. We propose LiLib, a lightweight continual-learning scheme in which a UAV maintains a small library of recursive-least-squares experts. A windowed residual test detects drift; a short probe phase then either reuses the best stored expert or creates a new one. In simulations based on four standard urbanization profiles, LiLib reduces prediction RMSE from 5.89 dB (best sliding-window baseline) to 4.03 dB (p < 0.001), lowers the error shortly after a return to a known environment from 12.3 dB to 5.7 dB, and recovers 99% of the throughput of a regime-aware oracle in rate adaptation. The library stores four experts in under 0.5 KB, and identifies regimes with 92% purity without labels. When a second UAV is initialized with the library of a peer, its error after environment changes halves. LiLib does not reach the oracle, and similar regimes may be merged when shadowing is strong. The results indicate that, for recurring drift, remembering is more effective than re-adapting.

</details>


### [74] [Structure, Not Belief: Correlated Thompson Sampling from LLM-Derived Covariance in Combinatorial Semi-Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07470)
*Vikram Kakaria,Anish Kataria,Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将多臂老虎机臂间关系由LLM一次性分区编码为RBF核相关矩阵Σ，使组合汤普森采样（CTS）的后验采样具有相关性（仅塑造采样动态、不注入信念），在理想化高斯采样器下给出贝叶斯遗憾界，并在合成与真实新闻基准上显著降低遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTS对每个臂独立采样后验，探索动态忽略了臂间关系，导致在存在结构性关联的实例上产生冗余探索、遗憾偏高。作者希望以最小改动引入臂间相关性（尤其是由LLM提供的高层分区先验），从而加速探索，同时保持信念更新完全由真实奖励驱动。

Method: 对臂集合，LLM仅被查询一次以给出一个分区；通过簇排名的RBF核将分区转换为正定相关矩阵Σ；每轮后验样本以Σ为协方差进行相关采样，而Beta后验仍仅由真实奖励更新（LLM影响"采样器如何移动"而非"相信什么"）。理论上给出理想化高斯采样器的自包含贝叶斯遗憾界，其信息增益分解为来自K簇结构的K log T项与增长至d log T的岭项。实验包括16个合成Bernoulli族、Microsoft MIND-small新闻基准（d=200真实文章），并做无LLM消融：用受控质量的模拟oracle，对比随机分区、采样噪声的简单退火，以及与oracle质量匹配的信念注入。

Result: 相关采样器在T=2500时相对CTS在16个合成Bernoulli族上降低遗憾19%（T=25000、数据自适应核时为6-7%），在MIND-small（d=200）上降低41%；伪观测热启动无增益。√(d/K)的改进属有限时域瞬态，仅当簇内相关趋于1时精确。无LLM消融显示：在无结构实例上，增益源于核形状本身（随机分区或对采样噪声的简单退火即可复现），而与oracle质量匹配的信念注入从不带来帮助。

Conclusion: LLM的作用实质是提供一种有效的相关性/核形状先验来塑造采样的探索几何，而非注入关于臂价值的信念；收益主要来自核形状，且随水平线增长而衰减为瞬态改进，信念注入无额外价值。

Abstract: Combinatorial Thompson sampling (CTS) draws independent posterior samples for every arm, so its exploration dynamics ignore any relation among arms. We study a minimal change to those dynamics: an LLM is queried once for a partition of the arms, the partition becomes a positive-definite correlation matrix $Σ$ through an RBF kernel on cluster ranks, and the per-round posterior sample is drawn with covariance $Σ$ while the Beta posteriors are updated from real rewards only, so the LLM shapes how the sampler moves, not what it believes. We give a self-contained Bayesian regret bound for the idealized Gaussian sampler whose information gain splits into a $K\log T$ term from the $K$-cluster structure and a ridge term that grows to $d\log T$: the $\sqrt{d/K}$ improvement over independent sampling is a finite-horizon transient, exact only as the within-cluster correlation tends to one. The correlated sampler reduces regret by 19% over CTS on 16 synthetic Bernoulli families at $T=2{,}500$ (6-7% at $T=25{,}000$ with data-adaptive kernels) and by 41% on the Microsoft MIND-small news benchmark ($d=200$ real articles), while pseudo-observation warm starts give nothing. An LLM-free ablation with a simulated oracle of controlled quality shows that on unstructured instances the gain is a property of the kernel shape (a random partition, or a plain tempering of the sampling noise, reproduces it), while belief injection at matched oracle quality never helps.

</details>


### [75] [Learning a Mixture of GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07562)
*Tiago da Silva,Amauri H. Souza,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出统一理论框架描述GFlowNet混合模型，分为连续索引与离散索引两类，并据此提出Stratum-Conditioned GFlowNets，实现并行训练、加速收敛与模式覆盖且几乎不增加计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNet集成采样器虽能提升状态空间探索与收敛性，但存在显著运行时开销，且集成方法与基础模型之间的概念联系尚不清晰，缺乏统一理论解释。

Method: 提出描述GFlowNet混合模型的通用理论框架，并特化为连续索引（CI）与离散索引（DI）两类集合。CI被解释为随机特征展开；DI涵盖既有训练方法，并支撑新提出的Stratum-Conditioned（SC）GFlowNets——受Markov链Doob h变换启发，按给定模块函数分解状态空间并限制各分量在特定子集采样，支持集中式与分量级高度并行训练。

Result: CI GFlowNets在图结构任务中可证明提升采样器表达力，并通过谱移减小学习不稳定性；DI GFlowNets涵盖先前方法；SC GFlowNets的集中式与分量级训练均显著加速学习收敛并提升模式覆盖，且未引入可忽略外的额外计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为GFlowNet集成提供了统一理论视角，并通过SC GFlowNets在不增加显著计算开销的前提下实现加速收敛与更优模式覆盖，兼具理论解释力与实用效率。

Abstract: Learning an ensemble of GFlowNets to sample from a discrete target distribution has become a common approach for achieving better state space exploration and convergence than that of a monolithic sampler. However, these methods often add a substantial runtime overhead to the base model, and their conceptual connection remains elusive. To address this, we first propose a general-purpose theoretical framework for describing a mixture of GFlowNets, which we specialize into continuously (CI) and discretely indexed (DI) collections. On the one hand, we show CI GFlowNets can be interpreted through the lens of a random features expansion, provably boosting the sampler's expressivity in graph-structured tasks and reducing learning instability via spectral shifting. On the other hand, we demonstrate DI GFlowNets encompass prior approaches for GFlowNet training and provide the foundation for the newly proposed Stratum-Conditioned (SC) GFlowNets. This method, which is inspired by the Doob's h-transform of Markov chains, decomposes the state space according to a prescribed modular function and restricts each component to sample from a distinct subset of it. Importantly, SC GFlowNets support centralized and component-wise embarrassingly parallel training, and we show both of them significantly speed up learning convergence and mode coverage without introducing any non-negligible extra computation.

</details>


### [76] [Will the Judge Flip? Predicting Position-Sensitive LLM Judgments from Residual Stream Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07115)
*Hashmath Shaik,Gnaneswar Villuri,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM评判者(position bias/顺序偏见)的顺序翻转可预测性问题：利用首次裁决前的残差流激活训练线性探针，在JudgeBench与MT-Bench上均能有效预测翻转，且优于使用置信度、logits等非激活特征的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判在评估两个候选回答时，呈现顺序会影响其裁决（position flip）。常规检测需对每一对进行正反两次评判，使评判成本翻倍。作者希望探索是否能在不增加评判次数的前提下，仅凭首次裁决前的内部激活预测该翻转倾向。

Method: 在首次裁决前记录残差流激活，使用嵌套分组交叉验证，在JudgeBench的534对样本上评估正则化线性探针，覆盖三个Qwen3裁判与Llama-3.1-8B。将探针与一个组合基线进行对比，该基线使用口头置信度、裁决标签logits、回答长度及裁判初始选择。进一步在JudgeBench上训练后冻结探针，直接迁移至1,802个MT-Bench比较进行测试。

Result: 线性探针AUROC达.621–.850，比组合基线高.062–.113 AUROC。冻结探针在MT-Bench上AUROC为.685–.853，无需MT-Bench拟合或重新校准。

Conclusion: 裁决前的残差流激活携带可预测候选顺序易感性的信息，探针能有效预测顺序翻转，并在跨数据集迁移中保持性能，优于本文评估的非激活类预测器。

Abstract: The order in which candidate responses are presented can change an LLM judge's verdict. Detecting such a position flip ordinarily requires judging each pair in both orders, which doubles the number of judgments. We investigate whether residual stream activations recorded immediately before the initial verdict can predict a flip. We use nested grouped cross-validation to evaluate regularized linear probes on 534 JudgeBench pairs for three Qwen3 judges and Llama-3.1-8B. The linear probes achieve AUROCs of .621-.850 and outperform a combined baseline that uses verbalized confidence, verdict-label logits, response lengths, and the judge's initial choice by .062-.113 AUROC. Linear probes trained on JudgeBench and then frozen achieve AUROCs of .685-.853 on 1,802 MT-Bench comparisons without MT-Bench fitting or recalibration. These results show that pre-verdict activations support prediction of susceptibility to candidate order and outperform the non-activation predictors evaluated here.

</details>


### [77] [Adversarially Trained Linear Transformers Are Optimal Robust In-Context Learners for Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07754)
*Soichiro Kumano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文理论研究了对抗预训练模型鲁棒性向未见任务迁移的可能性，证明深度线性Transformer在混合高斯分类任务族上经对抗预训练后，可通过上下文学习在无额外对抗训练的新任务上达到鲁棒贝叶斯误差，而标准训练模型无法做到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练虽是最可靠的防御手段，但计算成本高且每个新任务都需重新付出代价。鲁棒基础模型提供了替代方案：一次对抗预训练后通过轻量适配迁移鲁棒性。然而一个根本问题尚未解决：预训练获得的鲁棒性能否在没有额外对抗训练的情况下迁移到未见任务。

Method: 针对一族高斯混合分类任务，采用充分深的线性Transformer进行跨任务的对抗预训练，并通过从干净示范中进行上下文学习来评估在未见任务上的表现；同时分析梯度流下的收敛性、准确率-鲁棒性权衡以及示范复杂度。

Result: 充分深的线性Transformer经跨任务对抗预训练后，可通过干净示范的上下文学习，在未见任务上渐近达到鲁棒贝叶斯误差；而标准训练的模型无法达到这一目标。此外论文还分析了梯度流收敛性、准确率-鲁棒性权衡和示范复杂度。

Conclusion: 对抗预训练获得的鲁棒性可以在无需额外任务特定对抗训练的情况下迁移到未见任务，单一规模化对抗预训练模型即可在新任务上实现最优鲁棒性，为鲁棒基础模型的有效性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Adversarial training is one of the most reliable defenses against adversarial attacks, but its high computational cost must generally be paid anew for each task. Robust foundation models offer a promising alternative: adversarially pretrain a model once and then transfer its robustness to downstream tasks through lightweight adaptation. However, a fundamental question remains open: can robustness acquired during pretraining transfer to unseen tasks without further adversarial training? In this study, we answer this question affirmatively. A single model adversarially pretrained at scale can achieve optimal robustness on new tasks without additional task-specific training. Specifically, we show that, for a family of Gaussian-mixture classification tasks, a sufficiently deep linear transformer adversarially trained across tasks can asymptotically attain the robust Bayes error on previously unseen tasks through in-context learning from clean demonstrations. By contrast, a standardly trained model cannot. We further analyze convergence under gradient flow, an accuracy--robustness trade-off, and demonstration complexity.
