Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, particularly Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) variants, enable efficient adaptation at the edge, the limiting resource for Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) adaptation is often not the number of trainable parameters but the activation state that must be retained until the backward pass. This paper introduces Memory-Floor LoRA (MemFLoRA), a low-rank CNN adapter built around a memory-first design principle rather than a direct application of transformer-oriented LoRA. Instead of merely reducing trainable weights, we define an activation-memory-floor criterion: trainable backward computations must not depend on full-width layer inputs. The resulting adapter freezes the down-projection, trains a scale-matched up-projection, and combines eval-mode backbone normalization with activation-minimal backward rules, reducing saved state to the low-rank branch. Evaluated on three Human Activity Recognition (HAR) datasets and two CNN backbones under subject, body-location, and sensor-placement shifts, MemFLoRA reduces saved-activation memory by 98.5-98.7% and peak training-state memory by 94.9-97.3% relative to full fine-tuning, while matching or exceeding CNN PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [216] [Principled Under Pressure: Post-Training Decides Whether LLMs Act on Their Own Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08670)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了248个场景的预注册测试面板，用"第三视角判断"作为模型自身标准的参照，量化语言模型在压力下"说一套做一套"（言行不一）的差距，并发现该差距取决于后训练配方而非预训练权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型日益作为智能体行动，存在一种评估盲区：模型声称某行动错误却仍会执行它。这类"明知故犯"的失败与"不知对错"的失败性质不同，而仅评估其陈述价值观（stated values）无法捕捉这一差距。

Method: 构建预注册面板，含248个场景、覆盖五类压力。每个场景对同一模型提问两次：一次作为智能体选择行动，一次以第三人称问哪一选项正确，以模型自身判断为基准。每个场景配有移除压力的孪生版；每个模型还配有阳性对照（由操作者命令其执行违规行动），以区分"无差距"与"测量失效"。测试四个instruct模型；另做脱离chat template的对照，以及行动前对利害/规范进行推理的干预实验。

Result: OLMo-3-7B-Instruct在约五分之一施压场景中执行了自己判定为错的行动，且显著多于移除压力的同样场景。四个模型中差距依后训练配方而异：OLMo-3与Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct存在差距；Tulu 3在全面板（阈值约0.01）及自身最高压力场景上均无差距；Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct全面板无差距（约0.02），在自身场景上未定（0.083，-0.028~0.195）。Meta配方与Ai2的Tulu 3同源于Llama-3.1权重，仅Meta的配方携带该差距。在chat template之外读取chat模型会使差距符号反转（OLMo-3为-0.038 vs 模板下+0.055），三种配方中有两种出现此失真。行动前对利害进行推理，在两个携带差距的模型上都将选择拉回自身判断（对照等长非道德任务亦然）；在OLMo-3上，点明所涉规范贡献约三分之一效果。

Conclusion: 该"言行差距"是后训练配方的可测量目标，而非预训练权重的固定属性。

Abstract: Language models increasingly act as agents. An agent that says an action is wrong and then takes it anyway is a different failure from one that does not know better, and evaluations of stated values cannot see it. We build a pre-registered panel of 248 scenarios across five kinds of pressure. Each scenario is posed twice to the same model, once as the agent choosing what to do and once in the third person asking which option is right, so the model's own judgment is the reference. Every scenario has a twin with the pressure removed, and every model gets a positive control in which its operator orders the violating action, so that a missing gap can be told apart from a blind instrument. On OLMo-3-7B-Instruct, the model takes the action it judged wrong on about one in five pressuring scenarios, more often than on the same scenarios with the pressure removed. Across four instruct models the gap depends on the post-training recipe: OLMo-3 and Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct carry it; Tulu 3 shows none on the whole panel (above about 0.01 in probability) or on its own most-pressuring scenarios; Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct shows none on the whole panel (above about 0.02) and is unresolved on its own (0.083, -0.028 to 0.195). Meta's recipe and Ai2's Tulu 3 start from the same Llama-3.1 weights, and only Meta's carries the gap. Reading a chat model outside its chat template reverses the sign of its gap with nothing at stake (-0.038 against +0.055 under the template on OLMo-3), a distortion present on two of three recipes. On both models that carry it, reasoning about the stakes before acting moves the choice back toward the model's own judgment, against a same-length non-moral task, with or without the pressure; on OLMo-3, naming the norm at stake does about a third of that. The gap is a measurable target for post-training recipes, not a fixed property of pretrained weights.

</details>


### [217] [Variance-Optimal Off-Policy Evaluation with Conjunct Effect Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08677)
*Nicolò Felicioni,Michael Benigni,Maurizio Ferrari Dacrema,Paolo Cremonesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VOCEM估计器，通过最优插值系数在OffCEM与DR之间权衡，降低离策略评估方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文赌博机的离策略评估中，动作级重要性加权方差过大。双重稳健（DR）估计虽在共同支撑下无偏，但保留了高方差的动作级权重；OffCEM用更稳定的簇级权重替代，却依赖奖励模型的局部正确性。二者各有代价，需要一个能兼顾两者的方法。

Method: 作者证明在DR与OffCEM所需假设下，存在一族无偏估计器可在两者之间插值。据此提出VOCEM估计器，选择插值系数以最小化方差，并以闭式解给出总体最优系数。

Result: 理论上，VOCEM的方差不超过OffCEM或DR任一端点。实验在受控合成环境及两个大动作空间基准上进行，在全部23个评估条件下均优于两个端点，表现出更强的稳定性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在DR与OffCEM假设成立的条件下，可通过最优插值实现无偏且方差最小的估计，VOCEM为高方差动作级加权的离策略评估提供了更稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) for contextual bandit policies becomes challenging when action-level importance weighting incurs excessive variance. Doubly robust (DR) estimation remains unbiased under common support but retains these high-variance action-level weights. A prior estimator, Off-policy evaluation with Conjunct Effect Model (OffCEM), replaces them with more stable cluster-level weights, at the cost of relying on local correctness of the reward model. In this paper, we show that, under the assumptions required by DR and OffCEM, there exists an unbiased family of estimators that interpolates between OffCEM and DR. Building on this result, we propose the Variance Optimal-CEM (VOCEM) estimator, which selects the interpolation coefficient to minimize variance. We derive the population-optimal coefficient in closed form and show that the resulting estimator has variance no larger than either endpoint, OffCEM or DR. Experiments in controlled synthetic settings and on two large-action benchmarks show that VOCEM improves upon both endpoints in all 23 evaluated conditions, exhibiting greater stability and empirical robustness.

</details>


### [218] [A Systematic Study of Small Language Models on Abstract Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08680)
*Nur A Zarin Nishat,Jens Lehmann,Andrei Aioanei,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究以ARC-TGI为受控基准，对超过1000次训练的三种小型语言模型进行剖析，发现抽象推理的端点准确率无法揭示模型是否真正习得可迁移规则；性能高度依赖优化、任务族、评估分布与响应格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象推理基准上的端点准确率无法区分模型是习得了可迁移规则，还是仅拟合了分布特异的规律性。作者希望在可控条件下厘清这一区别，并系统刻画技能获取的效率与稳定性、分布外鲁棒性及其与模型族和任务表述的交互。

Method: 在ARC-TGI基准上，将抽象网格变换组织为可控任务族，支持重采样、空间平移与跨基准迁移。对仅解码器、编码器-解码器、混合专家三类模型族在监督微调下开展1000余次运行，考察技能获取效率与稳定性、分布外泛化、模型族与任务表述的交互，并进行逐层注意力特征诊断；同时对比可执行规则归纳与直接网格生成两种响应格式。

Result: 分布内准确率可达较高水平，但技能获取对优化敏感且在任务族间分布不均；分布外性能急剧下降，即使规则保持不变仅改变网格尺度亦如此。增加训练集深度与广度带来不均衡的收益，额外上下文示例的作用依模型族而异。可执行规则归纳能产生直接网格生成未观察到的正确解。在部分任务上，注意力诊断呈现不同的集中度与上下文依赖模式，但不足以确立一般性因果机制。

Conclusion: 抽象推理分数是条件性的，取决于模型、适配范式、评估分布与响应格式；单凭端点准确率不足以判断规则的可迁移性。

Abstract: Endpoint accuracy on abstract-reasoning benchmarks does not reveal whether a language model has acquired a transferable rule or fit distribution-specific regularities. We study this distinction in small language models on the ARC-TGI benchmark, which organizes abstract grid transformations into controllable task families and supports resampling, spatial shifts, and cross-benchmark transfer. Across more than 1,000 runs, we profile decoder-only, encoder--decoder, and mixture-of-experts model families under supervised fine-tuning. We examine the efficiency and stability of skill acquisition, robustness beyond the training distribution, interactions with model family and task formulation, and layer-wise attention signatures that accompany behavioral differences. Substantial in-distribution accuracy is attainable, but acquisition is sensitive to optimization and unevenly distributed across task families. Performance deteriorates sharply outside the training distribution, including when the rule is retained but grid scale changes. Greater training-set depth and breadth yield uneven gains, while the effect of additional in-context examples depends on model family. Executable-rule induction also yields correct solutions not observed under direct grid generation. On selected tasks, attention diagnostics show distinct concentration and context-dependence profiles, but do not establish general causal mechanisms. Overall, abstract-reasoning scores are conditional on the model, adaptation regime, evaluation distribution, and response format.

</details>


### [219] [Probabilistic Counterfactual Inference for Discrete Outcomes in Gaussian-Process Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08689)
*Juliette Sinnott,Amir-Hossein Karimi,Mohammad Kohandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的概率框架，将高斯过程预测器与显式外生噪声机制配对，实现混合变量类型（连续父节点、离散子节点）下的反事实推断，并证明所得机制可复现拟合模型的观测与干预分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有GP-SCM反事实推断主要针对连续内生变量，无法处理包含离散子节点（而父节点连续）的因果图，应用范围受限。

Method: 为离散结果推导精确的条件噪声消解程序：二值变量采用均匀阈值，名义类别采用Gumbel-max竞争机制，有序变量采用潜高斯切点模型；在干预下传播消解噪声，并计入GP潜函数的后验不确定性。

Result: 在已知真值反事实的合成SCM上评估精度、一致性与耦合误设鲁棒性。关键发现：对有序数据误用类别耦合会使反事实误差约增大三倍，即使观测拟合相当，且该误差随数据增多不下降；随训练集增大，结构方程收敛到真值而反事实误差趋于平台。反向地，对名义数据强加虚假顺序则会损害拟合方程本身。

Conclusion: 耦合方式的选择必须基于结构性理由，而不能仅凭拟合优度判断；错误耦合造成的反事实误差无法通过增加数据消除。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference in Gaussian-process structural causal models (GP-SCMs) has been developed primarily for continuous endogenous variables, limiting applicability to causal graphs that contain discrete child nodes with continuous parents. We introduce a unified probabilistic framework for counterfactual inference with heterogeneous variable types by pairing GP predictors with explicit exogenous noise mechanisms. For discrete outcomes, we derive exact conditional noise-abduction procedures using a uniform threshold for binary variables, a Gumbel-max race for nominal categories, and a latent Gaussian cut-point model for ordinal ones. In each case, we propagate abducted noise through interventions while accounting for posterior uncertainty in the GP latent functions, and prove that the resulting mechanisms reproduce the fitted model's observational and interventional distributions. On synthetic SCMs with known ground-truth counterfactuals, we evaluate estimation accuracy, consistency, and robustness to coupling misspecification. A key finding is that applying a categorical coupling to ordinal data inflates counterfactual error roughly threefold even when observational fit remains comparable, and that this error does not diminish with more data. As the training set grows, the fitted structural equation converges to the truth while the counterfactual error flattens onto a floor. In the reverse direction, forcing a false order onto nominal data instead degrades the fitted equation itself. The choice of coupling must therefore be justified on structural grounds rather than read off the fit.

</details>


### [220] [GeneICL: A Tabular Foundation Model for Bulk Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08694)
*Michael Bohl,Alexander Theus,David Wissel,Valentina Boeva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 GeneICL，一个基于转录组感知预训练先验和参数高效循环架构的 420 万参数表格基础模型，在 80 项临床结局预测任务上取得最优综合排名，且参数量最多减少 387 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据虽被广泛测量，但临床结局预测受高维、强特征相关和标注数据有限制约；大型自监督转录组基础模型常不及简单监督基线，而表格基础模型多基于通用合成数据预训练，缺乏转录组结构。作者据此提出核心问题：缺失的关键是否为转录组感知的预训练而非模型规模。

Method: 提出 GeneICL 表格基础模型，含 420 万参数：将基于实测 bulk 表达谱构建的半合成预训练先验与参数高效循环架构相结合；并通过 Cox 偏似然残差将右删失生存预测无训练地归约为回归任务。

Result: 在覆盖分类、回归与生存分析的 80 项临床结局预测任务上，表格基础模型整体优于自监督转录组模型；GeneICL 在评估的基础模型与调优基线中取得最佳综合排名，且参数量最多减少 387 倍，推理无需梯度更新，可在笔记本 CPU 上数秒内完成预测。

Conclusion: 结果表明转录组感知的预训练先验（而非规模）是关键要素，GeneICL 以极小参数量和高效推理实现了跨任务的最优综合性能，凸显其为临床结局预测提供了一种轻量、实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Gene expression is widely measured in biomedicine, yet clinical outcome prediction remains challenging due to high dimensionality, strong feature correlations, and limited labeled data. Large self-supervised transcriptomic foundation models often fail to outperform simple supervised baselines. Tabular foundation models offer an alternative through in-context learning, but are typically pretrained on generic synthetic data rather than transcriptomic structure. We ask whether transcriptomics-aware pretraining, rather than scale, is the missing ingredient. Towards this end, we introduce GeneICL, a 4.2M-parameter tabular foundation model combining a semi-synthetic pretraining prior built from measured bulk expression profiles with a parameter-efficient recurrent architecture. We further enable right-censored survival prediction via a training-free reduction to regression using Cox partial-likelihood residuals. We evaluate GeneICL on 80 clinical outcome-prediction tasks spanning classification, regression, and survival. Tabular foundation models consistently outperform self-supervised transcriptomic models, while GeneICL achieves the best overall rank among evaluated foundation models and tuned baselines. GeneICL does so with up to 387$\times$ fewer parameters, no gradient updates at inference, and predictions within seconds on a laptop CPU.

</details>


### [221] [Optimal and Efficient Online Inverse Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08735)
*Anupam Gupta,Guru Guruganesh,Honghao Lin,Vahab Mirrokni,Renato Paes Leme,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在在线逆线性优化中给出了首个确定性、多项式时间、达到最优遗憾 $O(\sqrt d)$ 的算法，正面回答了 Sakaue 提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线逆线性优化中，学习者需在不观察目标的情况下学习专家固定的未知线性目标。Sakaue 近期用随机算法取得了最优遗憾 $O(\sqrt d)$，但每轮需 $(dT)^{O(d)}$ 次线性优化，计算代价极高，因此提出能否在多项式时间内达到该最优遗憾的开放问题。本文旨在解决这一计算效率问题。

Method: 提出一种确定性算法，是 Sakaue 等与 Cai 等变度量（variable-metric）算法的变体：当查询点距离做度量更新处足够远时，撤销该度量更新。该机制避免了随机化与指数级线性优化开销。

Result: 算法对任意时间跨度 $T$ 均取得遗憾 $O(\sqrt d)$（确定性界），且运行时间为 $d$ 与 $T$ 的多项式，显著优于此前 $(dT)^{O(d)}$ 每轮线性优化次数。

Conclusion: 确定性多项式时间算法可实现 $O(\sqrt d)$ 最优遗憾，正面回答了 Sakaue 的开放问题，表明在线逆线性优化的最优遗憾并不需要随机化或指数级计算代价。

Abstract: In online inverse linear optimization, a learner recommends an action and then observes the choice of an expert who maximizes a fixed, unknown linear objective on $\mathbb{R}^{d}$; the goal is to learn to optimize this objective without observing it. Sakaue recently obtained the optimal regret $O(\sqrt d)$ with a randomized algorithm making $(dT)^{O(d)}$ linear optimizations per round, and asked whether it can be attained in polynomial time. We answer positively: our deterministic algorithm has regret $O(\sqrt d)$ for every horizon $T$ and runs in time polynomial in $d$ and $T$. It is a variant of the variable-metric algorithms of Sakaue et al.\ and Cai et al., in which a metric update is revoked once the query point moves far enough from where the update was made.

</details>


### [222] [On the Computational Tractability of Robust Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08740)
*Vanessa Kosoy,Vinayak Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究bandit场景下不可实现学习（环境不属于假设类）的鲁棒bandit问题。针对已有工作仅有遗憾界但缺乏计算保障的问题，识别出一个特殊情形，给出多项式时间学习算法，达到 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾，并证明该特殊情形的若干小幅推广是NP-hard的，表明该情形处于可处理性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不可实现学习依赖agnostic learning保证，但在监督学习之外难以获得。近期提出的imprecise/robust bandits为bandit场景的不可实现学习提供了新思路，并已有 $\Theta(\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾的学习器，但缺乏计算复杂度保障。作者希望填补这一计算可行性空白，并指出高效学习器对AI对齐问题可能具有关键意义。

Method: 识别鲁棒bandit问题中的一个特殊情形，为其设计多项式时间学习算法；同时通过归约证明该特殊情形的若干小幅推广是NP-hard的，以此界定可处理性边界。

Result: 对识别的特殊情形给出多项式时间学习器，实现 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾；证明该情形的多个小幅推广为NP-hard，说明该特殊情形恰好位于可处理与不可处理的边界。

Conclusion: 本文在鲁棒bandit的不可实现学习问题上取得计算可行性进展：找到一个多项式时间、近最优遗憾的特殊情形，并通过NP-hard性结果刻画其紧邻边界的性质。作者将其视为面向AI对齐所需计算高效学习器的一个小步骤。

Abstract: Learning when the environment does not belong to the learner's hypothesis class is typically handled using agnostic learning guarantees. However, for anything beyond supervised learning, agnostic guarantees are difficult to come by. Recently, imprecise bandits (Kosoy, 2025) (later renamed to robust bandits in Appel and Kosoy, 2025) were introduced as another approach to unrealizable learning in the bandits setting and a $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret learner was shown for a large class. However, no computational guarantees were provided. In this paper we identify a special case that admits a polynomial-time learner with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret. We also show that several small generalizations of this special case are NP-hard thus indicating that the special case is at the boundary of what is tractable. It has been recently suggested (Kosoy, 2018) that computationally efficient learners for unrealizable learning problems are crucial for solving the AI alignment problem. This work is a small step in that direction.

</details>


### [223] [Reinforcement Learning with Conformal Action Sets: An Application to Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08743)
*Wenwen Si,Honghao Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLCP（带校准剪枝的强化学习），利用critic分数与在线阈值自适应调整保留动作集，理论上给出代理漏检率上界与价值损失分解，并在两个数据集上验证多样性与会话深度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐通常使用固定列表长度，但会话内有用候选数量是变化的，固定slate size导致资源浪费与多样性受限，因此需要自适应地裁剪动作集。

Method: RLCP基于critic分数和在线阈值自适应确定保留动作集，阈值由“集合是否包含代理目标动作”的二值反馈更新；理论上证明沿自适应轨迹的代理漏检率确定性上界，并将价值损失精确分解为过滤损失与选择损失。

Result: 在代理与critic近似条件下，该分解给出考虑不完美选择与集合截断的有限会话奖励上界，且不要求学习参数收敛。在KuaiRand-Pure与MovieLens 1M上，19种配置中至少一种RLCP变体取得最高目录多样性，为最强基线的1.11×至5.21×，会话深度具竞争力且保留集不更大。

Conclusion: RLCP通过校准剪枝实现了自适应动作集规模，在理论上提供代理漏检率与奖励的可控界，在实验上显著提升目录多样性而不牺牲会话深度或增加集合规模。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders typically use a fixed slate size even though the number of useful alternatives changes within a session. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Calibrated Pruning (RLCP), which adapts the retained action set using critic scores and an online threshold. The threshold is updated from binary feedback indicating whether the set contains an action in a proxy target. We prove a deterministic bound on the observed proxy miss rate along adaptive trajectories. To quantify the effect of pruning on reward, we derive an exact decomposition of value loss into filtering and selection losses. Under explicit proxy and critic approximation conditions, this decomposition yields a finite session reward bound that also accounts for imperfect selection and set truncation, without requiring the learning parameters to converge. Experiments on KuaiRand-Pure and MovieLens 1M compare two RLCP implementations with four RL baselines. In each of the 19 configurations, at least one RLCP variant achieves the highest catalog diversity, reaching $1.11\times$ to $5.21\times$ that of the strongest baseline, with competitive session depth and no larger retained sets.

</details>


### [224] [Linear Bandits under Exact Sliding-Window Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08745)
*Seyed Mohammad Hadi Hosseini,Yasin Abbasi-Yadkori,Sattar Vakili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究精确滑动窗口约束下的线性赌博机问题：离线时证明凸性与循环平移不变性使平稳解最优；在线时指出仅靠几何结构不足，提出转移直径τ与历史状态直径D，分别设计稀有切换OFUL算法，取得次线性遗憾界，并在实验中保持精确可行性且策略更新大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动窗口约束要求任意连续动作块属于给定可行集，这类约束在现实决策中常见，但现有线性赌博机研究多未处理精确滑窗约束。论文旨在刻画该类约束下离线最优结构，并解决在线学习中几何结构不足以支撑学习、次线性遗憾可能不可实现的问题。

Method: 离线：在奖励函数已知时，分析凸性与循环平移不变性对平稳解最优性的作用，w∣T时为最优，否则有O(w)加性间隙。在线：引入转移直径τ量化可行可达性，提出稀有切换OFUL算法；进一步去除循环不变性，将近期动作历史建模为有限记忆控制问题的状态，引入历史状态直径D，结合乐观剩余视野规划与稀有策略更新设计算法。

Result: 在循环平移不变设定下，相对离线最优可行轨迹取得Õ(d√T+τd+w)遗憾；在一般滑动窗口约束下取得Õ(d√T+dD+w)遗憾。在真实与合成基准上，方法保持精确可行性，奖励与遗憾与基线相当，同时策略更新次数显著更少。

Conclusion: 精确滑窗约束下，离线最优可用平稳性刻画；在线学习需要额外可达性度量。转移直径τ与历史状态直径D分别捕捉两类约束下的可行通信结构，配合稀有切换/稀有策略更新可实现次线性遗憾。实验验证方法在可行性与更新效率上的优势。

Abstract: We study linear bandits under exact sliding-window constraints, where every consecutive block of actions must belong to a prescribed feasible set. In the offline setting, where the reward function is known, we show that convexity and cyclic-shift invariance make a stationary solution optimal when $w\mid T$ and within an additive $O(w)$ gap otherwise. In the online setting, we show that geometric structure alone is insufficient for learning, and sublinear regret can be impossible. We introduce a transition diameter $τ$ that quantifies feasible reachability and develop a rare-switching OFUL algorithm with regret $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T}+τd+w)$ against the offline-optimal feasible trajectory. Finally, we remove cyclic invariance and consider general sliding-window constraints, where optimal behavior may be non-stationary. We represent recent action history as the state of a finite-memory control problem and introduce a history-state diameter $D$ that measures feasible communication between viable histories. Combining optimistic remaining-horizon planning with rare policy updates, we obtain a regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T}+dD+w)$. We evaluate our approach on real-world and synthetic benchmarks, showing that it maintains exact feasibility while achieving reward and regret comparable to baselines with substantially fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [225] [Neural Petri flows for chemical reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08750)
*Jose Eduardo Escrig Molina,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Neural Petri Flow（NPF），一种在任意权重取值下仍严格保持 Petri 网语义的架构：硬编码守恒所强制的点火形式与局部性，仅学习速率律或分类读出。该模型在原子映射、反应分类与正向预测三类任务上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Petri 网的反应模型与神经网络多将网结构当作消息传递的脚手架，其学习到的语义并不保证符合 Petri 网本身（守恒性、使能规则等）。作者追问：什么样的架构在其权重取任意值时仍然是 Petri 网。

Method: 从理论出发，指出 Petri 网的所有语义共享点火形式 m′=m+Cσ、局部性（使能只读取变迁的输入）与使能规则；证明守恒性强制该点火形式、非负性在局部速率律下强制使能规则。由此仅保留速率律（即变迁点火倾向）为可学习部分，提出 NPF：学习速率律或分类读出，其余全部硬编码为无参层。在价键网（valence net）上，原子映射、反应分类与正向预测被统一为同一点火向量上的三个任务。

Result: 1) 无训练条件下，最小点火向量在 Golden 集上映射准确率 88.8%，高于 RXNMapper 的 85.6%；在 EnzymeMap 酶反应上为 88.7%，高于 77.9%。2) 在 USPTO-480K 上，NPF 基于这些点火向量训练可预测 87.7% 的产物，仅用 1% 训练子集时仍达 67.4%。3) ECREACT 的 EC 号在第三层级预测准确率 90.2%，领先最佳已发表方法 5.6 个百分点。4) 以电子为 token 时，同一 token 博弈对 FlowER 基元步骤的首位预测 90.5%，超过已发表基线，且所有 top-1 预测均为有效分子，无需过滤器。

Conclusion: 通过将 Petri 网中由守恒与局部性所强制的结构与使能规则硬编码为无参层，只学习速率律，NPF 在任意权重下都保持严格的 Petri 网语义，并在原子映射、反应分类与正向预测等多个化学任务上取得优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Petri nets have been used to describe chemical processes such as reactions.They map well to chemistry: Places are the bonds between atoms and the free valence of each atom, a token is a unit of bond order, a transition forms or breaks a bond, the conserved quantities are the valence budgets of the atoms, and the enabling rule is the valence rule. These semantics are not guaranteed by learned models of reactions or neural networks that are built on Petri nets that use the net as a scaffold for message passing. Here, we ask what architecture remains a Petri net for every value of its weights. We find the answer in the theory, where all semantics of a net share the firing form $m^\prime=m+Cσ$, locality, as enabling reads only the inputs of a transition, and the enabling rule, and we prove that conservation forces the firing form and that non-negativity forces the enabling rule on local rate laws. This leaves free the rate law, which is the propensity of each transition to fire. We introduce Neural Petri Flow, which learns this rate law, or a readout for classification, and hard-wires the rest as parameter-free layers. On what we denote a valence net, atom mapping, reaction classification, and forward prediction become three tasks on one firing vector. Without training, the minimum firing vector maps 88.8% of the curated Golden set against 85.6% for RXNMapper, and 88.7 against 77.9% of the enzymatic reactions of EnzymeMap. On USPTO-480K, NPF trained on these firing vectors predicts 87.7% of the products and 67.4% when trained on a 1% subset of the training reactions. EC numbers of ECREACT are predicted at the third level for 90.2% of reactions, 5.6 points ahead of the best published method. With electrons as tokens, the same token game predicts 90.5% of the elementary steps of FlowER first, ahead of the published baseline, and every top-1 prediction is a valid molecule without a filter.

</details>


### [226] [Conformal Prediction Sets Quantify Information Gain: A Theoretical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08785)
*Kevin Zhang,Stephen Bates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用面向集合值预测的决策论广义熵，为“预测集大小作为不确定性度量”提供信息论基础，并证明集合大小缩减与香农互信息的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测能给出带有限样本覆盖保证的预测集，但预测集大小常被当作不确定性的启发式指标，其信息论依据尚不清晰，本文旨在补齐该理论基础。

Method: 引入基于共形预测集大小与覆盖率的广义信息度量族，给出香农互信息的精确积分表示；在标准分类设定下，证明额外信息导致的共形集大小缩减（i）被该族中依赖校准的成员夹逼，（ii）满足数据处理不等式，误差至有限样本校准与模型误差项。

Result: 理论上将共形预测与经典信息论量关联，并支持以集合大小缩减作为信息增益度量；在11个分类设定上验证了理论，且在贪心特征选择实验中显示集合大小缩减与香农互信息对特征的排序可能不同。

Conclusion: 共形预测的集合大小可被赋予信息论解释，但其与香农互信息并不等价，实际应用中两者对特征的评价可能不一致。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a popular tool for uncertainty quantification that outputs prediction sets with finite-sample coverage guarantees. While prediction set size is commonly used as a heuristic measure of uncertainty, the information-theoretic basis for this interpretation remains poorly understood. In this work, we provide such a foundation using a decision-theoretic generalization of entropy tailored to set-valued prediction. In particular, we introduce a family of generalized information measures based on the size and coverage of conformal prediction sets. Notably, Shannon mutual information admits an exact integral representation in terms of these measures. We then show that, in standard classification settings, the reduction in conformal set size from additional information (i) is sandwiched between calibration-dependent members of this family and (ii) obeys a data processing inequality, both up to finite-sample calibration and model error terms. Together, our results formally relate conformal prediction to classical information-theoretic quantities and justify using set-size reduction as an information gain metric. Empirically, we validate our theory across 11 classification settings and show that set-size reduction and Shannon mutual information can rank features differently in a greedy feature selection experiment.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Statistical Turbulence and High-Fidelity Disturbance Fields for Quadrotor Flight Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06874)
*Xun Huang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种风场保真度等级（无风、离散1-cosine阵风、统计湍流、合成相干结构、大涡模拟大气边界层）对PPO四旋翼控制器鲁棒性的影响，发现"训练-测试"矩阵出乎意料地平坦，最廉价的结构化训练风（离散阵风域随机化）在所有测试列中均排名第一，而控制权限（而非风场真实度）才是决定鲁棒性的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习四旋翼控制器通常仅在简化风模型下训练，然而风场"保真度"（而非风速大小）对策略鲁棒性的影响尚未被量化。作者旨在厘清：投入更高保真度的风场建模是否能实际提升控制器的泛化与鲁棒能力，从而指导训练风场的投入取舍。

Method: 构建五种递进的扰动保真度等级：无风飞行、离散1-cosine阵风、统计湍流、合成相干结构、大涡模拟（LES）大气边界层场；采用全交叉"训练-测试"评估矩阵对PPO智能体进行评测，并以级联PID与几何SE(3)控制器作为免训练基线参考，覆盖0–12 m/s风速区间。在控制器对比之前，先对所有扰动数据进行验证：每个合成生成器均定量对照其解析解或认证标准，LES场则对照设定的对数律剖面。实验平台为悬停状态的竞速级四旋翼，并在对风敏感的27 g平台上复现排序。

Result: 训练-测试矩阵极为平坦：不同保真度训练出的策略在各测试风场下表现差异很小；最廉价的结构化训练风（离散阵风域随机化）在所有测试列中排名第一，该排序在27 g风敏感平台上得到复现；经一次整定的几何控制器能够以零坠机率覆盖所有学习型PPO策略的性能区间。机制诊断表明，限制鲁棒性的是控制权限而非风场真实度。

Conclusion: 风场保真度的投入应根据平台的"风敏感性"来定标：对于控制权限充足、风敏感度低的平台，高保真风场建模带来的鲁棒性收益有限，廉价的域随机化即可达到最优效果。这为强化学习四旋翼控制器的训练风场设计提供了"按需保真"的实用准则，并强调了控制权限在鲁棒性中的主导地位。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning quadrotor controllers are usually trained under simplified wind models, yet the impact of wind-field fidelity, as opposed to magnitude, on policy robustness remains unquantified. This paper compares five disturbance-fidelity levels, from wind-free flight and discrete 1-cosine gusts through statistical turbulence and synthetic coherent structures to large-eddy-simulation fields of the atmospheric boundary layer, in a full cross-fidelity train test evaluation of proximal policy optimization (PPO) agents, with cascaded PID and geometric SE(3) controllers as training-free references, over a 0-12 m/s wind sweep. Before any controller comparison is made, all disturbance data are validated: every synthetic generator is checked quantitatively against its analytical or certification-standard reference, and the large-eddy-simulation fields against the imposed log law. On a racing-class quadrotor in hover, the train test matrix is remarkably flat, and the cheapest structured training wind, which is discrete-gust domain randomization, ranks first in every test column, a ranking replicated on a wind-sensitive 27 g platform; a once-tuned geometric controller brackets the learned PPO policies at zero crash rate. Mechanism diagnostics show that control authority, not wind realism, bounds robustness, so wind-fidelity investment should scale with platform wind sensitivity.

</details>


### [228] [Impact of bubble nucleation on the functioning of the pulsating heat pipe: numerical simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06901)
*Vadim Nikolayev,Iaroslav Nekrashevych*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用自研PHP数值模拟程序CASCO，研究气泡生成（沸腾）对10匝水-铜管式脉动热管振荡特性的影响，通过改变成核过热度ΔTnucl与热负荷Pe，获得热阻Rth变化规律及流型图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气泡生成（沸腾）对PHP运行有积极作用，通常预期Rth(ΔTnucl)单调递增；但小ΔTnucl下该函数反而递减并出现极小值，其机理（运行模式转变）尚需阐明。

Method: 使用内部开发的PHP数值模拟代码CASCO，对10匝水-铜管式PHP（蒸发段各匝独立加热）进行模拟。固定其他参数，仅改变成核势垒（即成核局部过热度ΔTnucl）与热负荷Pe，在水平及垂直有利倾角下计算热阻Rth，并分析蒸发器内液塞平均穿透深度的差异，绘制Pe–ΔTnucl流型图。

Result: 大ΔTnucl时Rth(ΔTnucl)单调递增，符合预期；小ΔTnucl时Rth(ΔTnucl)递减，因而存在极小值。该极小值源于稳定运行模式之间的转变，不同模式下液塞进入蒸发器的平均穿透深度显著不同；水平PHP中对应间歇振荡与连续振荡两种模式。

Conclusion: 气泡生成对PHP性能的影响非单调：Rth随ΔTnucl存在极小值，由运行模式转变（液塞穿透深度差异）引起。水平PHP下模式对应间歇与连续振荡，并给出了Pe–ΔTnucl坐标下的运行模式图。

Abstract: In this communication, we discuss results obtained with the numerical in-house PHP simulation code CASCO (French abbreviation for Advanced PHP simulation code). The aim is to study the impact of bubble generation (boiling) on the established oscillation of a PHP with a high turn number (10-turn water-copper tubular PHP with independently heated turns in the evaporator region). We vary the nucleation barrier value, i.e. the local superheating $Δ$Tnucl at which the nucleation occurs and the heat load Pe; other simulation parameters are kept constant. The PHP heat transfer performance is evaluated with the PHP thermal resistance Rth for both horizontal and vertical favorable PHP orientation. Since the bubble generation plays a positive role for the PHP functioning, one expects that the function Rth($Δ$Tnucl) monotonously increases. This is true for high $Δ$Tnucl. For small $Δ$Tnucl, the Rth($Δ$Tnucl) function is decreasing, thus a minimum occurs. It appears because of a transition between the regimes of stable PHP functioning. The regimes strongly differ by the average penetration of liquid plugs into the evaporator. For horizontal PHP, these regimes correspond to the intermittent and continuous oscillations. The regimes are discussed and the regime map in the coordinates Pe -$Δ$Tnucl is obtained.

</details>


### [229] [Well-posed by Design: Learning Constitutive Laws from Velocity Data using Convex Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07236)
*Gonzalo G. de Diego,Georg Stadler*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出ICNNE——输入凸神经网络，通过对称化与惩罚项精确满足凸性、框架无关性、偶性及耗散性，直接学习耗散势而非本构律，解决速度数据反演中前向PDE适定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从速度等间接观测学习复杂流体本构律是PDE约束反问题，表达力强的神经参数化易破坏前向物理模型的适定性，需在结构保证与拟合能力间取得平衡。

Method: 学习耗散势（标量函数）而非本构律，使凸性、框架无关性、耗散性传播到连续介质力学；ICNNE经对称化精确施加凸性、框架无关性与第二应变率不变量的偶性，缓解小应变率下的数值不稳定；以惩罚弱施加势原点零梯度满足耗散性；用有限元与神经网络耦合（Firedrake+PyTorch）计算损失及其梯度。

Result: 在可压缩Navier-Stokes、Herschel-Bulkley屈服应力流、Hibler粘塑性海冰模型及无已知本构律的离散元数据四类问题上，学习到的势（i）在已知情形恢复真值物理，（ii）准确迁移至训练未见几何，（iii）在无结构方法发散的工况下成功。

Conclusion: 将数学结构嵌入参数化（而非损失函数）是从间接数据稳健学习物理的有效路径。

Abstract: Learning constitutive laws of complex fluids from velocity data (i.e. indirect observations) is a PDE-constrained inverse problem in which expressive neural parameterizations risk breaking the well-posedness of the forward physics model. We address this tension by learning, rather than the constitutive law itself, the dissipation potential: a scalar function whose convexity, frame-indifference, and dissipativity propagate into the underlying continuum mechanics and guarantee the structural properties needed for a well-posed and generalizable forward PDE. We introduce ICNNE, an input-convex neural architecture that exactly enforces convexity, frame-indifference, and evenness in the second strain-rate invariant via symmetrization, alleviating the numerical instabilities that occur at small strain rates in standard formulations. By weakly enforcing zero gradients in the origin of the potential via penalization, ICNNE also satisfies the dissipativity property. The loss objective and its gradient are computed using a combination of finite element and neural network methods, coupling the Firedrake and PyTorch libraries. We evaluate on four problems spanning compressible and incompressible regimes: compressible Navier-Stokes, Herschel-Bulkley yield-stress flow, Hibler's viscous-plastic sea-ice model, and discrete-element-method data with no known constitutive law. We show that the learned potentials (i) recover ground-truth physics where it is known, (ii) transfer accurately to geometries unseen during training, and (iii) succeed in regimes where unstructured methods diverge. These results suggest that embedding mathematical structure into the parameterization, rather than into the loss, is a robust path to learning physics from indirect data.

</details>


### [230] [(Why) Jets quantise trapped acoustic modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07414)
*Brandon Yeung,Michael N. Stavropoulos,Vincent Jaunet,Peter Jordan,Oliver T. Schmidt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 高亚声速湍流射流在势核中捕获声学模态，这些模态位于Strouhal数宽度约O(0.1)的窄频带内，并由O(10)个间距约O(0.01)的离散频率组成；本文通过大涡模拟与纹影实验结合SPOD分析证实该假设，并建立驻波共振模型预测量化全局模态频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证先前关于高亚声速湍流射流势核中声学模态频带内部结构的假设：每个窄频带由多个离散频率组成，并进一步理解这些被捕获模态的物理机制与稳定性本质。

Method: 针对Mach 0.9、雷诺数10^6的射流，采集约100,000个纹影实验快照与大涡模拟(LES)快照；采用带正弦锥化并强制射流旋转与反射对称性的谱本征正交分解(SPOD)算法；结合局部、全局、模态与非模态稳定性理论预测被捕获模态，并基于理论边界构建同相驻波共振模型。

Result: 纹影与LES结果均显示被捕获的SPOD模态在St-x与St-k平面内受边界约束；边界使模态在St、x和k上量化，使St中第n_x阶模态沿x和k有n_x个波腹；发现模态与非模态稳定性之间存在竞争证据；仅由理论边界构建的同相驻波共振模型能预测量化的全局模态频率。

Conclusion: 高亚声速湍流射流势核中的声学模态确实由一系列离散、量化的频率构成，其结构可由理论边界和同相驻波共振模型描述，且模态与非模态稳定性机制共同参与其形成。

Abstract: High-subsonic turbulent jets trap acoustic modes in their potential core. The modes lie in narrow frequency bands that are $O(0.1)$-wide in Strouhal number, $St$. By studying a Mach 0.9, Reynolds number $10^6$ jet, this work confirms an earlier hypothesis: each band is made up of $O(10)$ discrete frequencies, $ΔSt \sim O(0.01)$ apart. To resolve them, we collect $O(100\,000)$ snapshots from dedicated schlieren experiments and large-eddy simulations (LES), and apply spectral proper orthogonal decomposition (SPOD). Our SPOD algorithm uses sine tapers and enforces the jet's rotational and reflectional symmetries. For both the schlieren and the LES, the trapped SPOD modes are bounded in the $St$-$x$ and $St$-$k$ planes, where $x$ and $k$ are respectively the axial coordinate and wavenumber. The bounds quantise the modes in $St$, $x$, and $k$, such that the $n_x$th mode in $St$ has $n_x$ antinodes along $x$ and $k$. We then predict trapped modes with local, global, modal, and non-modal stability theories. We find evidence of a competition between modal and non-modal stability. From only the theoretical bounds, we build a simple in-phase standing-wave resonance model that predicts the quantised global-mode frequencies.

</details>


### [231] [Small density differences, large Richardson numbers: buoyancy decides where gypsum deposits in a centifluidic confluence cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07305)
*Isaac Appelquist Løge,Candela Almazán Pérez,Maike W. Baltussen*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 小通道内两股密度差仅百分之几的流体混合通常按常密度处理，但本文通过实验与模拟证明，当存在化学反应（生成石膏）时该假设失效：沉积位置由浮力主导，须以理查森数而非相对密度差作为判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中普遍认为，当两股水溶液密度差仅为几个百分点或更小时，可用常密度模型描述其在微小通道内的混合。作者质疑该假设在反应体系中是否成立，尤其是微流控/离心流控设备中常见的流速条件下。

Method: 在汇流单元（confluence cell）中令氯化钙与硫酸钠溶液相遇生成石膏，进行反应流动实验与常密度数值模拟对比；并以染料示踪实验系统考察密度差（0.14%–2.1%）、三种通道高度和32个流量条件下的混合行为，用整体理查森数 Ri = g′H/U² 关联染料流到达对侧壁面的距离。

Result: 常密度模拟预测石膏沿通道中线的底面沉积，但实验观察到石膏在两侧壁形成条带，且流量降低时沉积带起始位置向上游移动。染料实验表明染料流到达对侧壁的距离由 Ri 控制，遵循 L/H = 3.31 Ri^-0.66，覆盖四个数量级的 Ri（R²=0.89）。当两股流体密度匹配时，多数条件下染料流不再到达对侧壁，密度匹配的反应实验也得到底面沉积，与常密度模拟一致。

Conclusion: 相对密度差小并不构成忽略浮力的充分条件，还必须要求理查森数足够小；而在离心流控装置典型流速下，Ri 往往并不小，因此常密度假设在此类反应流动中不成立。

Abstract: The mixing of two aqueous streams in small channels is usually modeled with constant density when the difference in density between the streams is only a few percent or less. In this paper, we investigate whether this holds in a confluence cell where calcium chloride and sodium sulfate meet and form gypsum. A constant density simulation of the reacting flow results in deposition on the floor along the channel centreline. However, in the experiment, gypsum instead forms bands on both side walls, and the deposition bands start further upstream as the flow rate decreases. Dye experiments with density differences between the streams of 0.14 to 2.1\,\%, three channel heights, and 32 flow rates show that the distance at which the dyed stream reaches the opposite side wall is governed by the bulk Richardson number, $Ri=g'H/U^2$, and follows $L/H = 3.31\,Ri^{-0.66}$ over four decades of $Ri$ ($R^2=0.89$). When the two streams' densities match, the dyed stream no longer reaches the opposite wall under most conditions, and in a density-matched reacting experiment, gypsum deposits on the floor, as the constant-density simulation predicts. A small relative density difference is thus not a sufficient condition for neglecting buoyancy: the Richardson number must also be small. For the flow rates typical of centifluidic devices, it often is not.

</details>


### [232] [Equations of motion for dust particles in dust-collecting devices (Translated article from USSR Journal Otoplenie i Ventilyatsiya, No. 4 (1934): 27-29)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07315)
*L. S. Klyachko*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 这是一篇英文译本的序言：作者克服馆际互借困难，获得并翻译了Klyachko 1934年关于流体中球体阻力的俄文原始文献，以纠正西方文献中的二手误传并提升其可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西方文献对该1934年工作的引用稀少且描述、评论多不准确，多数作者似仅凭二手信息、未读原文；同时该文原始期刊难以通过图书馆系统获取，故需翻译并公开出版以便全球英文读者查阅。

Method: 文献检索与馆际互借获取扫描件；阅读俄文原文并与现代文献中的各类关联式进行比较；将该历史性论文翻译为英文并发表于公开文献。

Result: 作者获得莫斯科俄方图书馆提供的扫描件；发现Klyachko 1934年流体中球体阻力公式与现代文献中的多种关联式相比最为简洁优雅。

Conclusion: 决定翻译并公开发表该历史文献，以纠正西方学界的二手误读，并使英文读者便捷获取这一被忽视的重要工作。

Abstract: Preface for the English Translation: While checking out early USSR journal articles for our literature search, we recently had tremendous difficulty acquiring this 1934 Klyachko original publication through our library systems. After obtaining and reading a scanned copy of it supplied by the Russian library in Moscow, we realized that relatively scarce citations of this work in the Western literature are often accompanied by inaccurate descriptions and comments, indicating that most Western authors might have gotten only second-hand knowledge about it and never seemed to have read the original article. Compared with various correlation formulas in the modern literature, the Klyachko 1934 formula for sphere drag in fluid flow appears to be the most elegant and cleanest one thus far. Therefore, we decided to make an effort to translate this historical work and publish it in the open literature to facilitate its accessibility to English readers worldwide.

</details>


### [233] [Only Linear Constraints Survive Coarse-Graining: Evaluating Physics-Constrained Neural Operators on Stochastically Forced Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07811)
*Michael Groom,Rafael Oliveira*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 在粗粒化随机强迫数据上,PDE残差损失并不严格成立;作者利用滤波后仍有效的线性守恒约束(连续性与全局动量平衡),在FNO输出上施加闭式投影,以极低成本显著提升稳定性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息损失将离散PDE残差加入数据项,但仅当所施加方程在被拟合场上是封闭的才严格有效。对于粗粒化、随机强迫的数据,非线性项与滤波算子一般不交换,粗粒化场不满足原始方程,将残差驱零会把隐式闭合嵌入学习算子,导致物理不一致与训练偏差。

Method: 在周期域、归一化且平移不变滤波下,常数系数线性约束在滤波网格上仍保持有效。对二维不可压Navier-Stokes方程,选取连续性与全局动量平衡两条此类约束,在Fourier神经算子(FNO)输出上施加闭式投影,计算复杂度为O(N log N)。实验在二维各向同性湍流(截断使强迫完全位于截断波数之外)与已解析Kolmogorov流上进行,并与普通FNO、物理信息FNO及测试时优化作对比。此外,对非线性约束,用从粗粒训练数据单独拟合的常数亚网格通量闭合全局能量平衡后再施加。

Result: 投影后连续性误差为5.0×10⁻⁷,对比普通FNO的0.51与物理信息FNO的0.17;全局动量平衡误差为8.6×10⁻¹¹,对比7.5×10⁻⁴与8.3×10⁻⁴。投影仅增加2%训练时间,并将448步内能量保持有界的rollout比例从0.18提升到0.87。在已解析Kolmogorov流上,测试时优化每条轨迹的代价约为投影的3,300倍,且最大散度高出三个数量级。对非线性约束,用常数亚网格通量闭合全局能量平衡,消除了强制未闭合平衡带来的20.9%能量亏缺,并使所有自由运行rollout在2,000步内保持有界。

Conclusion: 线性、平移不变的守恒约束在粗粒化滤波网格上依然严格成立,可通过闭式投影以近乎零成本精确施加,兼顾物理一致性与长期稳定性;非线性约束在适当建模其未闭合项后同样可有效施加。该思路为空领域代理模型的物理约束设计提供了可推广的路径。

Abstract: A physics-informed loss adds a discretised PDE residual to the data term, but is only strictly valid if the equations being enforced are closed on the fields being fitted. This fails on coarse-grained, stochastically forced data: nonlinear terms in the PDE generally do not commute with the filter, so the coarse-grained fields do not satisfy the original equations, and driving their residual to zero encodes an implicit closure into the learned operator. Under normalised, translation-invariant filtering on a periodic domain, constant-coefficient linear constraints remain valid on the filtered grid. For the 2D incompressible Navier-Stokes equations, two such constraints are continuity and global momentum balance. We enforce these exactly by applying a closed-form projection on the output of a Fourier neural operator (FNO) at $O(N \log N)$ cost. On two-dimensional isotropic turbulence, truncated so that its forcing lies entirely beyond the cutoff wavenumber, the projection achieves a continuity error of $5.0\times10^{-7}$ against 0.51 for a plain FNO and 0.17 for a physics-informed FNO, and a global momentum balance error of $8.6\times10^{-11}$ against $7.5\times10^{-4}$ and $8.3\times10^{-4}$. The projection only costs an extra 2% of training time, and takes the fraction of rollouts whose energy remains bounded at 448 steps from 0.18 to 0.87. On resolved Kolmogorov flow, test-time optimisation costs approximately 3,300$\times$ as much per trajectory as the projection, while leaving maximum divergence three orders of magnitude higher. Nonlinear constraints can also be enforced once their unclosed terms are modelled: closing the global energy balance with a constant subgrid flux, fitted from the coarse training data alone, removes the 20.9% energy deficit that enforcing the unclosed balance produces and keeps every free-running rollout bounded over 2,000 steps.

</details>


### [234] [Number Theory of Decaying Turbulence 3: Comparing with Physical and Numerical Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07433)
*Alexander Migdal,Wei Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将Euler系综解对自由衰减不可压湍流的预测与DNS及风洞实验进行对比，验证了谱塌缩、标度指数与衰减律，并指出理论适用条件与红外截断的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Euler系综理论（系列前两篇论文所推导）对自由衰减湍流的预测能力，明确其在真实有限雷诺数流动中的适用性与局限。

Method: 将Euler系综解的预测与Rodhiya和Sreenivasan的4096³ DNS及Max Planck风洞实验（Re_λ最高5779）对比；预测谱形固定，仅拟合归一化与尺度；通过抛物线律选取湍流吸引子，研究谱塌缩、二阶结构函数指数与衰减律，并考察红外截断k_0的影响。

Result: 坍缩谱仅依赖瞬时雷诺数，Saffman与Loitsyansky初始谱结果一致；外推至无穷雷诺数时，理论与模拟在涡旋量程主体区域吻合到1%；能量平衡精确成立；衰减律E∝t^{-5/4}、Ω∝t^{-9/4}仅在截断进入k^{-7/2}尾部时成立；风洞中纵向指数外推与单尺度奇系综在大分离处吻合；Matsuzawa等人的自由衰减湍流团过小、过不均匀、过弱，未能显现吸引子。

Conclusion: Euler系综解的核心预测得到DNS与实验支持，可靠检验应基于谱主体而非依赖红外截断的衰减律；衰减指数依赖初始红外谱，标准交叉形式在大分离处失效。

Abstract: We compare the predictions of the Euler-ensemble solution of freely decaying incompressible turbulence with simulations and experiments: the scaling function of the energy spectrum, the index of the second-order structure function and the decay laws. The predictions have a fixed shape; only their normalization and scale are fitted. In the final $4096^3$ direct numerical simulations of Rodhiya and Sreenivasan, the turbulent attractor is selected by a parabola law for the growth of the bulk length. Along it the collapsed spectrum depends only on the instantaneous Reynolds number, identically for Saffman and Loitsyansky initial spectra; extrapolated to infinite Reynolds number, it agrees with the theory to 1% in the bulk of the enstrophy range, below the dissipation range. The energy balance holds exactly. The energy integral of the theory diverges in the infrared, so energy and enstrophy depend on how a finite flow cuts off the spectrum: with a sharp cutoff $k_0$, the decay laws, energy $\propto t^{-5/4}$ and enstrophy $\propto t^{-9/4}$, are reached only when the cutoff enters the $k^{-7/2}$ tail of the spectrum. Such a cutoff is only an estimate; the reliable test is the bulk of the spectrum. Fitted to the simulations, the cutoff lies at the lower edge of the bulk, where the spectrum still carries the initial data, and the decay exponents depend on the initial infrared spectrum. In the Max Planck wind tunnel at $Re_λ$ up to 5779, the longitudinal index extrapolated to infinite Reynolds number matches the odd ensemble with one scale parameter at large separations, where standard crossover forms fail. The freely decaying turbulent blob of Matsuzawa et al. is too small, too inhomogeneous and too weak to display the attractor. The theory is derived in the first two papers of this series.

</details>


### [235] [Erasing and Regenerating the Turbulent Cascade Arrow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07449)
*Yang Zhao,Daocheng Zhou,Hongfu Zhang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过 Haar 分布 Hermitian 相位在保留同模二次信息（模态幅值、能谱、同模双时间关联）的前提下随机化湍流相位，发现任何可积奇通量泛函的期望为零，表明"级串方向箭头"需要超出二阶记录的额外信息，而被测非线性 Navier-Stokes 动力学可重建该信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流级串的方向性（正向/逆向能量传递的箭头）由何种信息决定尚不清楚。本文旨在检验：当仅保留同模二次信息（幅值、能谱、同模双时间关联）时，级串的方向选择性是否仍然存在，从而界定方向性所依赖的信息范围。

Method: 构造 Haar 分布 Hermitian 相位系综，保持规定的同模二次信息不变；对相位-Haar 系综中可积奇通量泛函的期望作解析与数值统计；在有限系综上评估平均传递与正向事件偏置；设置独立分块检验尺度-8 均值判据；并在匹配的演化协议中对比非线性 Navier-Stokes 动力学、线性演化与幅值匹配的冻结对照。

Result: 相位-Haar 系综中可积奇通量泛函期望为零；有限系综上平均传递与主分块的正向事件偏置被强烈压制；独立分块保留符号分离但未通过尺度-8 均值判据；在匹配演化协议中，非线性 Navier-Stokes 动力学使选定的带符号通量标量恢复到接近自然参考值，而线性演化与冻结对照不能；通量与局域三次几何标量可同时恢复，但高局域关联可在箭头被抹除后仍存留。

Conclusion: 带符号传递所依赖的信息超出所保留的二次记录，仅凭二次信息不足以支撑级串方向箭头；被检验的非线性动力学能够重建该缺失信息，而线性演化与幅值匹配的冻结控制则不能。

Abstract: Which information tells a turbulent flow which way to cascade? We apply Haar-distributed Hermitian phases that retain prescribed same-mode quadratic information, including modal amplitudes, the energy spectrum, and same-mode two-time correlations. For the phase-Haar ensemble, the expectation of any integrable odd flux functional is zero. Finite ensembles strongly suppress mean transfer and forward-event bias in the primary block. An independent block retains sign separation but fails the scale-8 mean criterion. In matched evolution protocols, nonlinear Navier-Stokes dynamics restores the selected signed-flux scalar close to its natural reference, whereas linear evolution and an amplitude-matched frozen control do not. Flux and a local cubic geometry scalar recover together, yet high local correlation can survive erasure of the arrow. Signed transfer thus requires information beyond the retained quadratic record; the tested nonlinear dynamics rebuilds it.

</details>


### [236] [Vectorial discrete unified gas kinetic scheme for continuum compressible flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07664)
*Yinghao Ma,Ziyang Xin,Zhaoli Guo*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出矢量离散统一气体动理学格式（V-DUGKS），用于连续介质可压缩流动，具有可调Prandtl数、更低参考温度敏感性和更高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量DUGKS在连续介质可压缩流动中效率与稳定性受限，且对数值参考温度敏感；需构建一个能统一处理质量、动量与能量、并支持可调Prandtl数的矢量动理学框架。

Method: 采用矢量动理学框架，质量、动量与总能量由耦合分布函数表示，动量与能量输运分别松弛，实现可调Prandtl数；平衡态分布截断至二阶Hermite；仅需二阶速度矩恢复可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，故四阶Gauss-Hermite求积即可精确求矩；消除三阶Hermite项以使用紧凑离散速度集；采用有限体积、特征通量评估与梯形碰撞积分。

Result: 通过激波管、Shu-Osher、二维黎曼及三维Taylor-Green涡等数值测试验证了精度与鲁棒性；在相同CFL约束下，三维Taylor-Green涡算例获得约2.2–2.9倍加速，得益于更大允许时间步与简化平衡态。

Conclusion: V-DUGKS为连续介质可压缩流动模拟提供了一个稳健且高效的动理学框架。

Abstract: A vectorial discrete unified gas kinetic scheme (V-DUGKS) is proposed for continuum compressible flows. In the vectorial kinetic framework, mass, momentum, and total energy are represented by coupled distribution functions with separate relaxation processes for momentum and energy transport, enabling an adjustable Prandtl number. All equilibrium distributions are truncated at the second-order Hermite level. Since only second-order velocity moments are required to recover the compressible Navier-Stokes equations, fourth-order Gauss-Hermite quadrature is sufficient for exact moment evaluation. Eliminating third-order Hermite terms allows compact discrete velocity sets to be used uniformly for all distribution functions, reducing sensitivity to the numerical reference temperature and improving stability and efficiency over the scalar DUGKS. The scheme employs a finite-volume formulation with characteristic-based flux evaluation and trapezoidal collision integration. Numerical tests, including shock-tube, Shu-Osher, two-dimensional Riemann, and three-dimensional Taylor-Green vortex problems, demonstrate its accuracy and robustness. For the three-dimensional Taylor-Green vortex, V-DUGKS achieves a speed-up of about 2.2-2.9 under the same CFL constraint due to a larger allowable time step and simplified equilibrium formulation. These results show that V-DUGKS provides a robust and efficient kinetic framework for continuum compressible flow simulations.

</details>


### [237] [Airborne liquid marble: Evaporation dynamics of liquid marble in acoustic levitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07703)
*Keigo Noro,Xiao Ma,Koji Hasegawa*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究同步观测了声悬浮液体弹珠的形态与蒸气浓度场，揭示其蒸发与变形的耦合行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体弹珠可作为声悬浮下的无接触微反应平台，但其在声场中的变形、内流与蒸发等复杂行为尚缺乏深入理解。

Method: 采用背光成像与背景导向纹影（BOS）方法，同时可视化声悬浮液体弹珠的液滴形态与周围蒸气浓度场，并测量其蒸发动力学。

Result: 在中等相对湿度（RH=40%）下，液体弹珠的蒸发速率与蒸气分布接近纯水滴，表明颗粒壳影响甚微；在高、低湿度下差异显著，归因于疏水颗粒层引入的蒸发阻力与界面特性。此外，纯水滴保持准球形，而液体弹珠随时间逐渐扁平化，表明颗粒壳施加了力学约束。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了声场中液体弹珠蒸发与变形的耦合机制，可为无污染微流控与微反应器系统设计提供依据。

Abstract: Liquid marbles (LMs), droplets encapsulated by hydrophobic particles, allow for non-wetting manipulation and containerless handling, making them well-suited for applications such as microreactors. When integrated with acoustic levitation, the LMs serve as contact-free reaction platforms. However, the complex behaviours associated with acoustic fields, including deformation, internal flow, and evaporation, remain insufficiently understood. This study investigates the evaporation dynamics of acoustically levitated LMs. Simultaneous visualisation of the droplet morphology and surrounding vapour concentration fields was achieved using backlighting and the Background-oriented Schlieren (BOS) method. At intermediate relative humidity (RH = 40%), the evaporation rates and vapour distributions of LMs closely resembled those of pure water droplets, indicating that the particle shell exerts minimal influence under these conditions. Conversely, significant differences were observed at high- and low-humidity, attributable to the evaporation resistance and interfacial properties introduced by the hydrophobic particle layers. Furthermore, although pure water droplets maintained a quasi-spherical shape, LMs demonstrated progressive flattening over time, suggesting mechanical constraints imposed by the particle shell. These findings provide new insights into the coupled evaporation and deformation behaviours of LMs in acoustic fields, informing the design of contamination-free microfluidic and microreactor systems.

</details>


### [238] [Direct numerical simulations of multi-layer Rayleigh-Taylor instability under asymmetric interfacial density stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07714)
*Abhijeet Guha,Prasoon Suchandra,Aditi Sengupta*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用三维全可压缩高保真数值模拟研究界面Atwood数对多层Rayleigh-Taylor不稳定性演化、湍流混合及能谱特征的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多界面热分层RTI中界面Atwood数如何影响不稳定性增长、界面耦合、湍流混合与谱特性尚缺乏系统认识。

Method: 使用全可压缩三维高保真Navier-Stokes求解器，对三层流体、两个由温度差形成的非稳定密度界面开展十组不同界面At的DNS，并统计最大涡量、混合层增长、密度脉动、湍动能及重力势能–TKE–耗散间的能量分配。

Result: 所有工况均经历起爆、准指数增长与非线性饱和阶段；界面At增大使起爆提前、涡量放大增强、湍流混合层发展加快。弱分层近似单界面RTI，强分层形成两个同时活跃的混合层并相互作用、合并为耦合湍流区；密度脉动与TKE的壁法向分布随分层增强而展宽；晚期谱在中波数区间呈约-11/5标度。

Conclusion: 界面Atwood数控制多界面RTI的起爆时序、混合层耦合模式与湍流输运强度，晚期-11/5谱标度反映浮力主导的湍流输运。

Abstract: Direct numerical simulations of thermally stratified multi-layer Rayleigh-Taylor instability are performed to investigate the influence of interfacial Atwood number (At) on instability growth, interfacial coupling, turbulent mixing, and spectral characteristics. A fully compressible, three-dimensional, high-fidelity Navier-Stokes solver has been used to simulate ten cases spanning a broad range of interfacial $At$ to examine the evolution of coupled instability dynamics. The configuration consists of three superposed fluid layers with unstable density stratification across two interfaces generated through temperature variations. The temporal evolution of the flow is characterized using the maximum vorticity, mixing-layer growth, density fluctuations, turbulent kinetic energy, and energy partitioning between gravitational potential energy, TKE, and dissipation. The instability evolution is shown to proceed through distinct onset, quasi-exponential growth, and nonlinear saturation stages for all configurations. Increasing interfacial At leads to earlier instability onset, enhanced vorticity amplification, and accelerated development of turbulent mixing layers. The weakly stratified configurations exhibit dynamics resembling effectively single-interface RTI, whereas the strongly stratified cases develop two simultaneously active mixing layers that subsequently interact and merge into a coupled turbulent region. The wall-normal distributions of density fluctuations and TKE demonstrate progressive broadening of the mixing region and increasing redistribution of turbulent activity with increasing stratification strength. The late-stage spectra exhibit an approximate -11/5 scaling over an intermediate range of wavenumbers, consistent with buoyancy-dominated turbulent transport.

</details>


### [239] [A Lagrangian analysis of coherent structures in 2D annular Rayleigh-Bénard convection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08024)
*Luis Álamo,Jezabel Curbelo,Kathrin Padberg-Gehle*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文从拉格朗日视角研究对流不稳定性，提出多种拉格朗日诊断量，揭示对流羽流、相干涡旋结构与径向热通量及动力学转变之间的联系，并给出粘性边界层厚度的判别准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对流不稳定性研究多基于欧拉描述，难以刻画全局、长时间存活的相干流动结构如何自组织。作者希望从拉格朗日角度获得新认识，并建立拉格朗日诊断量与经典欧拉量（如Nusselt数、边界层厚度）的对应关系。

Method: 研究对象为二维环形流体层，内部均匀加热，受径向重力和径向温度梯度作用。作者提出若干新的拉格朗日诊断量，在广泛的无量纲参数范围内进行拉格朗日分析：利用有限时间Lyapunov指数（FTLE）场局部极大值曲线刻画对流羽流；用流动非零曲率表征对流卷内的相干旋转运动；引入滑动窗口方法处理有限时间运动学量，并与径向热通量（Nusselt数）对比；还基于径向FTLE剖面提出粘性边界层（UBL）厚度的判别准则。

Result: 建立了对流羽流与FTLE局部极大值曲线之间的启发式对应关系；刻画了对流卷内与非零曲率相关的相干旋转运动；通过滑动窗口方法揭示了有限时间运动学量与Nusselt数所量化径向热通量之间的直接关系，并识别出在两者上均留下痕迹的动力学转变；提出的基于径向FTLE剖面的UBL厚度准则经经典欧拉方法验证。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日诊断量能够有效刻画对流不稳定性的相干结构与动力学转变，并与欧拉热输运量和边界层特征建立联系，表明拉格朗日视角可为对流自组织研究提供补充性且一致的分析框架。

Abstract: Convective instabilities are studied from a Lagrangian perspective in order to gain new insights into how the resulting global, long-lived coherent flow structures organize themselves. We consider a 2D annular fluid layer uniformly heated from the inside and subject to radial gravity and propose several new Lagrangian diagnostics for characterizing its convective instabilities. In particular, we perform a comprehensive Lagrangian analysis of the flow field across a wide range of dimensionless parameters, establishing a heuristic correspondence between convective plumes and curves of local maxima of Finite-Time Lyapunov Exponent (FTLE) fields. Moreover, we characterize coherent swirling motions inside convective rolls, which are associated to the non-zero curvature of the flow. By introducing a sliding window method for our finite-time kinematic measures, we demonstrate a direct relationship with radial heat flux quantified by means of the Nusselt number and identify relevant dynamical transitions that leave a trace on both. Finally, we propose a formal criterion for determining viscous boundary layer (UBL) thickness by virtue of radial FTLE profiles and validated against a classical Eulerian method.

</details>


### [240] [Motif: A Modular Finite-Volume Framework for Transient Incompressible Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08326)
*Utku Şentürk*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: Motif是一个基于交错网格有限体积法的二维不可压缩流动求解器，本文介绍其理论、实现与验证，并比较标准投影法与Perot（1993）近似二阶投影法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为教学与研究框架，需要一个实现细节充分、数值性质明确的二维不可压缩流动求解器，并为其向直接数值模拟（DNS）发展奠定基础，同时便于他人独立复现。

Method: 在交错笛卡尔网格上采用有限体积法离散控制方程；对流项用二阶Adams–Bashforth显式处理，扩散项用Crank–Nicolson隐式处理，压力–速度耦合采用投影法（标准投影与Perot近似二阶投影）；重点讨论离散算子、边界条件、压力时间处理、守恒性以及数值耗散与色散。

Result: 通过周期、壁面约束和进出口流动问题在均匀及光滑拉伸网格上的验证，考察了时空收敛性、离散质量守恒、动能行为、拟涡能和能谱；结果显示了空间离散的预期精度，并区分了两种投影方法的时间行为。

Conclusion: 本文既为独立实现Motif提供了足够详细的描述，也分析了与其持续发展至直接数值模拟相关的数值性质。

Abstract: This document presents the theory, implementation, and verification of Motif, a two-dimensional incompressible flow solver developed as a teaching and research framework. The governing equations are discretized using the finite-volume method on staggered Cartesian grids. Convection is treated explicitly using the second-order Adams--Bashforth method, diffusion implicitly using the Crank--Nicolson method, and pressure--velocity coupling using projection methods. Both the Standard projection and the approximate second-order projection of Perot (1993) are considered. Particular attention is given to the discrete operators, boundary conditions, temporal treatment of pressure, conservation properties, and numerical dissipation and dispersion. The implementation is verified using a sequence of periodic, wall-bounded, and inlet--outlet flow problems on uniform and smoothly stretched grids. Spatial and temporal convergence, discrete mass conservation, kinetic-energy behavior, enstrophy, and energy spectra are examined. The results demonstrate the expected accuracy of the spatial discretization and distinguish the temporal behavior of the two projection approaches. The document is intended both to describe Motif in sufficient detail for independent implementation and to examine the numerical properties relevant to its continued development toward direct numerical simulation.

</details>


### [241] [Physically Organized Latent Spaces in Unsupervised Autoencoders: Evidence from Aerodynamic Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08451)
*Renato Tognaccini,Ettore Saetta,Gianluca Iaccarino*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 自编码器仅通过重构训练，即可自发按物理变量与气动规律组织潜在表征；采用序贯训练，将无粘流场与边界层效应解耦，得到三维潜在空间，其中第三维与雷诺数和气动阻力强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自编码器在无监督条件下学习到的潜在表征是否能够自发反映物理意义与气动规律，并检验这种物理组织在不同随机初始化下的可复现性。

Method: 使用复杂度递增的气动数据库（从线性解析理论到非线性分离RANS流），训练仅以重构为目标的自编码器；并采用序贯训练的无监督自编码器获得三维潜在空间表征，将无粘流场与边界层效应解耦；通过交叉验证回归与仿射对齐度量评估编码结构。

Result: 潜在坐标本身随随机初始化变化，但其底层物理组织可复现，甚至在定量上可复现；第三潜在变量与雷诺数和气动阻力强相关；物理参数与响应在学习到的坐标中被强烈且线性地编码，且在不同独立训练间保持一致。

Conclusion: 仅基于重构训练的自编码器能够自发形成具有物理意义的气动表征，且该表征具有可复现性与线性可解码性，为无监督流场降维建模提供了依据。

Abstract: Using aerodynamic databases of progressively increasing complexity - from linear analytical theory to nonlinear separated RANS flows - we demonstrate that an autoencoder, trained solely by reconstruction, can spontaneously organize the latent representation according to physically meaningful variables and aerodynamic laws. Although the latent coordinates themselves vary under random initialization, the underlying physical organization is reproducible, even quantitatively. In addition, we present a three-dimensional latent space representation of the airfoil flow obtained by an unsupervised autoencoder with sequential training in which the inviscid field is decoupled from the boundary layer effects with the third latent variable strongly correlated with the Reynolds number and aerodynamic drag. Cross-validated regression and affine-alignment measures show that physical parameters and responses are strongly and linearly encoded in the learned coordinates, consistently across independent trainings.

</details>


### [242] [Simulation of Weakly Ionized Hypersonic Flows with Reactive Species Weighting Scheme in the Direct Simulation Monte Carlo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08476)
*Takato Morimoto,Virgile Charton,Eiichiro Yamaoka,Kiyoshi Kinefuchi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将保守物种加权方案扩展到反应流，采用分裂-合并概念处理不同权重的粒子，验证了质量与总能量守恒，并成功应用于OREX再入飞行条件的电子数密度与通信黑障预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱电离流中电子、离子等痕量物种数密度极低，传统DSMC难以有效分辨；需要将保守物种加权方案扩展到反应流，以在高超声速再入条件下预测通信黑障起始所需的电子数密度。

Method: 基于保守物种加权方案，引入物种相关权重的分裂-合并概念；不同权重物种间的反应碰撞按各自权重处理，通过产生整数个产物粒子并对反应物与产物施加概率方法以满足质量守恒；在单胞解离与交换反应算例中验证，并应用于OREX飞行条件（105.0–92.8 km）。

Result: 单胞解离与交换反应算例中质量、总能量守恒及摩尔分数历史与传统DSMC一致；OREX算例中平动与内能温度与传统DSMC吻合，获得连续空间分布的痕量电子数密度，与飞行观测的通信黑障起始一致；计算离子电流再现了飞行静电探针数据总体趋势，并量化了壁面离子复合与N+O缔合电离速率不确定性的影响，计算电流范围覆盖现有测量值。

Conclusion: 该扩展方案为弱电离反应流中痕量带电物种提供了守恒且有效的DSMC模拟能力，可用于高超声速再入通信黑障预测，并具备向测量范围之外条件外推的潜力。

Abstract: The conservative species weighting scheme enables the direct simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) method to resolve trace species such as electrons and ions in weakly ionized flows. The present study extends this scheme to reacting flows, utilizing a split-merge concept for particles with species-dependent weights. Reactive collisions between differently weighted species are processed according to their respective weights, introducing the production of an integer number of product particles and a probabilistic approach applied to both reactant and product particles to satisfy mass conservation. The extended scheme was validated in single-cell dissociation and exchange reaction test cases, verifying mass and total energy conservation and the mole-fraction histories against conventional DSMC. This extension provides the electron number density required for predicting the onset of communication blackout at high altitudes during hypersonic reentry. The method was applied to the Orbital Re-entry Experiment (OREX) flight conditions from 105.0 km to 92.8 km altitude. Translational and internal temperatures showed good agreement with conventional DSMC results, validating the macroscopic flow-field computation. The scheme enabled continuous spatial distributions of trace electron number density, consistent with the communication blackout onset observed in the flight data. The computed ion current reproduced the overall trend of the in-flight electrostatic probe data. The influences of the ion recombination at the vehicle surface and of the uncertainty of the N + O associative-ionization rate were quantified, and the range of the computed currents encompassed the available measurements. The 105.0 km case extended the prediction beyond available measurements, consistent with the overall trend.

</details>


### [243] [Thermocapillary stabilization of Liquid Space Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08654)
*Ryan Engle,Valeri Frumkin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文研究微重力下利用空间均匀、时间调制加热稳定薄液面镜，通过周期性加热诱发热毛细振荡流抑制持续加热引起的不稳定性，并结合多重尺度分析、线性稳定性分析与数值模拟，给出稳定性判据与衰减时间，实现米级变形在数天尺度内的稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力环境缺乏重力主导的静水恢复力，薄液面镜的自由表面易受扰动失稳；而持续加热会引发持续热毛细流并导致不稳定，因此需要一种可主动抑制不稳定、促使表面弛豫的方法。

Method: 基于长波模型进行多重尺度分析，推导多个加热周期内耦合的表面温度演化；随后开展线性稳定性分析，得到各表面模式的稳定性判据与衰减时间；最后用数值模拟验证理论预测。

Result: 多重尺度分析获得耦合表面温度演化，线性稳定性分析给出各模式稳定性判据与衰减时间；数值模拟验证了解析预测，并表明可将米级表面变形在数天时间尺度内稳定。

Conclusion: 空间均匀的时间调制加热可产生振荡热毛细流，抑制持续加热的不稳定性，使自由表面通过热辐射、热毛细输运与毛细作用协同弛豫；该策略为微重力下薄液面镜的稳定提供可行途径。

Abstract: We investigate how spatially uniform, time-modulated heating can be used to stabilize a thin liquid mirror in microgravity. Periodic heating generates oscillatory thermocapillary flows that suppress the instability associated with sustained heating, allowing the free surface to relax through the combined effects of thermal radiation, thermocapillary transport, and capillarity. Using multiple-scale analysis of a long-wave model, we derive the coupled surface-temperature evolution over many heating cycles. A subsequent linear stability analysis yields the stability criteria and decay times of individual surface modes. Numerical simulations validate the analytical predictions and demonstrate stabilization of meter-scale surface deformations over timescales of days.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Economic resources, sporting efficiency and uncertainty: a stochastic optimization model of football performance in Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06967)
*Thierno Thioune,Babacar Mbaye Ndiaye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个随机最优控制框架，将足球绩效视为不确定性下效率驱动的生产过程，推导HJB方程并给出最优投资策略与验证定理，并结合数值解、蒙特卡洛模拟及AFCON 2025 24支国家队的横截面分析进行实证检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有体育经济学研究多采用SFA/DEA等静态效率测度，难以刻画效率随时间的演化以及投资决策的动态反馈。作者希望在不确定性框架下引入随机微分方程与最优控制，弥补静态方法在动态机制解释上的不足。

Method: 建立含经济投入、遵循随机微分方程的潜在效率变量与体育发展投资控制变量的随机优化模型；推导贴现HJB方程，刻画最优投资政策，在显式增长条件下证明验证定理，并说明控制问题适定需要有界投资预算。理论上与SFA/DEA传统相比较，并通过网格加密收敛性验证的HJB数值解与蒙特卡洛模拟进行说明。实证上基于GDP、人口与FIFA排名数据，对AFCON 2025的24支国家队进行横截面分析，采用异方差稳健推断估计国别条件对数效率残差。

Result: 数值解与蒙特卡洛模拟验证了理论结果；控制问题适定需有界投资预算。实证显示残差与FIFA积分因构造原因机械且强相关，作者量化了这种依赖关系；塞内加尔、摩洛哥持续超出其GDP隐含潜力，而南非、坦桑尼亚等则表现低于潜力。这些模式与AFCON实际结果大体一致。

Conclusion: 动态控制表述相对静态SFA/DEA方法能补充效率演化与投资政策的机制刻画；但所估计残差也包含制度质量、海外侨民人才与人口结构等未纳入模型的不可观测因素，因此结果解释需谨慎。

Abstract: We propose a stochastic optimization framework in which football performance is modeled as the result of an efficiency-driven production process under uncertainty. The model incorporates economic inputs, a latent efficiency variable evolving according to a stochastic differential equation, and a control variable representing investment in sports development. We derive the associated discounted Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equation, characterize the optimal investment policy, prove a verification theorem under explicit growth conditions, and show that a bounded investment budget is required for the control problem to be well posed. We relate our efficiency measure to the stochastic frontier and data envelopment analysis (SFA/DEA) traditions used elsewhere in sports economics, and clarify what a dynamic control formulation adds relative to those static approaches. The theoretical results are illustrated with a numerical solution of the HJB equation -- validated by a grid-refinement convergence study -- and Monte Carlo simulations. A cross-sectional analysis of the 24 AFCON 2025 national teams, based on GDP, population, and FIFA ranking data, estimates country-specific conditional log-efficiency residuals using heteroskedasticity-robust inference.
  We show that these residuals are mechanically and strongly correlated with FIFA points by construction, quantify this dependence, and interpret the results accordingly. Teams such as Senegal and Morocco consistently outperform their GDP-implied potential, whereas others, including South Africa and Tanzania, tend to underperform.
  While these patterns are broadly consistent with observed AFCON outcomes, the estimated residual also captures unobserved factors, such as institutional quality, diaspora talent, and demographic structure, that are not included in the model.

</details>


### [245] [Local $W^{2,1}_{p}$ convergence of penalization for time-inconsistent stopping problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06997)
*Xiaodong Luo,Xiang Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将惩罚方法推广到一般类时间不一致停止问题，构造惩罚PDE与惩罚控制问题，证明了局部 $W^{2,1}_{p}$ 收敛，并在凹性假设下得到控制问题到停止问题的收敛，最后用两个算例数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优停止问题的惩罚方法已有成熟结果，但时间不一致停止问题因缺乏动态规划原理、解的概念更复杂，其惩罚逼近尚缺乏系统研究。本文旨在建立该情形下的惩罚理论与收敛保证。

Method: 引入对应时间不一致停止问题的惩罚PDE问题与惩罚控制问题；针对时间不一致性带来的差异，发展新的分析论证，证明惩罚参数趋于无穷时惩罚PDE解局部 $W^{2,1}_{p}$ 收敛到变分不等式的解；在附加凹性假设下证明惩罚时间不一致控制问题收敛到原停止问题。

Result: 建立了惩罚PDE解到变分不等式解的局部 $W^{2,1}_{p}$ 收敛；在凹性条件下得到惩罚控制问题到时间不一致停止问题的收敛；通过两个时间不一致停止算例进行数值验证。

Conclusion: 惩罚方法是研究时间不一致停止问题的有效工具，但时间不一致性导致与经典最优停止惩罚方法存在本质差异，需要新的论证；所得收敛结果为该问题的数值求解与理论分析提供了基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the penalization method for a general class of time-inconsistent stopping problems. Inspired by some existing results on penalization for optimal stopping problems, we introduce the penalized PDE problem and the penalized control problem for time-inconsistent stopping problems, uncovering some interesting discrepancy stemming from the time-inconsistency. As the penalization tends to infinity, we develop some novel arguments to rigorously establish the local $W^{2,1}_{p}$ convergence results for the solution of the penalized PDE problem to a solution of the variational inequality associated to the time-inconsistent stopping problem. Under an additional concavity assumption, we further derive the convergence of the penalized time-inconsistent control problem to the time-inconsistent stopping problem. We revisit two examples of time-inconsistent stopping to numerically illustrate the convergence theory.

</details>


### [246] [Optimization by Reparametrization: Time-Warped Mirror Flows with Singular Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07040)
*Cristian Vega,Cesare Molinari,Lorenzo Rosasco,Silvia Villa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究过参数化模型的优化问题，通过将复杂模型的拟合视为简单模型的冗余重参数化，分析线性过参数化的梯度流，并证明其等价于原始参数上的镜像流，从而给出适定性与收敛性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动问题通常需要定义复杂模型并拟合数据，神经网络是典型例子。拟合过程可视为优化问题，但往往非凸，导致优化保证难以获得。作者希望利用“过参数化”视角，将复杂模型看作某更简单模型的冗余重参数化，从而借助简单模型的优化理论获得保证。

Method: 作者首先形式化“将模型视为简单模型的冗余重参数化”这一思想；随后考察若干类线性过参数化的梯度流，证明它们对应于原始参数上的合适镜像流（mirror flows）；重点研究这些镜像流，并建立其适定性与收敛性；最后给出多个具体实例，包括全连接线性网络和带权重归一化的网络。

Result: 建立了线性过参数化梯度流与原始参数镜像流之间的对应关系；对所研究的镜像流证明了适定性与收敛性；在全连接线性网络和带权重归一化网络等具体情形下给出了实例验证。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了过参数化在优化中的作用，并刻画了相应的隐式正则化性质，为理解复杂模型优化提供了一种通过冗余重参数化获得理论保证的途径。

Abstract: Solving data-driven problems requires defining complex models and fitting them to data, neural networks being a motivating example. The fitting procedure can be seen as an optimization problem, which is often non-convex, and hence optimization guarantees are hard to derive. An opportunity is provided by viewing the model of interest as a redundant reparameterization--an overparameterization--of some simpler model for which optimization results are easier to achieve. In this paper, after formalizing the above idea, we revisit some recent results and derive new ones. In particular, we consider the gradient flow of some classes of linear overparameterizations and show that they correspond to suitable mirror flows on the original parameters. Our main contribution relates to the study of the latter, for which we establish well-posedness and convergence. Several specific instances are provided, including fully connected linear networks and networks with weight normalization. The results yield insight into the role of overparameterization in optimization and corresponding implicit regularization properties.

</details>


### [247] [The Cost of Differential Privacy in Linear-Quadratic Dynamic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07238)
*Chih-Yuan Chiu,Matthew Hale*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有异构目标的多智能体随机线性二次(LQ)动态博弈中隐私与性能的权衡，推导隐私代价的解析表达式，并证明目标一致时隐私噪声必然带来正代价、目标错位时可出现负代价(即噪声同时保护隐私并提升均衡性能)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协调中，策略性智能体需共享状态或目标的敏感信息，由此产生性能与隐私之间的张力。现有工作缺乏在动态博弈(尤其异构目标)框架下对该权衡的定量刻画。

Method: 构建随机线性二次(LQ)动态博弈模型：智能体向云计算端共享经噪声扰动的状态与参考信息，云计算反馈纳什均衡策略，注入噪声经过校准以满足差分隐私。推导各智能体无限时域稳态隐私代价(相对于非隐私博弈)的解析表达式，并按目标对齐程度分情形进行理论分析。

Result: 得到无限时域稳态隐私代价的解析表达；证明当智能体目标充分对齐时，注入隐私噪声必然产生正的性能代价；刻画出一类目标充分错位的博弈，其隐私代价可严格为负。数值实验验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 隐私噪声并非总是损害性能：当交互智能体目标充分错位时，噪声可同时保护隐私并提升某智能体的均衡性能，揭示了隐私与性能之间反直觉的非单调关系。

Abstract: Multi-agent coordination often requires strategic agents to share sensitive information about their states or objectives, creating a tension between performance and privacy. Our paper studies this tradeoff in stochastic linear-quadratic (LQ) dynamic games with heterogeneous agent objectives. In our framework, agents share noise-perturbed state and reference information with a cloud computer that computes feedback Nash equilibrium strategies, with the injected noise calibrated to provide differential privacy. We derive an analytical expression for each agent's infinite-horizon steady-state cost of privacy relative to the non-private game. Then, we prove that when agents' objectives are sufficiently aligned, the injection of privacy noise necessarily incurs a positive performance cost. In contrast, we characterize a class of games with sufficiently misaligned objectives across agents for which an agent's cost of privacy can be strictly negative. Thus, counterintuitively, noise can simultaneously protect privacy and improve the equilibrium performance of an agent when the objectives of interacting agents are sufficiently misaligned. Finally, we present numerical experiments which corroborate our theoretical contributions.

</details>


### [248] [A Single-Loop, Constant-Batch First-Order Penalty Method for Stochastic Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07290)
*Xingyu Chen,Ming Yang,Quanqi Hu,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出随机单循环常批一阶罚方法SICO，仅用O(1)批次即可达到O(ε^{-6})与O(ε^{-4})样本复杂度，首次在单循环常批设定下匹配全一阶SBO最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于罚方法的随机双层优化虽摆脱了二阶导数预言机，但在非凸-强凸设定下，一阶方法通常依赖嵌套循环或大批次，才能在有界方差或均方光滑假设下达到O(ε^{-6})或O(ε^{-4})样本复杂度。由于精确逼近需要较大的罚参数，如何用单循环罚方法与常批达到这些速率仍是挑战，也是文献中的开放问题。

Method: 提出SICO（Stochastic SIngle-loop COnstant-Batch）一阶罚方法，融合两个互补机制：一是每轮对原始下层问题与罚问题各执行一次随机梯度更新，并通过投影控制二者迭代点之间的间隔；二是对上层梯度估计器施加指数移动平均（EMA）以稳定估计。

Result: 在无偏、有界方差随机梯度假设下，SICO每轮仅用O(1)个随机梯度样本即可达到O(ε^{-6})样本复杂度；在额外假定下层随机梯度满足均方光滑时，同样以O(1)批次将复杂度改进至O(ε^{-4})。

Conclusion: SICO是首个在单循环、常批设定下匹配全一阶SBO方法已知最优收敛率的工作，解决了文献中提出的开放问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in penalty-based methods for stochastic bilevel optimization (SBO) have eliminated the need for second-order derivative oracles. However, for stochastic nonconvex-strongly convex bilevel problems, existing first-order methods typically rely on nested loops and/or large batch sizes for attaining $O(ε^{-6})$ or $O(ε^{-4})$ sample complexity under standard bounded-variance assumption or mean-square smoothness assumption. Achieving these rates with a single-loop penalty method and a constant batch size remains challenging due to a large penalty value needed for an accurate approximation. To address this challenge, we develop a stochastic SIngle-loop COnstant-Batch first-order penalty method (SICO) that combines two complementary ingredients. First, it performs one stochastic-gradient update per-iteration for both the original lower-level and penalized problems, with a projection that controls the separation between their iterates. Second, it applies an exponential moving average to stabilize the upper-level gradient estimator. We show that this combination achieves $ O(ε^{-6}) $ sample complexity using only $O(1)$ stochastic-gradient samples per iteration under unbiased, bounded-variance stochastic gradients. Under the additional mean-square smoothness assumption on the lower-level stochastic gradients, the same algorithm improves the complexity to $O(ε^{-4})$ also with $O(1)$ batch size. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to match the best-known convergence rate for fully first-order SBO methods using a single loop and a constant batch size. This result addresses an open problem posed in the literature.

</details>


### [249] [Model-Based Galerkin Lifting with Exact LTI Decomposition for Guaranteed $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ Output Feedback Control of Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07299)
*Burak Kurkcu,Christopher Phan,Aleksandar Zecevic,Maryam Khanbaghi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的Galerkin框架，用于已知动力学非线性系统的输出反馈控制，通过执行器增广与闭包残差显式建模，设计仅依赖测量跟踪误差的线性动态控制器，并给出闭环稳定性与跟踪性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性系统输出反馈控制中提升模型依赖不变可观测子空间、难以处理非控制仿射系统、需在线提升等问题，寻求一种保持执行器动力学与常值输入矩阵、无需不变子空间的有限维精确分解与控制器设计方法。

Method: 采用带执行器增广的模型化Galerkin框架，将提升非线性动力学精确分解为LTI部分与有限阶非闭合加性残差；利用闭包残差、执行器实现缺陷和输出重构误差的区域界，在标准H∞设计中综合控制器；控制器为仅由测量跟踪误差驱动的线性动态系统，无需在线提升，并保留状态坐标以直接约束原状态。

Result: 推导出先验包含条件；在该条件下非线性闭环前向完备，状态与跟踪误差满足显式瞬态界并一致最终有界。小车倒立摆算例验证了闭环保证，并与全状态反步法对比：仅用测量跟踪误差的输出反馈控制器达到几乎相同的标称跟踪RMSE，且峰值跟踪误差更低。

Conclusion: 该框架为已知动力学非线性系统提供无需在线提升、无需不变可观测子空间的输出反馈控制途径，兼顾理论稳定性保证与实用跟踪性能，并可扩展至非控制仿射系统。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a model-based Galerkin framework for output-feedback control of nonlinear systems with known dynamics. With a suitable actuator augmentation, the finite-dimensional realization preserves the actuator dynamics and the constant input matrix. We retain finite-order nonclosure as an explicit additive residual. The LTI part and this residual describe the lifted nonlinear dynamics exactly. The decomposition does not require an invariant subspace of observables and also applies to non-control-affine systems. We use regional bounds on the closure residual, actuator-realization defect, and output-reconstruction error in the standard $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ design. The proposed controller is a linear dynamic system driven only by the measured tracking error and requires no online lifting. Retaining the state coordinates gives direct bounds on the original state. We use these bounds to derive an a priori containment condition. Under this condition, the nonlinear closed loop is forward complete, and the state and tracking error satisfy explicit transient bounds and are uniformly ultimately bounded. The cart-pendulum example illustrates the nonlinear closed-loop guarantees and compares the controller with full-state backstepping. Although the output-feedback controller uses only the measured tracking error, it achieves almost the same nominal tracking RMSE as backstepping, with a lower peak tracking error.

</details>


### [250] [A continuous-time limit for particle swarm optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07373)
*Pascal Bianchi,Radu Dragomir,Yerkin Yesbay*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究无动量、带个体最优记忆和全局最优交互的PSO连续时间模型，保留原始的不可微选择过程，给出小步长极限为随机微分包含，并刻画何时可恢复真实选择子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有PSO连续时间模型多依赖平滑近似来回避个体最优和全局最优选择过程的不可微性，可能改变原始算法的关键选择机制。作者希望在不平滑选择规则的前提下，严格刻画PSO的小步长连续时间极限。

Method: 构建无动量PSO的连续时间模型，保留不连续的个体最优更新与全局最优选择过程；取小步长极限，分析弱极限点，利用随机微分包含描述极限动力学；结合布朗噪声、正则性与非退化假设，研究快切换全局最优选择子引发的凸化交互，并给出恢复原始选择子的条件。

Result: 弱极限点满足随机微分包含：个体最优变量服从松弛记录条件，全局最优选择子的快切换导致凸化交互；在适当假设下可恢复真实原始选择子；布朗噪声使个体最优过程等同于极限轨迹的最新记录位置，并证明几乎每个时刻取得全局最优的粒子唯一。

Conclusion: 所得连续时间动力学同时保留个体最优和全局最优两种选择规则，无需对其进行平滑近似，为PSO的渐近理论提供了更贴近原算法的随机微分包含框架。

Abstract: Particle swarm optimization (PSO) is a popular class of gradient-free methods for global optimization. We study a continuous-time model for momentum-free PSO with personal-best memory and global-best interaction. Unlike previous models which rely on smooth approximations, we keep the original discontinuous personal- and global-best processes. We study the small-step limit and show that weak limit points satisfy a stochastic differential inclusion: personal-best variables obey a relaxed record condition, while fast switching of the global-best selector leads to a convexified interaction. We then give conditions under which the true original selectors are recovered. Under suitable regularity and nondegeneracy assumptions, Brownian noise allows us to identify the personal-best processes with the latest record positions of the limiting trajectories and to prove that the particle attaining the global best is unique at almost every time. The resulting continuous-time dynamics retain both selection rules without smoothing them.

</details>


### [251] [Linear Occupancy Homomorphisms for Discounted Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07441)
*Yihan Liu,Sarah H. Q. Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出线性占用测度同态（LOH），在可行占用测度空间上定义MDP同态，用线性变换矩阵刻画结构性质，并给出有效性判定、与软同态及因子化鲁棒MDP的联系，以及在鲁棒MDP改进和大规模MDP分解中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MDP同态通过状态与状态-动作空间上的映射定义，难以直接利用占用测度空间的线性结构，也限制了其在鲁棒MDP与大规模MDP分解中的应用。作者希望建立一种在占用测度空间上定义、可用线性变换矩阵表示的新同态框架，以刻画并利用MDP的结构性质。

Method: 提出线性占用同态（LOH）：一类作用于MDP可行占用测度空间的变换，可表示为线性变换矩阵。作者推导有限维线性不等式约束以判定候选变换矩阵是否为合法LOH，并给出相应的变换后MDP构造；进一步建立LOH与软MDP同态、因子化矩阵鲁棒MDP之间的联系；在变换矩阵可逆时，证明原MDP等价于变换后的约束MDP，并给出LOH保持最优值的充要条件。
