Result: 给出了合法LOH的有限维线性不等式认证条件及其对应变换MDP的构造；建立了LOH与软MDP同态、因子化矩阵鲁棒MDP的关联；证明了可逆情形下原MDP与变换后约束MDP的等价性，并导出保持最优值的充要条件。在两个场景中验证了LOH的效用：将非矩形参数不确定集转化为同态矩形MDP以改进鲁棒MDP解，以及将大规模MDP分解为较小的独立MDP。

Conclusion: LOH提供了一种在占用测度空间上定义、以线性矩阵表示的MDP同态框架，兼具理论刻画能力与实际应用价值，可用于鲁棒MDP求解改进与大规模特MDP分解。

Abstract: We introduce linear occupancy homomorphisms (LOHs), a class of Markov decision process (MDP) transformations that defines the MDP homomorphism over the feasible occupancy measure space of MDPs. In contrast to classic MDP homomorphism frameworks, which are defined through mappings over the state and state-action spaces, LOHs map between occupancy measure spaces and are representable as linear transformation matrices that expose important structural properties of the homomorphism itself. For this class of MDP transformations, we derive finite dimensional linear inequality constraints that certify when a candidate transformation matrix is a valid LOH and derive the corresponding transformed MDP. We further establish a connection between LOHs, soft MDP homomorphisms, and factored matrix robust MDPs. When the linear transformation matrix is invertible, we prove that the original MDP is equivalent to a constrained MDP after the homomorphic transformation, and derive necessary and sufficient conditions under which the LOH preserves the optimal value. We illustrate the benefits of LOHs in two settings: improving robust MDP solutions by transforming nonrectangular parameter uncertainty sets into homomorphic rectangular MDPs, and decomposing a large MDP into smaller independent MDPs.

</details>


### [252] [PLUTO: An Agentic AI Tool for Interactive Spacecraft Rendezvous Trajectory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07573)
*Eleanor Brosius,Yuji Takubo,Daniele Gammelli,Simone D'Amico,Marco Pavone*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: PLUTO：将LLM智能体编码工具融入序列凸规划（SCP）架构的交互式轨迹设计框架，实现自然语言到可执行最优控制问题的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸轨迹优化技术虽已成熟，但广泛应用受限于需要大量领域专业知识来针对不同任务场景定制问题建模。LLM推理与智能体编码能力为解决这一可访问性瓶颈提供了新路径。

Method: PLUTO将高层语义输入映射为明确定义的数学约束，并保持适用于SCP的结构化表示；通过自动凸化流水线处理约束并直接嵌入可执行的最优控制建模，系统化连接用户意图与轨迹生成；辅以可视化约束验证与场景参数化。

Result: 在涵盖10类约束的50条自然语言交会任务提示评估中，PLUTO为每条提示均生成了轨迹，其中94%的轨迹同时满足任务意图的定量与定性要求。

Conclusion: 该工作探索了智能体AI在弥合先进优化算法与面向工程师的轨迹设计工作流之间差距的潜力。

Abstract: Modern space missions require trajectory design methods capable of efficiently adapting to diverse mission objectives and operational constraints. Despite the maturity of non-convex trajectory optimization techniques, their broader adoption remains limited by the substantial domain expertise required to tailor problem formulations across varying mission scenarios. Recent advances in large language model (LLM) reasoning and agentic AI coding capabilities offer a promising pathway to improving the accessibility of advanced trajectory optimization techniques. This paper introduces PLUTO (Plain Language Understanding for Trajectory Optimization), an interactive trajectory design framework that integrates agentic AI coding tools within a sequential convex programming (SCP) architecture. The proposed system maps high-level semantic inputs to well-defined mathematical constraints while preserving a structured representation suitable for SCP. These constraints are processed through an auto-convexification pipeline and incorporated directly into executable optimal control formulations, thereby systematically connecting user intent and trajectory generation. Visual constraint verification and scenario parameterization further support an intuitive environment for trajectory design across a wide range of application scenarios. Within an evaluation of 50 natural-language rendezvous mission prompts spanning 10 constraint types, PLUTO generated a trajectory for every prompt, and 94% of the resulting trajectories satisfied both the quantitative and qualitative requirements of the mission intent. Ultimately, this work explores the potential of agentic AI to bridge the gap between advanced optimization algorithms and engineer-focused trajectory design workflows.

</details>


### [253] [Sharp conditioning and quantitative stability of optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07577)
*Yuanlong Ruan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究二次运输问题中可行方案与真实最优映射的接近程度，在温和的密度与矩条件下，针对可能无界或非紧支撑的源和目标，给出了尖锐的模数（modulus）估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当已知某可行方案的二次运输成本接近最优成本时，希望刻画该可行方案在多大程度上接近于真正的最优映射（Brenier 映射）。已有结果多依赖势函数估计，且需要源支撑的凸性或密度有界等较强假设，本文旨在在更弱、更一般的条件下建立定量稳定性。

Method: 在源密度属于 $L^p$ 且具 $n$ 阶矩（令 $s=1-1/p$，$\eta=sn/[n+(d-1)s]$）的条件下，对目标施加约束 $\int |y|^m[\log(e+|y|)]^\beta\,d\nu\leqslant 1$，通过构造并分析最坏情形类一致条件（class-uniform conditioning）的尖锐模数，直接对映射误差进行估计，而不经过势函数估计。

Result: 对任意小的 $t>0$、$m\geqslant 2$、$\beta\geqslant 0$，最坏情形条件模数与 $t^{\eta(m-2)/[m(1+\eta)-\eta]}[\log(e/t)]^{-\beta(2+\eta)/[m(1+\eta)-\eta]}$ 相当（尖锐）。当 $m=2$ 时，每个 $\beta>0$ 给出尖锐的对数模数，而 $m=2,\beta=0$ 的极端情形不存在消失模数。对平方映射误差与重心投影误差均验证了匹配的下界。

Conclusion: 所得尖锐映射误差界可直接导出常见源 Brenier 映射的定量稳定性，避免势函数估计带来的信息损失；在相同或更弱条件下（尤其不假设源支撑凸性或源密度有界）严格改进了 Delalande-Mérigot 与 Letrouit-Mérigot 的相应结果。

Abstract: When a feasible plan is obtained whose quadratic transport cost is known to be close to the optimal cost, we try to determine how close the feasible plan is to the true optimal map under mild density and moment conditions. The source and target may be unbounded or have non-compact supports. We show that for a source with density in $L^p$ and an $n$-th moment, let $s=1-1/p$ and $η=sn/[n+(d-1)s]$. Under the target constraint $\int |y|^m[\log(e+|y|)]^β\,dν\leqslant1$, the worst-case class-uniform conditioning has a sharp modulus comparable to \[
  t^{η(m-2)/[m(1+η)-η]}
  [\log(e/t)]^{-β(2+η)/[m(1+η)-η]}, \] whenever $t>0$ is small. This holds for $m\geqslant2$ and $β\geqslant0$. When $m=2$, every $β>0$ gives a sharp logarithmic modulus, whereas the $m=2,\,β=0$ extreme has no vanishing modulus. The matching lower bound is verified for both the squared map error and barycentric projection error. The sharp map error bounds allow us to directly derive quantitative stabilities of common source Brenier maps without passing through potential estimates, thereby avoiding loss of information. These stabilities strictly improve the corresponding results of Delalande-Merigot \cite{delalande2023quantitative} and Letrouit-Mérigot \cite{letrouit2026gluing} under identical or weaker settings, particularly no convexity of the source support or bounds of the source density are assumed.

</details>


### [254] [PLADOS: A Parameter-free Landing Algorithm for Decentralized Optimization on the Stiefel Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07750)
*Shu Li,Jiang Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 PLADOS——一种针对 Stiefel 流形去中心化优化的无参数 landing 算法，通过免 retraction 通信与受限割线不等式实现局部收缩，取得线性加速与理论收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stiefel 流形上的去中心化优化在机器学习与信号处理中应用广泛，但非凸流形约束与一致性约束的耦合使得算法常需引入额外调参（如罚参数、步长），限制了实用性。作者旨在设计一种无参数、通信高效且具备理论保证的方法。

Method: 提出 PLADOS：每次迭代将局部随机梯度更新与一次免 retraction 的通信步骤结合，运行于通信图之上。针对仅含罚项的方案，刻画了非凸 Stiefel 流形与一致性约束交集的一种类凸性质，用受限割线不等式（restricted secant inequality）保证流形一致性误差的局部收缩，且不引入需调优的参数。

Result: 对于 n 个节点、各使用规模为 b 的独立本地批次，证明 PLADOS 在 K 次迭代后达到渐近收敛率 O(σ/√(nbK) + (nξ²)^{1/3}/K^{2/3})，并实现关于节点数的线性加速，其中 σ²>0 为采样方差、ξ² 度量梯度异质性。实验表明算法在多种步长与网络下稳定、时间高效，且随节点数呈线性加速。

Conclusion: PLADOS 为 Stiefel 流形去中心化优化提供了免调参、免 retraction 的可行方案，兼具局部收缩理论保证与线性加速的收敛率，实验验证了其稳定性与效率。

Abstract: Decentralized optimization on the Stiefel manifold has broad applications in machine learning and signal processing. We propose PLADOS, a parameter-free landing algorithm for decentralized optimization on the Stiefel manifold. Each iteration combines local stochastic gradient updates with a single retraction-free communication step over the communication graph. A key convex-like property of the intersection of the nonconvex Stiefel manifold and consensus constraints is characterized through a restricted secant inequality for the penalty-only scheme, ensuring local contraction of the manifold consensus error without introducing additional parameters that require tuning. For $n$ nodes using independent local batches of size $b$, we prove that PLADOS achieves linear speedup with respect to the number of nodes with an asymptotic convergence rate of $O(σ/\sqrt{nbK}+(nξ^2)^{1/3}/K^{2/3})$ after $K$ iterations, where $σ^2>0$ is the sampling variance and $ξ^2$ quantifies gradient heterogeneity. Experiments demonstrate stability across tested stepsizes and networks, time efficiency, and linear speedup with respect to the number of nodes.

</details>


### [255] [Stochastic Subgradient Descent at Sharply Repulsive Points: A Bounded Noise Counterexample and Gaussian Noise Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07777)
*Shu Li,Jiang Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文回答Bianchi等人提出的公开问题，证明在非弱凸可定义设定下，具条件四阶矩控制的全向噪声不足以普遍几乎必然避开尖锐排斥临界点；但若目标为局部Lipschitz可定义且噪声为高斯，则可实现对任意有限尖锐排斥点集的几乎必然同时避开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由Bianchi、Hachem与Schechtman（2024, Remark 4）的公开问题驱动，探究何种噪声条件与目标函数正则性可保证随机次梯度下降普遍避开尖锐排斥临界点。

Method: 构造反例：全局Lipschitz、强制、可定义但非弱凸的目标函数，配以R²球面上独立均匀分布噪声，用双循环论证建立重标迭代的一致界。正面结果：对局部Lipschitz可定义目标与高斯噪声，构造差商函数，证明若以正概率收敛到尖锐排斥点将产生期望下降为0的平稳分布，与Fréchet次梯度范数的全局正下界矛盾。

Result: 反例中随机次梯度下降从指定球内任意初始点以概率1收敛到非极小的尖锐排斥临界点。正面结果中高斯噪声实现任意有限尖锐排斥点集的几乎必然同时避开，高斯密度的处处正性起关键作用。

Conclusion: 条件四阶矩控制的全向噪声在非弱凸可定义设定下不充分；局部Lipschitz可定义性配合高斯噪声足够保证几乎必然避开。

Abstract: Motivated by the open question of Bianchi, Hachem, and Schechtman (Bianchi et al., 2024, Remark 4), we show that in the non-weakly convex definable setting, omnidirectional noise with conditional fourth-moment control does not suffice for universal almost-sure avoidance of sharply repulsive critical points. We demonstrate this failure by constructing a globally Lipschitz, coercive, definable objective that is not weakly convex. For this objective and its sufficiently small linear perturbations, stochastic subgradient descent with independent noise uniformly distributed on a ball in $\mathbb{R}^2$ converges with probability $1$ to a nonminimal sharply repulsive critical point from every initial point in a specified ball. A two-cycle argument establishes uniform bounds on the rescaled iterates, yielding convergence to the sharply repulsive critical point. In contrast, for locally Lipschitz definable objectives, Gaussian noise ensures simultaneous almost-sure avoidance of any finite collection of sharply repulsive points. Through a construction of difference quotient functions, we show that convergence to any such point with positive probability would produce a stationary distribution with expected descent $0$, whereas a global positive lower bound on the Fréchet subgradient norms of the limiting functions forces strictly positive expected descent under the same distribution. This contradiction proves avoidance, with the everywhere positive Gaussian density playing a key role.

</details>


### [256] [GPU-accelerated wind farm layout search with a distilled endogenous wake model (EndoWake)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07844)
*Martina Fischetti,Matteo Fischetti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出EndoWake内生尾流模型，实现GPU高速风电场布局优化，并揭示尾流模型保真度与优化布局质量的非等价性，以及两种优化伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电场布局优化需最大化年发电量，而尾流效应决定发电量。精确尾流模型（如RANS）速度过慢，无法用于搜索；同时评估尾流模型保真度与所优化布局质量之间的关系尚不明确。

Method: 基于EndoWake——一种内生尾流模型，将每个网格单元风速设为变量，通过线性约束与上风向邻域关联，使尾流场、选址决策与项目约束共享同一混合整数模型。其四个参数针对RANS单尾流场一次性标定，GPU每秒每风向可评估超百万布局。进一步设计"筛选-确认"搜索：GPU上由EndoWake筛选候选移动，PyWake确认每个接受移动。

Result: 在Lillgrund对比EndoWake引导搜索与PyWake流程（以无人调用的RANS为评判），发现模型保真度不等于优化布局质量：搜索被采样风向间隙（"sirens"）吸引，并存在每个尾流边缘外侧的乐观带（"mermaid cells"，此前未见报道）。在随机风向下，两种流程统计上不可区分。在二十个未见起点上，1块GPU+30个CPU进程的筛选-确认搜索比纯PyWake搜索快7.9倍（匹配其PyWake值），年发电量指标快14.9倍。

Conclusion: 尾流模型的保真度并非优化布局质量的可靠指标；EndoWake的GPU高速筛选结合PyWake确认可显著加速布局优化，同时保持解的质量，为风电场布局优化提供高效且可靠的方案。

Abstract: Wind farm layout optimization decides where to place turbines to maximize annual energy production, and wake effects decide how much of that energy is produced. Accurate wake models are too slow for a search. We build on EndoWake, an endogenous wake model in which the wind speed at every grid cell is a variable linked to its upwind neighbors by linear constraints, so that wake field, siting decisions and project constraints share one mixed-integer model. Its four parameters are calibrated once against the single-wake field of a Reynolds-averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) simulation, and a GPU scores over a million layouts per second per wind direction. Comparing EndoWake-guided searches with a PyWake-based pipeline at Lillgrund, under a RANS judge that no search calls, revealed that the fidelity of a wake model is not the quality of the layouts optimized under it. A search is drawn to the gaps between sampled wind directions, the sirens; a diagnostic of the field finds a second artifact, an optimistic band off each wake edge, the mermaid cells, not reported before to our knowledge. Judged at random directions, the two pipelines are statistically indistinguishable. Our main result puts this speed to use: EndoWake screens the candidate moves of a local search on the GPU, and PyWake confirms every accepted move. On twenty unseen starts, with one GPU and thirty CPU processes, this screen-and-confirm search matches the PyWake value of a search on PyWake alone 7.9 times faster, and 14.9 times faster on the annual energy.

</details>


### [257] [Time-Efficient Active Bearing-Only Localization with Reception and Coverage Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07893)
*Ao Xiao,Fangfang Zhou,Qiteng Guo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究在带误差方位测量与未知但有界接收半径条件下，用移动传感器定位并清除静止全向无线电源，目标是在保证接收与清除的前提下最小化固定续行策略下的期望剩余行动时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统搜索与定位方法通常忽略方位误差、未知接收半径及完成时间等实际因素，难以在保证成功接收与清除的同时优化整体任务时长，因此需要一个兼顾可保证性与效率的决策方法。

Method: 方法采用三圆盘滤波器保证接收，用最小包围圆认证所有与方位一致的源位置被覆盖；通过蒙特卡洛样本平均搜索，在有限候选集中按完整任务时间（含移动、读数、终端服务及有限回退扫描）排序选点。

Result: 参考情形下5000次独立验证任务平均耗时60.40 s（约95%置信区间[59.96, 60.84] s），相较规定横向点节省19.39%，相较短行程点节省0.79%；五种初始几何共25,000次验证全部成功，平均完成时间均低于两种规定设计；同网格目标消融表明仅最小化行程或读数次数可能增加平均完成时间。

Conclusion: 所提方法能在给定传感模型与假设下保证接收和清除，并在期望完成时间上优于规定几何与单一成本目标，说明按完整任务时间优化优于仅最小化行程或读数次数。

Abstract: We consider the localization and removal of a stationary omnidirectional radio source using a mobile sensor with bounded bearing errors and an unknown reception radius within known bounds. The objective is to minimize expected remaining action time under a fixed continuation policy while guaranteeing reception and source removal under the sensing model. A three-disk filter ensures reception, and a minimum enclosing circle certifies coverage of all bearing-consistent source positions. Monte Carlo sample-average search ranks a finite candidate set by complete mission time, including travel, readings, terminal service, and a finite fallback scan that ensures completion under the stated assumptions. In the reference case, 5000 independent validation missions average 60.40 s (approximate 95\% confidence interval: [59.96, 60.84] s), saving 19.39\% relative to a prescribed lateral point and 0.79\% relative to a short-travel point. All 25,000 selected-point validation missions across five initial geometries succeed, with lower mean completion times than both prescribed designs. A same-grid objective ablation shows that minimizing travel or reading count alone can increase mean completion time.

</details>


### [258] [Higher-dimensional Golden Section, Generalized Fibonacci Sequence, and Multiple Online Leasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07901)
*Hu Maolin,Luo Chu,Xu Weidong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了经典黄金分割与在线租赁问题的联系，将其推广至高维黄金分割与多设备在线租赁，并给出了最优竞争策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黄金分割与在线租赁问题之间存在内在联系，但该联系尚未被系统刻画；同时，多设备在线租赁的最优策略在高维情形下是否存在显式解、如何逼近，仍需深入研究。

Method: 发现黄金分割点(√5−1)s/2与两对滑雪板在线租赁最优算法的对应关系；定义几何型与调和型高维黄金分割τ^(m)、T^(m)并建立比例λ与基ω的方程；将k-广义Fibonacci序列扩展为双无限m-广义Fibonacci序列，引入生成序列与Fibonacci矩阵并研究其子矩阵；针对m对滑雪板设计控制与平衡策略，用竞争分析证明最优性，并提出连续松弛问题与尾数加权法求解。

Result: 证明两对滑雪板情形下最优竞争比为(5+√5)/4；建立高维黄金分割的代数刻画及与广义Fibonacci序列的关系，导出含Fibonacci矩阵的行列式公式；证明多设备在线租赁的最优策略恰为m维调和型黄金分割法，并通过误差分析表明尾数加权法具有高精度。

Conclusion: 研究统一了黄金分割与在线租赁理论，将经典结果推广到高维与多设备情形，为高维无显式解问题提供了松弛近似与数值求解途径。

Abstract: We discover a connection between the classical golden section and the online leasing problem: when the number of skis increases from one to two, the optimal competitive algorithm is to rent two pairs until the golden section point$(\sqrt{5}-1)s/2$ within [0,s], then buy one pair, and finally buy the second at time $s$, achieving the optimal competitive ratio of $(5+\sqrt{5})/4$. Building on this, we explore higher-dimensional golden sections and multiple online leasing.
  For the golden section, we define geometric-type and harmonic-type higher-dimensional golden sections, $τ^{(m)}$ and $T^{(m)}$, based on two equivalent propositions dividing the unit segment into $m$ segments. We establish equations for the geometric-type ratio $λ$ and harmonic-type basis $ω$, and investigate their algebraic characteristics. Extending the $k$-generalized Fibonacci sequence to a bi-infinite $m$-generalized Fibonacci sequence, we establish their relationship. We introduce novel concepts including generating sequences and the Fibonacci matrix, investigating its submatrices. Results such as a determinant formula involving the Fibonacci matrix demonstrate its effectiveness in studying generalized Fibonacci sequences.
  For online leasing, we consider multiple online leasing of $m$ pairs of skis. We design a control and balancing strategy and prove it is optimal via competitive analysis. We show the optimal strategy is precisely the $m$-dimensional harmonic-type golden section method. Since explicit solutions may not exist for dimensions higher than four, we propose a continuous relaxation problem. We explore using its relaxed solution to approximate the optimal solution or the largest real root. We propose the mantissa weighting method to solve the equation, achieving high accuracy through error analysis.

</details>


### [259] [Adaptive Average Current-Mode Control of a Buck Converter Supplying Plasma Arc Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07930)
*Lukas Ecker,Thomas Voglhuber-Brunnmaier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性等离子弧负载下的降压变换器，提出一种自适应平均电流模式控制器，通过在线电感及等效串联电阻辨识与前馈弧压补偿实现快速电流跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子过程（气体流动波动、弧运动、附着条件变化、电极相互作用）会引起弧电压突变甚至熄弧，电源需在负载快速变化下仍能调节并限制弧电流，要求快速电流控制及用于监督稳定的执行层，而传统控制依赖在线非线性弧模型，难以实现。

Method: 基于变换器电感动态的平均模型，将电流误差反馈与实测弧电压的前馈补偿相结合；对电感和等效串联电阻进行在线自适应，以补偿变换器参数不确定性，无需在线非线性弧模型；采用Lyapunov分析证明有界参考假设下平均闭环系统的估计误差有界及电流渐近跟踪。

Result: 在氩等离子弧上的实验表明，控制器可实现接触引弧（contact-opening ignition）以及在弧电压变化条件下的电流参考跟踪。

Conclusion: 该自适应平均电流模式控制器可作为等离子应用中的快速电流控制层，为监督稳定提供合适的执行层。

Abstract: Reliable operation of plasma processes requires a power supply that can regulate and limit the arc current despite rapid changes of the electrical load. Gas-flow fluctuations, arc motion, changing attachment conditions, and electrode interactions can cause abrupt arc-voltage variations and may lead to arc interruption. The power supply must therefore provide fast current control and a suitable actuation layer for supervisory stabilization. This paper presents an adaptive average current-mode controller for a buck converter supplying a nonlinear plasma arc load. Based on an averaged model of the converter inductor dynamics, the controller combines current-error feedback with feedforward compensation of the measured arc voltage. Online adaptation of the inductance and its effective series resistance compensates for converter-parameter uncertainty without requiring an online nonlinear arc model. A Lyapunov analysis establishes bounded estimation errors and asymptotic current tracking for the averaged closed-loop system under bounded-reference assumptions. Experiments with an argon plasma arc demonstrate contact-opening ignition and current-reference tracking under varying arc- voltage conditions, supporting the controller as a fast current-control layer for plasma applications.

</details>


### [260] [An exact algorithm for the pickup and delivery problem with time windows and requests split across multiple stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08053)
*Ali Mehsin Alyasiry,Michael Forbes,Ella Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并求解了带时间窗与跨多堆栈分割请求的取送货问题（PDPTWRSMS），扩展了PDPTWMS，采用改进的"碎片法"求解，并验证了分割请求可显著降低路由成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDPTWMS应用场景（如零担运输中货物以托盘等标准化容器装载）中，车辆货舱被划分为容量有限的隔间且须遵循后进先出（LIFO）策略。原模型限制请求不可跨隔间分割，也难以处理单请求超过单隔间容量的情况，因此需要扩展为PDPTWRSMS以更贴近实际并降低成本。

Method: 将车辆装载空间划分为容量有限的隔间，每个隔间装卸遵循LIFO策略；允许一个请求在所有隔间间分割，且单个请求可超过单个隔间容量，仅受整车容量限制。采用改进的碎片法（method of fragments）进行求解。

Result: 计算实验表明，应用该扩展可带来显著的路由成本降低（总行驶距离和/或所用车辆数）。在不限制LIFO的PDPTWMS版本中，当车辆有三个或更多隔间时分割收益更大，而两个深隔间时收益较小。该方法能求解大量PDPTWRSMS实例，并显著优于当前PDPTWMS的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 允许请求跨隔间分割的PDPTWRSMS扩展具有实际价值，能有效降低路由成本，且改进碎片法是求解该类问题的有效且优于现有最先进方法的手段。

Abstract: This paper addresses an extension of the pickup and delivery problem with time windows and multiple stacks (PDPTWMS). We call the new extension the pickup and delivery problem with time windows and requests split across multiple stacks (PDPTWRSMS). Applications of the PDPTWRSMS arise in less-than-truckload transportation systems where loads are packed as pallets or other standardised containers. In the PDPTWRSMS, the vehicle's loading space is divided into compartments of finite capacity. Loading and unloading in each compartment must obey the last-in-first-out (LIFO) policy. Moreover, a request can be split among all compartments, and a single request can also exceed a single compartment's capacity. Thus, each request is only limited to the vehicle's capacity. Computational experiments show that applying this extension can lead to substantial routing cost reductions (the total distance travelled and/or the number of vehicles used), and, compared with versions of the PDPTWMS where compartments are not restricted to a LIFO policy, splitting is worth more once the vehicle has three or more compartments, though less when it has two deep ones. We apply a modified version of the recently proposed method of fragments. Results confirm that this approach can solve many PDPTWRSMS instances and significantly outperforms the current state-of-the-art method for the PDPTWMS.

</details>


### [261] [Shape optimization with inradius constraint and emergence of honeycomb structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08072)
*Dorin Bucur,Giuseppe Buttazzo,Alexis de Villeroché*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究在固定内切圆半径、且补集为等半径ε、互不相交且相互距离有下界的圆盘的开子集中，最大化平均扭转刚度，证明当ε充分小时最优值由具有正六边形蜂窝结构的区域渐近达到，并推广至Cheeger常数等变分能量，给出ε/内切圆半径的显式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何与谱优化中，扭转刚度等变分能量在给定约束下的最优形状长期受关注。本文关注带有"圆盘障碍"（补集为半径ε、相互分离的圆盘）且固定内切圆半径的二维区域，探索最优构型是否呈现规则蜂窝结构，并寻求可推广到其他变分能量的统一框架。

Method: 将问题通过Delaunay三角剖分约化为在顶点带圆形挖孔的三角形区域上的混合Dirichlet–Neumann问题，分析其能量；利用该方法处理小ε极限，推导最优渐近构型并构造显式界。

Result: 当ε充分小时，最大化平均扭转刚度的最优值可由补集呈现规则蜂窝（正六边形）结构的区域序列渐近达到；将方法推广到Cheeger常数等变分能量，并给出比率ε/内切圆半径的显式上界。

Conclusion: 规则蜂窝结构在给定内切圆半径与分离圆盘约束下是扭转刚度等变分能量的渐近最优构型；Delaunay三角剖分与带圆孔的混合边值问题的约化提供了一个一般性框架，可用于分析其他形状泛函。

Abstract: We maximize the average torsional rigidity among open subsets of $\mathbb{R}^2$ with fixed inradius whose complements consist of pairwise disjoint balls of fixed radius $\varepsilon$ with mutual distances bounded below. We prove that, provided $\varepsilon$ is sufficiently small, the optimal value is asymptotically attained by sequences of domains whose complements exhibit a regular honeycomb structure. The proof is reduced, via Delaunay triangulations, to the analysis of a mixed Dirichlet--Neumann problem on triangular domains with circular cutouts at their vertices. Our approach is quite general and extends to other variational energies, such as the Cheeger constant, for which we also derive an explicit bound of the ratio $\varepsilon/$ inradius.

</details>


### [262] [A Logarithmic-dual-update Bregman ADMM for Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08087)
*Di Hou,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了带对数对偶更新的熵Bregman交替方向乘子法（BADMM）求解离散最优传输问题，在一般代价与$\alpha=0$初始下证明了原始-对偶序列的$R$-线性收敛，并给出加速与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵BADMM采用简单的行、列归一化、无内迭代，CPU/GPU实现高效，但对一般OT问题的收敛性此前尚未建立；本文旨在填补这一理论空白并提升大规模计算效率。

Method: 从严格正的可行原始点与零乘子出发，对任意固定乘子步长$\alpha\in(0,2)$和足够大的固定罚参数，分析一般OT代价下原始-对偶序列的收敛；进一步刻画原始极限为初始点向最优解集的熵Bregman投影，并证明极限原始-对偶解的严格互补性；对二维网格上的可分代价利用卷积核结构加速矩阵-向量乘积并降低存储。

Result: 证明了原始-对偶序列的$R$-线性收敛（即使原始极限位于边界）；确立了熵Bregman投影极限刻画与严格互补性；数值实验显示对一般代价矩阵具竞争力，对二维网格边际上的平方欧氏代价相对HOT、HALO等先进求解器有显著加速，最大测试问题（每个边际$2048\times2048$网格点）在单GPU上约100秒求解。

Conclusion: 该工作为熵BADMM求解一般OT问题提供了收敛性理论保证与几何刻画，并通过核结构加速实现了大规模问题的高效求解。

Abstract: This paper studies an entropy Bregman alternating direction method of multipliers (BADMM) with logarithmic dual update for solving discrete optimal transport (OT) problems. The algorithm uses simple row and column normalizations without inner iterations, allowing efficient CPU and GPU implementations. However, the convergence of this method for general OT problems has not been established. Starting from a strictly positive feasible primal point and a zero multiplier, we establish $R$-linear convergence of the primal--dual sequence for general OT costs with any fixed multiplier stepsize in $(0,2)$ and a sufficiently large fixed penalty, even when the primal limit lies on the boundary. Our analysis further characterizes the primal limit as the entropy Bregman projection of the initial point onto the optimal solution set and establishes strict complementarity of the limiting OT primal--dual solution. For separable costs on two-dimensional grids, we exploit the kernel structure to accelerate matrix--vector products and reduce storage requirements. Numerical experiments show competitive performance for general cost matrices and substantial speedups over state-of-the-art solvers, including HOT and HALO, for squared Euclidean costs on two-dimensional-grid marginals. The largest tested problem, with $2048\times2048$ grid points in each marginal, is solved in approximately 100 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [263] [Optimal Control of a Class of Reaction-Diffusion Systems with Pointwise State Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08114)
*Eduardo Casas,Konstantinos Chrysafinos,Mariano Mateos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究半线性反应扩散方程组的最优控制问题，证明解的存在唯一性并推导一阶必要与二阶充分最优性条件，数值实验表明激活剂与抑制剂均可被有效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Brusselator、Schnakenberg、Gray--Scott等反应扩散模型建立统一的最优控制理论框架，且在不假设数据非负性的条件下讨论无约束、控制约束与状态约束三类问题。

Method: 针对半线性反应扩散方程组，在不加非负性假设下证明解的存在唯一性；对无约束、控制约束和状态约束最优控制问题，推出一阶必要最优性条件与二阶充分最优性条件，其中控制通过边界作用于激活剂方程。

Result: 证明了解的存在唯一性，并得到三类问题的一阶必要与二阶充分最优性条件；数值算例显示抑制剂与激活剂均能被高效控制。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于多种典型反应扩散系统，并可推广至带约束的最优控制问题，边界控制策略对激活剂和抑制剂均有效。

Abstract: We study optimal control problems governed by a system of semilinear reaction-diffusion equations. The assumptions on the governing system are formulated so that our results apply to several models, including the Brusselator, Schnakenberg, and Gray--Scott systems. We prove the existence and uniqueness of a solution to the governing system without imposing any non-negativity assumption on the data. This allows us to study unconstrained, control-constrained, and state-constrained optimal control problems. For all these problems, we derive first-order necessary and second-order sufficient optimality conditions. The control acts on the activator equation through the boundary. Numerical examples demonstrate that both the inhibitor and the activator can be efficiently controlled.

</details>


### [264] [Movement Stability of D-Optimal Sensor Placement Under Projector Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08152)
*Isabella Yin,Laura P. Schaposnik*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究时变D-最优子集选择中基于固定移动图上瓶颈匹配距离的稳定性问题，提出“半径P处的距离边际”概念，证明当投影算子漂移小且距离边际超过显式阈值时，新目标的每个最优解都落在该半径内，并构造加权路径反例说明此类分离信息不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时变D-最优子集选择用被替换坐标数衡量变化，但这并非物理位移：当一组移动传感器的最优选择发生变化时，单个传感器可能需要跨越整张图。因此需要以固定移动图上的瓶颈匹配距离来度量真实移动量，并研究在何种条件下最优解不会发生大范围迁移。

Method: 定义固定移动图上的瓶颈匹配距离作为移动度量，并定义半径P处的距离边际为距当前最优解超过P的配置中最小的旧目标损失。主结果为：若投影算子漂移足够小且所选半径处的距离边际超过显式阈值，则新目标的每个最优解都落在该半径内，无需打破平局规则或对子集的均匀条件假设。另构造加权路径反例、给出有限证书、局部与Voronoi限制保持全局最优的条件，以及过采样与正则化扩展，并识别适用的学习字典准则。

Result: 主定理表明投影漂移小且距离边际超过显式阈值时，新目标的所有最优解都在给定半径内，且不依赖打破平局或均匀条件假设。加权路径构造证明：在固定基线特征间隙下，任意小的扰动都能把最优传感器从图的一端移到另一端，因此仅依赖扰动大小和特征间隙的界无法随扰动消失。

Conclusion: 该工作以瓶颈匹配距离重新刻画时变D-最优子集选择的稳定性，给出基于距离边际的显式稳定性阈值与必要性反例，并提供有限证书、局部与Voronoi限制的全局最优保持条件及过采样、正则化扩展，明确了结果适用的学习字典准则。

Abstract: In time-varying D-optimal subset selection, the number of replaced coordinates does not measure physical displacement: when the optimal selection for a set of mobile sensors changes, a single sensor may have to cross the entire graph. We therefore measure movement by a bottleneck matching distance on a fixed mobility graph, and define the distance margin at radius P as the smallest old-objective loss among configurations farther than P from the current maximizer. Our main result shows that if the projector drift is small and the distance margin at a chosen radius exceeds an explicit threshold, then every maximizer of the new objective lies within that radius, without tie-breaking rules or uniform conditioning assumptions over subsets. Moreover, we show that separation information of this kind is genuinely needed: a weighted-path construction shows that, at a fixed baseline eigengap, an arbitrarily small perturbation can move an optimal sensor from one end of the graph to the other, so no bound depending only on perturbation size and eigengap can vanish with the perturbation. We also give a finite certificate, conditions under which local and Voronoi restrictions retain a global optimum, and oversampled and regularized extensions, and we identify the learned dictionary criteria to which the results apply.

</details>


### [265] [Spatially Explicit Optimal Harvesting and Adjoint-Based Diagnostics for Nonlinear Biomass Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08374)
*Tin Nwe Aye,Wolfgang Bock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了含Beverton-Holt繁殖、自然死亡、扩散与捕捞的空间显式非线性生物量模型，证明其适定性并确定存续-灭绝临界捕捞阈值，进而建立分布最优控制问题及状态-伴随最优性系统，数值模拟揭示扩散降低空间异质性、伴随变量与最优捕捞场的空间变化有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为将空间生物量动态与未来管理价值和捕捞决策相耦合，需在生物量模型中同时纳入密度依赖繁殖、空间扩散与分布捕捞，并从最优控制视角刻画捕捞策略与生物量边际价值。

Method: 从受产卵亲体生物量数据启发的简化生物量模型出发，构建含Beverton-Holt产量、自然死亡、扩散驱动空间移动与捕捞的反应-扩散方程，采用Neumann边界条件与空间分布捕捞；证明正性、有界性与适定性；确定区分空间均匀平衡存续与灭绝的临界捕捞阈值；建立分布最优捕捞问题，推导状态-伴随最优性系统与控制的逐点投影刻画。

Result: 给出了生物量动力系统的正性、有界性与适定性；识别出存续-灭绝的临界捕捞阈值；获得伴随变量作为捕捞目标中生物量边际未来价值的度量；数值模拟显示扩散逐步削减生物量的初始空间异质性，而在所考察参数区间内伴随变量与最优捕捞场的空间变化有限。

Conclusion: 状态-伴随-控制耦合框架可将空间生物量动态与未来管理价值及捕捞决策联系起来；伴随变量提供了超越生物量丰度的诊断信息。

Abstract: We develop a spatially explicit nonlinear biomass model for exploited fish populations incorporating density-dependent Beverton-Holt production, natural mortality, diffusion driven spatial movement, and harvesting. Starting from a reduced biomass model motivated by spawning stock biomass data, we derive a reaction-diffusion model with Neumann boundary conditions and spatially distributed harvesting. We establish positivity, boundedness, and well-posedness of the biomass dynamics, and identify a critical harvesting threshold separating persistence and extinction regimes of spatially homogeneous equilibria. A distributed optimal harvesting problem is then formulated, and the corresponding state-adjoint optimality system and pointwise projection characterization of the harvesting control are derived. The adjoint variable provides a measure of the marginal future value of biomass within the harvesting objective and offers a diagnostic that complements biomass abundance alone. Numerical simulations examine the coupled evolution of biomass, the adjoint variable, and optimal harvesting effort. The results show that diffusion progressively reduces the initial spatial heterogeneity in biomass, while the computed adjoint and optimal harvesting fields exhibit limited spatial variation under the parameter regime considered. These results illustrate how the coupled state-adjoint-control framework links spatial biomass dynamics with future management value and harvesting decisions.

</details>


### [266] [Budget-Constrained Multi-Consensus Decentralized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08508)
*Shuyi Ren,Nicol`o Michelusi,Erik G. Larsson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究预算约束下兼顾通信与计算效率的去中心化梯度下降（DGD），提出并分析多轮共识去中心化梯度下降（mcDGD），给出刻画共识质量与优化动态相互作用的有限时间收敛界，并导出最优共识分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源（通信与计算）有限的场景下，去中心化优化需要合理分配共识轮数与步长，而现有工作缺乏对“通信—计算”资源分配问题的系统分析，尤其缺乏刻画共识质量与优化动态耦合关系的理论工具。本文将多轮共识DGD作为通向更广泛通信—计算分配问题的第一步进行研究。

Method: 提出并分析mcDGD方案：允许每轮迭代的共识轮数与步长变化；基于DGD的统一分析框架推导有限时间收敛界。假设仅需局部目标函数凸，全局目标函数光滑且强凸。进而基于所得界给出资源约束下的共识分配策略，并分析其最优性（等分配在整数取整意义下最优）。

Result: 得到显式刻画共识质量与优化动态相互作用的有限时间收敛界；在给定步长规则下证明各迭代间等量分配共识轮数在整数取整范围内最优；数值实验验证理论结论，并相较现有多轮共识去中心化优化基线展示更优的通信—计算权衡。

Conclusion: mcDGD为资源受限下的通信—计算分配提供了可分析框架与原则性策略，等共识分配在特定步长规则下具有最优性；实验支持理论并表明其优于现有基线，是迈向更一般通信—计算分配问题的第一步。

Abstract: We investigate decentralized gradient descent (DGD) with emphasis on efficient communication and computation resource utilization under budget constraints. As a first step toward the broader communication-computation allocation problem, we consider and analyze a \textit{multi-consensus decentralized gradient descent} (mcDGD) scheme, where the number of consensus rounds and the stepsize are allowed to vary across iterations. Building on a unified analytical framework for DGD, we derive finite-time convergence bounds that explicitly characterize the interaction between consensus quality and optimization dynamics. Our analysis requires only convexity of the local objective functions while assuming smoothness and strong convexity of the global objective. The resulting bounds enable a principled consensus-allocation strategy under resource constraints, for which we show that equal allocation of consensus rounds across iterations is optimal under our stepsize rule, up to integer rounding. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical findings and demonstrate favorable communication-computation tradeoffs compared with existing multi-consensus decentralized optimization baselines.

</details>


### [267] [Primal-Dual Error Bounds and KKT Metric Subregularity in Convex Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08587)
*Jiani Li,Qingna Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究凸复合优化问题及其Fenchel对偶的原始与对偶误差界，通过引入约化原始-对偶KKT映射，建立了KKT度量次正则性与原始/对偶局部误差界之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸复合优化中原始与对偶误差界通常被分别研究，缺乏统一刻画其内在联系的框架。作者希望借助约化KKT映射，将原始误差界、对偶误差界与KKT度量次正则性统一起来。

Method: 原始问题使用邻近梯度残差，对偶问题（一般非光滑）使用次微分残差；构造约化原始-对偶KKT映射，其零点集等于原始与对偶解集的笛卡尔积（在可解性与强对偶假设下）；建立KKT映射度量次正则性与原始、对偶局部误差界的双向蕴含关系。

Result: 若复合矩阵行满秩且光滑梯度局部Lipschitz连续，KKT度量次正则性等价于原始局部误差界；若光滑项强凸，则等价于对偶局部误差界；在行满秩加强凸条件下，KKT度量次正则性等价于两个误差界同时成立。此外还建立了Luo-Tseng误差界与局部误差界的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理了二次-多面体模型、带一般凸正则项的强凸光滑模型以及超出多面体性与强凸性的正则化最小二乘模型，给出了原始、对偶与KKT误差界性质的统一分析。

Abstract: We study primal and dual error bounds for convex composite optimization problems and their Fenchel duals. For the primal problem, we use a proximal-gradient residual, while for the generally nonsmooth dual problem we use a subdifferential residual. We introduce a reduced primal--dual KKT mapping whose zero set coincides with the Cartesian product of the primal and dual solution sets under the standing solvability and strong-duality assumptions.
  We establish implications in both directions between metric subregularity of the reduced KKT mapping and the primal and dual local error bounds. If the composite matrix has full row rank and the smooth gradient is locally Lipschitz continuous, KKT metric subregularity is equivalent to the primal local error bound. If the smooth term is strongly convex, it is equivalent to the dual local error bound. Under full row rank and strong convexity, KKT metric subregularity is equivalent to the simultaneous validity of both error bounds.
  We also establish relations between the Luo--Tseng and local error bounds. The framework is then applied to quadratic--polyhedral models, strongly convex smooth models with general convex regularizers, and regularized least-squares models beyond polyhedrality and strong convexity, providing a unified treatment of primal, dual, and KKT error-bound properties.

</details>


### [268] [On the Exact Linearization of Multi-Input Discrete-Time Flat Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08697)
*Johannes Schrotshamer,Bernd Kolar,Markus Schöberl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多输入离散时间非线性系统的精确线性化，证明前向平坦系统可通过输入延拓实现静态反馈线性化，后向平坦系统可通过变量预延拓实现精确线性化，将已有双输入结果推广到任意m输入情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的前向/后向平坦离散时间非线性系统精确线性化结果仅针对双输入系统，缺乏对任意输入个数的通用结论，需要将该理论推广至m输入一般情形。

Method: 基于平坦参数化的结构性质：对前向平坦系统，采用适当变换输入的延拓（prolongation）使其静态反馈线性化；对后向平坦系统，构造性地证明通过对系统变量适当函数的预延拓（prelongation）可实现精确线性化。

Result: 提出了适用于任意m输入的前向与后向平坦系统精确线性化构造性方法，并用一个教学示例和四旋翼飞行器离散时间模型验证了所提线性化流程。

Conclusion: 前向平坦与后向平坦离散时间非线性系统均可通过相应延拓/预延拓方法实现精确线性化，且结论适用于任意输入个数，扩展了原有的双输入理论框架。

Abstract: In this contribution, we address the exact linearization of forward- and backward-flat discrete-time nonlinear systems with an arbitrary number of inputs. Building on structural properties of the flat parameterization, we show that forward-flat systems can be rendered static feedback linearizable by prolongations of suitably transformed inputs. Analogously, for backward-flat systems, we derive a constructive procedure showing that an exact linearization can be achieved by prelongations of suitably chosen functions of the system variables. In this way, we extend previously established results for two-input systems to the general m-input case. Finally, we illustrate the proposed linearization procedures by an academic example and a discrete-time model of a quadrotor.

</details>


### [269] [Sliced Wasserstein Barycenters: The Analysis Approach within the Barycentric Coding Model in the Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08734)
*Rocío Díaz Martín,James M. Murphy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文从变分视角研究切片Wasserstein重心，推导其一阶变分公式，并用于重心编码模型中的坐标恢复与可靠性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重心编码模型中，给定查询测度与有限概率测度字典，需要恢复单纯形约束的重心坐标，但切片Wasserstein重心关于经典2-Wasserstein几何的变分分析尚不清晰，缺乏显式梯度与最优性判据。

Method: 推导切片Wasserstein重心泛函关于2-Wasserstein几何的显式一阶变分公式，梯度表示为球面上1D单调传输位移的平均；由平稳性方程给出估计重心坐标的Gram矩阵判据（转化为二次规划），并由其不动点形式构造合成迭代生成新的重心测度；对高斯模板分析全局重心与临界点性质，并利用1D传输势构造全局最优性证书。

Result: 得到切片Wasserstein重心泛函的显式梯度表达；给出基于Gram矩阵的坐标估计二次规划与合成迭代；证明高斯模板下每个全局切片Wasserstein重心都是高斯分布，但泛函可能存在非高斯临界点；提供基于1D传输势的全局最优性证书；数值实验在合成查询上实现准确的坐标恢复，并展示坐标用于数据表示、平稳性残差用于可靠性评估。

Conclusion: 切片Wasserstein重心的变分分析为重心编码提供显式梯度、二次规划坐标估计、合成迭代与全局最优性证书，兼顾理论刻画与数值验证，并支持数据表示与可靠性评估。

Abstract: We study sliced Wasserstein barycenters from a variational perspective in Wasserstein space, with emphasis on the analysis problem in the barycentric coding model: given a query measure and a finite dictionary of probability measures, recover simplex-constrained barycentric coordinates. We derive an explicit first-variation formula for the sliced Wasserstein barycenter functional with respect to the classical 2-Wasserstein geometry. The resulting gradient is expressed as an average of 1D monotone transport displacements over the sphere. The stationarity equation yields a Gram-matrix criterion for estimating barycentric coordinates through a quadratic program, while its fixed-point form leads to a synthesis iteration to new barycentric measures. For Gaussian templates, we show that every global sliced Wasserstein barycenter is Gaussian; however, the functional may admit non-Gaussian critical points. We also provide a certificate for global optimality built from the 1D transport potentials. Numerical experiments demonstrate accurate coordinate recovery on synthesized queries and illustrate the use of these coordinates for data representation and stationarity residuals for reliability assessment.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [Memory Prediction Excess: A Probabilistic Quantity for Predictive Gain and Memory Length in Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06894)
*Jiahao Jiang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"记忆预测盈余"(MPE)指标，量化离散时间有限状态过程中，利用完整历史相对于仅用静态边际分布对预测准确率的平均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：过去信息究竟能在多大程度上提升对下一状态预测的正确概率。经典信息论方法（如互信息）刻画的是信息量而非预测准确率，因此需要一个直接以预测准确率为基础的互补性度量。

Method: 定义MPE为最优条件预测准确率期望与最优静态预测准确率之差；分析其基本性质（非负性、上界及其取等条件、退化情形）；引入取值于单位区间的归一化版本；通过比较不同长度历史的预测导出下界；扩展到有限长度历史得到FH-MPE，并定义达到与全历史同等预测性能所需的最小记忆长度。

Result: MPE恒非负；存在依赖静态准确率的上界，当且仅当未来几乎必然是过去的确定性函数时达到；刻画了MPE消失的退化情形；期望最优预测准确率对历史长度单调；对有限阶马尔可夫链，最小记忆长度被马尔可夫阶数所界定。

Conclusion: MPE及其变体以条件概率和预测准确率表述，提供了从预测效用角度理解记忆价值的概率视角，与经典信息论方法互补。

Abstract: A central question in the prediction of stochastic processes is the extent to which past information can improve the probability of correctly predicting the next state. We introduce the Memory Prediction Excess (MPE) to address this question quantitatively. The MPE measures the average improvement in prediction accuracy obtained by using the entire observed history relative to using only the static marginal distribution, in discrete-time finite-state processes. It is defined as the difference between the expected optimal conditional prediction accuracy and the optimal static prediction accuracy. Its basic properties are examined: the MPE is always non-negative; it admits an upper bound depending on the static accuracy, attained if and only if the future is almost surely a deterministic function of the past; and degenerate cases in which the MPE vanishes are characterized. A normalized version, taking values in the unit interval, is introduced as a dimensionless measure of predictive efficiency. A lower bound is derived by comparing predictions based on histories of different lengths, showing that the expected optimal prediction accuracy is monotone with respect to the history length. The framework is extended to finite-length histories, where the finite-history MPE (FH-MPE) measures the predictive gain attainable when only the most recent observations are retained. This leads to the notion of a minimal memory length required to achieve the same predictive performance as the full history. For finite-order Markov chains, this minimal memory length is shown to be bounded by the Markov order. The MPE and its variants are formulated in terms of conditional probabilities and prediction accuracies, offering a probabilistic perspective on the predictive utility of memory that is complementary to classical information-theoretic approaches.

</details>


### [271] [Low-Rank and Structured Sparse Tensor Decomposition for Anomaly Detection in Multivariate Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06930)
*Mohammad N. Bisheh,Che-Yi Liao,Kamran Paynabar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出两种无监督稀疏张量分解方法ES-CP与FG-Lasso，用于多变量函数型过程数据的异常检测与故障定位，通过匹配稀疏惩罚与故障结构提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量函数型数据在多传感器制造系统中广泛存在，其正常变异在样本、传感器和时间维度上强相关，而故障既可能表现为孤立偏差，也可能表现为集中于少数传感器特定时间轨迹中的结构化偏离，传统方法难以同时刻画这种多模态结构并准确检测与定位故障。

Method: 提出两种保持多模态结构的无监督稀疏张量分解方法：Entrywise Sparse CP Decomposition (ES-CP) 采用逐元素ℓ1惩罚以识别局部异常；Fiberwise Sparse-Group Lasso CP Decomposition (FG-Lasso) 结合逐元素与逐纤维惩罚，能同时检测局部偏差和集中于时间纤维的异常。两方法均以低秩CP分解表示正常过程行为，并通过交替优化及闭式稀疏分量更新进行估计。

Result: 两项仿真研究在不同故障结构、信号强度、噪声水平和缺失观测下评估性能：FG-Lasso在第一个研究几乎所有设置中取得最高或并列最高的宏F1分数，并在第二个研究中取得最高宏F1。多通道锻造过程案例中，FG-Lasso与ES-CP的宏F1分别为0.85和0.82，而TRPCA和基于PCA的检测器均不高于0.69。

Conclusion: 显式地将稀疏惩罚与预期故障结构相匹配，可同时改善高维函数型过程中的异常检测与故障定位性能，验证了FG-Lasso与ES-CP方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate functional data arise in many modern manufacturing systems, where multiple sensors record densely sampled process trajectories. Monitoring such data is challenging because nominal variation is strongly correlated across samples, sensors, and time, while faults may appear either as isolated deviations or as structured departures concentrated within a limited number of sensor-specific temporal trajectories. We propose two unsupervised sparse tensor decomposition methods that preserve this multimode structure. Entrywise Sparse CP Decomposition (ES-CP) uses an entrywise \(\ell_1\) penalty to identify localized anomalies, whereas Fiberwise Sparse-Group Lasso CP Decomposition (FG-Lasso) combines entrywise and fiberwise penalties to detect both localized deviations and anomalies concentrated within temporal fibers. Both methods represent nominal process behavior through a low-rank CP decomposition and are estimated using alternating optimization with closed-form sparse-component updates. Two simulation studies evaluate performance under different fault structures, signal severities, noise levels, and missing observations. FG-Lasso attains or ties the highest macro F$_1$ score in almost all settings in the first study and achieves the highest macro F$_1$ score. In a multichannel forging-process case study, FG-Lasso and ES-CP obtain macro F$_1$ scores of 0.85 and 0.82, respectively, compared with 0.69 or lower for TRPCA and PCA-based anomaly detectors. The results demonstrate that explicitly matching the sparse penalty to the anticipated fault structure improves both anomaly detection and fault localization in high-dimensional functional processes.

</details>


### [272] [Data Fusion for Errors-in-Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07048)
*Huali Zhao,Molei Liu,Tianying Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究目标研究中仅有单次易错替代变量、外部研究提供来自不同人群的重复替代测量的变量误差问题；提出条件可迁移性假设识别目标条件测量误差分布，构建数据融合估计量（Fuse-EIV），并建立统一谱理论、渐近展开、一致性、收敛速率及原子谱目标下的高斯/自助极限与推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标研究常只有单次易错暴露代理，外部重复测量虽有价值，但来源人群与目标人群存在异质性：测量误差分布可能依赖观测到的无误差变量，且无误差变量分布本身在两项研究间可能不同。既有方法难以在此异质性与误差异方差条件下识别并估计目标的测量误差分布，因此需要新的识别假设与数据融合估计框架。

Method: 1）引入条件可迁移性假设，结合额外重复误差条件，识别目标条件测量误差分布；2）基于识别结果提出面向广泛目标泛函的数据融合估计量，融合条件反卷积、灵活干扰参数估计与正交校正以降低对干扰估计的一阶敏感性；3）建立统一谱理论，同时覆盖扩散谱与有限原子谱目标泛函，给出一般渐近展开、一致性及目标特定的收敛速率界；4）对有限原子谱目标，在额外中心化条件下建立联合高斯与自助极限，实现光滑矩变换的推断。

Result: 收敛速率界同时依赖测量误差、潜在暴露与目标泛函的谱性质；有限原子谱目标可获得联合高斯与自助极限，从而在附加中心化条件下实现光滑矩变换推断。模拟中 Fuse-EIV 对主要暴露相关系数偏差较小；在 NHANES 应用中，考虑人群异质性与误差异方差会改变实证结论。

Conclusion: 条件可迁移性假设使外部重复测量可在源-目标异质性下用于识别目标条件测量误差分布；数据融合估计量配合正交校正与统一谱理论，在扩散谱与原子谱目标下均具良好渐近性质，并为特定目标提供有效推断；实证表明忽略人群异质性与误差异方差可能误导结论。

Abstract: We study errors-in-variables problems in which a target study contains only a single error-prone surrogate of an unobserved exposure, while an external source study provides repeated surrogate measurements from a different population. The measurement error distribution is allowed to depend on the observed error-free variables, and the error-free variable distribution itself may differ between studies. We introduce a conditional transportability assumption that enables the use of external repeated measurements under source-target heterogeneity. Together with additional replicate-error conditions, it identifies the target conditional measurement-error distribution. Building on this identification result, we develop a data-fusion estimator for a broad class of target functionals. The estimator combines conditional deconvolution, flexible nuisance estimation, and orthogonal correction that reduces first-order sensitivity to nuisance estimation. For the proposed estimator, we develop a unified spectral theory covering both diffuse-spectrum and finite atomic-spectrum target functionals, derive a general asymptotic expansion, and establish consistency and target-specific convergence-rate bounds. The resulting convergence-rate bounds depend jointly on the spectral properties of the measurement error, the latent exposure, and the target functional. For finite atomic-spectrum targets, we further establish joint Gaussian and bootstrap limits, yielding inference for smooth moment transformations under an additional centering condition. In the reported simulations, Fuse-EIV has small bias for the primary exposure-related coefficient. Applications to the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey illustrate how accounting for population heterogeneity and error heteroscedasticity can change empirical conclusions.

</details>


### [273] [sHAIL-Causal: A Sequential Staircase Procedure for Invariant Causal Predictor Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07057)
*Ernest Fokoué*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出sHAIL-Causal范式，用"拟合优度饱和+跨环境不变性"的联合准则替代结构风险最小化的复杂度控制，使阶梯式假设空间搜索能收敛到真实因果预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: general sHAIL将饱和判据留作开放问题，而仅以复杂度作为阶梯上升依据时，模型会被混杂预测器吸引——这类预测器可降低经验风险却不反映稳定因果结构，因此需要一个能够拒绝混杂的饱和判据。

Method: 在嵌套假设类H_0<H_1<...<H_K上执行顺序阶梯上升；饱和信号由拟合优度饱和与跨环境不变性组成的联合准则给出；理论上在逐变量Richness条件下证明不变性门控阶梯停机于真实因果预测集；针对贪心搜索失败（源于不变性统计量沿单变量路径的非单调性），提出有界穷举块播种加校准接受阈值的修正方案；并将保证扩展到顺序到达的环境，以及形式化"明智教师"对饱和学习者的干预。

Result: 理论与仿真表明：仅用复杂度的阶梯会被混杂预测器诱导；不变性门控阶梯在Richness条件下可证明停机于真实因果集；朴素贪心搜索即使满足Richness也无法恢复因果集，块播种+校准阈值可修复；顺序环境扩展给出在每次到达时都有效的置信保证，这是一次性穷举搜索在不重复全组合搜索的情况下无法提供的。

Conclusion: 不变性应作为阶梯式学习的门控饱和信号而非事后检验，从而以因果不变性取代纯复杂度正则；同时需以块播种克服不变性统计量的非单调性；该框架可自然延伸到在线环境与教师干预场景。

Abstract: We introduce sHAIL-Causal, the causal specialization of the Saturated Hierarchical Atomic Incremental Learning (sHAIL) paradigm: a sequential staircase procedure that ascends a nested hierarchy of hypothesis classes H_0 < H_1 < ... < H_K once a saturation signal indicates that mastery of the current stage has plateaued. Where general sHAIL leaves the saturation criterion open, sHAIL-Causal instantiates it with a joint criterion of goodness-of-fit saturation and cross-environment invariance, replacing the complexity control of Structural Risk Minimization. We show, theoretically and by simulation, that complexity-only staircases are seduced by confounded predictors that lower empirical risk without reflecting stable causal structure, whereas an invariance-gated staircase provably halts at the true causal predictor set under a per-variable Richness condition. We show that naive greedy search fails to recover the causal set even under Richness, trace the failure to non-monotonicity of the invariance statistic along single-variable paths, and validate a fix combining bounded-exhaustive block-seeding with a calibrated acceptance threshold. We then extend the guarantee to environments arriving sequentially, yielding a confidence guarantee that stays valid at every arrival, which one-shot exhaustive search cannot offer without repeating its full combinatorial search. We close by formalizing the intervention of a wise teacher who lifts a saturated learner off a plateau of boredom.

</details>


### [274] [Learning Decision-Stump Thresholds in Context: Dynamics of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07074)
*Hong Ha Le,Jackie Lok,Atsushi Nitanda,Yan Shuo Tan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文研究梯度预训练在双参数softmax注意力模型中如何学习"估计决策阈值"这一统计规则，证明在大分辨率初始化下，常数步长梯度下降产生误差为 Õ((m∧n)^{-1}+N^{-1}) 的冻结估计器，机制为参数协同发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策阈值估计需要定位未知边界附近的观测点，但梯度预训练如何从带标签上下文中习得这一统计规则、并迁移到新鲜上下文，缺乏理论刻画。论文旨在给出该学习过程的可证分析。

Method: 采用固定特征与不等式方向的双参数softmax注意力模型；预训练使用带标签上下文及其真实阈值，常数步长梯度下降在m个任务、每任务n个样本上进行，需仅凭上下文推断新阈值。分析基于大分辨率初始化，利用总体训练轨迹刻画参数协同发散，并通过控制相对于逐级参数尺度下收缩方向的梯度误差，证明有限语料上的迁移。

Result: 对每个固定内点阈值及任意新鲜上下文规模N，得到冻结估计器误差 Õ((m∧n)^{-1}+N^{-1})，其中两项分别对应有限预训练精度与新鲜上下文定位。总体训练先校准相对标签与特征分数，再使注意力尺度以 t^{1/4} 增长，总体阈值误差为 O(t^{-1/4})。并证明了在不需长时间跟踪总体轨迹的情况下，可认证一个增长的训练区间。

Conclusion: 论文刻画了梯度预训练习得阈值估计规则的机制与误差上界，揭示参数协同发散与注意力尺度增长的关键作用；同时指出单头模型的边界局限，并从统计角度解释反射对称化可能带来的改进。

Abstract: Estimating a decision threshold requires locating observations near an unknown boundary. We study how gradient-based pretraining learns this statistical rule in a two-parameter softmax-attention model with a fixed feature and inequality direction. Pretraining uses labeled contexts and their true thresholds; a fresh threshold must be inferred from context alone. Under a large-resolution initialization, constant-step gradient descent on $m$ tasks with $n$ examples each produces a frozen estimator with error $\widetilde O((m\wedge n)^{-1}+N^{-1})$ for each fixed interior threshold and every fresh-context size $N$. The two terms separate finite-pretraining accuracy from fresh-context localization. The mechanism is coordinated parameter divergence: population training calibrates the relative label and feature scores, then increases the attention scale as $t^{1/4}$, giving population threshold error $O(t^{-1/4})$. To transfer this mechanism to a fixed finite corpus, we control gradient errors relative to the shrinking directions of progress at successive parameter scales. This certifies a growing training interval without requiring long-time tracking of the population trajectory. We also identify the boundary limitation of the one-head model and explain statistically what a reflected symmetrization could achieve.

</details>


### [275] [A Query Is Not a Commitment: Learning to Correct Expert Answers in Online Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07084)
*Yannis Montreuil,Axel Carlier,Lai Xing Ng,Wei Tsang Ooi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究在线学习中的"延迟决策"问题：学习者在购买专家答案前先固定一个修正函数，再将函数作用于收到的答案，提出ORUCB算法实现高概率伪遗憾界O(√T·log(T+1))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不准确的专家在被修正后仍可提供有用信息。核心难点在于：观测到的损失同时反映专家质量与尚未完成的修正，早期误差会抑制那些"修正后本会有价值"的查询，导致学习与路由决策相互干扰。

Method: 提出ORUCB算法，将共享响应与专家专属响应进行多项式pooling；通过对累积响应学习误差的界来校准置信度加权的风险回归与探索，使路由器在决定购买哪些答案时能将该误差纳入考量。理论假设包括：有界残差与分歧、最优响应存在固定可行模型、自由风险与最优查询风险均为线性模型。

Result: 在固定问题参数下，校准算法达到高概率伪遗憾O(√T·log(T+1))；该保证允许奇异答案分布与共享响应误设，最优性是相对于有界响应类而言的。在四条测试流上，所选三次策略的含费成本低于七个"原样部署答案"的基线；与常见修正学习器的比较考察了路由，六种价格比较衡量了成本与查询率。

Conclusion: 修正能力与路由应联合学习：通过显式建模并校准响应学习误差，可在专家质量未知时避免过早弃用潜在有用的专家，从而在有限遗憾下实现成本更优的"先修正后采纳"决策。

Abstract: An inaccurate expert can still provide useful information after correction. We study online learning to defer in which the learner chooses an expert and fixes a correction function before purchasing its answer, then applies that function to the answer received. The difficulty is that observed losses reflect both expert quality and an unfinished correction: early errors can discourage queries that would be valuable after learning. We propose ORUCB, which pools shared and expert-specific polynomial responses. A bound on cumulative response-learning error calibrates confidence-weighted risk regression and exploration, allowing the router to account for this error when deciding which answers to buy. Under bounded residuals and disagreements, a fixed feasible model of optimal responses, and linear models of free and optimal queried risk, the calibrated algorithm achieves high-probability pseudo-regret $O(\sqrt T\log(T+1))$ over $T$ rounds for fixed problem parameters. The guarantee permits singular answer distributions and misspecified shared responses; optimality is relative to the bounded response class. On four test streams, the selected cubic policy has lower fee-inclusive cost than seven baselines that deploy answers unchanged. Comparisons with a common correction learner examine routing, while six-price comparisons measure cost and query rates.

</details>


### [276] [How Inefficient Is Natural Gradient Descent? From Exact Optimality to Θ( \sqrt{ \log d } ) Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07228)
*Guni Sharon,Alan Kuhnle*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文量化了自然梯度下降(NGD)沿混合测地线相对Fisher--Rao最短路径的额外开销，用低效比R≥1刻画，并按参数维度d给出三种regime的界，实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想化NGD在前向KL目标下沿混合测地线移动，而该路径通常比最短的Fisher--Rao路径更长，造成计算效率损失。论文旨在量化这一额外开销及其随维度的增长规律。

Method: 定义低效比R为混合测地线的Fisher长度除以Fisher--Rao距离，并求其在上确界随参数维度d的界。通过张量判据划分regime：(I)二次势或一维族（R=1）；(II)有界三阶偏度且Fisher--Rao直径有限（维度无关界）；(III)尺度族的乘积（如高斯协方差、Gamma率），R渐近Θ(√log d)。结合每步Fisher弦预算将R转化为步数成本。

Result: 理论上确立三种regime及其界。实验证实：二次势族(I)达到机器精度R=1；类别分布界π/(2√2)被逼近但未达到(II)；尺度乘积(III)的采样R随d增长，长距离高维移动时R≈1.5，R随d无界增长。

Conclusion: NGD在非二次族下沿混合测地线的路径开销可用R严格量化，且在尺度乘积族中随维度无界增长；这意味着在每步Fisher弦预算下，NGD所需步数至少是被Fisher--Rao测地线优化器的R倍，揭示了NGD在高维下的固有计算代价。

Abstract: Natural gradient descent (NGD) underlies common methods in ML. For dually flat families, idealized NGD on the forward Kullback--Leibler objective follows the mixture geodesic which is often longer than the shortest Fisher--Rao path. We quantify this overhead by the inefficiency ratio \(R \ge 1\), the Fisher length of the mixture geodesic divided by the Fisher--Rao distance, and bound its supremum over endpoint pairs as a function of the parameter dimension \(d\). A tensor criterion identifies the regime (I) families, with \(R=1\) everywhere: exactly those with quadratic potential or dimension one, such as fixed-covariance Gaussians. For non-quadratic families, we prove two further regimes: (II) bounded third-order skewness plus finite Fisher--Rao diameter yields a dimension-independent bound; and (III) for products of scale families---including Gaussian covariances and Gamma rates---\(R\) grows as \(Θ(\sqrt{\log d})\), unbounded in \(d\). Under a per-step Fisher-chord budget, \(R\) translates to a practical computational cost: NGD requires asymptotically at least \(R\) times as many steps as an optimizer following the Fisher--Rao geodesic. Experiments confirm all three regimes: \(R=1\) to machine precision for quadratic-potential families (I), the categorical bound \(π/(2\sqrt{2})\) is approached but not attained (II), and sampled scale-product \(R\) grows with \(d\), reaching \(R \approx 1.5\) for long, high-dimensional moves (III).

</details>


### [277] [Assumption-lean logistic regression with missing covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07292)
*Jyotishka Ray Choudhury,Kabir Aladin Verchand,Richard J. Samworth,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究协变量存在缺失且分布未知情形下的逻辑回归参数估计问题，提出一种基于 $Z$-估计与新型单调算子的随机逼近方法，在MCAR假设下实现了参数速率级别的信号恢复，并在理论上刻画了估计量的 $\ell_2^2$ 风险对缺失模式的依赖，给出上下界匹配的极小化极大分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典缺失协变量处理方法依赖精心设计的插补方案或似然近似，通常需要已知协变量分布，且常导致非凸 $M$-估计问题。这些方法在线性模型中表现良好，但在协变量分布未知的中等维非线性问题（如逻辑回归）中会出现严重失效。因此需要一个在协变量分布未知（仅有界）的"弱假设"设定下仍可靠的方法。

Method: 作者在协变量分布未知但有界的assumption-lean设定下，针对缺失协变量的逻辑回归设计了一种随机逼近（stochastic approximation）方法，其核心是基于 $Z$-估计（$Z$-estimation）引入一种新型单调算子（monotone operator），并证明该算法计算高效。

Result: 在协变量完全随机缺失（MCAR）假设下，算法实现可证明的参数速率信号恢复，并精确刻画估计量的 $\ell_2^2$ 风险与缺失模式（含异质观测概率）的关系。理论上证明该方法始终优于忽略缺失数据的"完整案例"（complete-case）估计量。即使在同质缺失（每个协变量独立以概率 $q$ 被观测）下，界也呈现对 $q$ 的复杂非标准依赖，较仅用完整案例有显著改进。作者进一步给出新的信息论下界，证明这种对 $q$ 的复杂依赖在极小化极大意义下是本质性的。

Conclusion: 该工作为协变量分布未知的缺失数据逻辑回归提供了计算高效且统计最优的估计方法，其风险刻画对缺失概率的依赖由匹配的上下界共同支撑，表明该方法在极小化极大框架下相较于完整案例分析具有根本性优势。

Abstract: Missing covariates are frequently encountered in supervised learning problems, and classical methods for estimation using such data use carefully chosen imputation schemes for missing data, or likelihood approximations that lead to nonconvex $M$-estimation problems. These methods and their relatives are suitable for scenarios in which the covariate distribution is known, and more broadly, have enjoyed tremendous success in linear models. But even in basic nonlinear problems such as logistic regression in moderate dimensions, such methods can experience drastic failure modes when the covariate distribution is unknown.
  Motivated by the need for reliable alternatives, we consider the problem of parameter estimation in logistic regression with missing covariates. Crucially, we operate in the assumption-lean setting where the covariate distribution is unknown (but bounded). We design a stochastic approximation method that is based on $Z$-estimation with a novel monotone operator, and establish that our algorithm is computationally efficient and achieves provable signal recovery at parametric rates under the hypothesis that covariates are missing completely at random. Our theory sharply characterizes the $\ell_2^2$ risk of the estimator in terms of the missingness profile, accommodating heterogeneous observation probabilities. Importantly, it shows that our method always outperforms the de facto ``complete-case'' estimator that ignores observations with any missing data. Even in the setting with homogeneous missingness (in which each covariate is observed independently with probability $q$), our bounds exhibit intricate and nonstandard dependence on $q$ that can yield significant improvements over using only complete cases. We complement our upper bounds with new information-theoretic lower bounds that show that this intricate dependence on $q$ is fundamental in a minimax sense.

</details>


### [278] [HyperNSDE: Personalized Neural SDEs for Joint Static-Longitudinal Clinical Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07383)
*Perrine Chassat,Agathe Guilloux*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出 HyperNSDE：以超网络用静态患者表征条件化潜在 Neural SDE 的连续时间生成模型，联合建模静态协变量、不规则纵向轨迹与观测时间，用路径分解与 signature-kernel 非对抗目标稳定训练，在合成与真实临床数据上提升观测时间保真度并具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗机器学习面临数据稀缺与隐私约束，合成患者数据是潜在解决方案。真实合成患者级临床数据需同时建模三类紧耦合成分：异质静态协变量、不规则采样的纵向轨迹、信息性观测时间。现有方法很少同时处理这三者。

Method: HyperNSDE：连续时间生成模型，通过超网络将潜在 Neural SDE 条件化于静态患者表征，使基线特征不仅作为初始条件、而是持续塑造轨迹演化，且无需轨迹编码器；随机潜在动力学刻画生成路径的真实变异。观测时间由依赖潜在状态的强度过程联合建模。训练方面，用确定性-随机路径分解配合非对抗的 signature-kernel 目标，稳定不规则随机路径的训练。

Result: 在模拟和真实临床数据集上，观测时间保真度提升，整体性能具有竞争力；匹配网格分析表明，预测与相关性指标受观测网格规则性和轨迹平滑度影响。

Conclusion: HyperNSDE 可统一生成静态协变量、不规则轨迹与观测时间，无需轨迹编码器即可让基线特征影响轨迹演化，为隐私约束下的临床数据合成提供了有效方案；同时提示评估指标须考虑观测网格规则性与轨迹平滑度等混杂因素。

Abstract: Synthetic patient data generation is a promising solution to the dual challenge of data scarcity and privacy constraints in healthcare machine learning. Realistic synthesis of patient-level clinical data requires jointly modeling heterogeneous static covariates, irregularly sampled longitudinal trajectories, and informative observation times - three tightly coupled components in practice yet rarely addressed together. We propose HyperNSDE, a continuous-time generative model that conditions a latent Neural SDE on static patient representations through a hypernetwork, allowing baseline characteristics to shape trajectory evolution beyond the initial condition without requiring a trajectory encoder, while stochastic latent dynamics capture realistic variability in generated paths. Observation times are modeled jointly through a latent-state-dependent intensity process, and training on irregular stochastic paths is stabilized via a deterministic-stochastic path decomposition with a non-adversarial signature-kernel objective. Experiments on simulated and real clinical datasets show improved observation-time fidelity and competitive performance, while matched-grid analyses reveal that forecasting and correlation metrics are affected by observation-grid regularity and trajectory smoothness.

</details>


### [279] [Bayesian Optimization on Function Spaces via Sparse RKHS Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07417)
*Davide Sartor,Meghan E. Huber,Donghyun Kim,Nathan Wycoff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 L0MO 方法用于函数型贝叶斯优化，在 RKHS 的稀疏核函数子集中同时优化核位置与系数，并通过新基准测试验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化针对有限维向量输入，但实际中参数常源于对内在函数关系的离散化，因此需要函数型贝叶斯优化（FBO），即在无限维函数空间中优化。

Method: 提出 L0 流形优化（L0MO），在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中搜索具有核函数稀疏表示的函数子集，同时优化核位置及其系数，并统一梳理与现有方法的关系。

Result: 开展了大规模计算研究，并构建将标准有限维测试函数迁移到无限维域的新型基准测试函数集。

Conclusion: 实验表明，总体上 L0MO 在广泛的测试基准上取得了优于现有先进方法的性能。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) has become an established methodology for minimizing black-box functions of a vector input. Often, however, this parameter vector arises from the discretization of an inherently functional relationship. Several recent articles have considered the Functional Bayesian Optimization (FBO) setting, in which the variable to be optimized is not a member of a finite dimensional vector space, but rather an infinite dimensional function space. In this work, we propose $L^0$ Manifold Optimization (L0MO), a simple approach to FBO which searches the subset of a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS) consisting of functions with a sparse representation in the kernel functions, optimizing both the kernel locations and their coefficients. We discuss in detail the relationship between our method and existing ones, providing a unifying lens through which to view prior works. To assess our method against the state of the art, we conduct an extensive computational study, and along the way develop a novel set of benchmark test functions which port standard finite-dimensional ones to the infinite dimensional domain. Our experiments demonstrate that, on balance, the proposed method achieves superior performance across a wide range of test benchmarks.

</details>


### [280] [Two-Sample Testing for Random Graphs without Vertex Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07503)
*Soham Dan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究未对齐顶点的图两样本检验：ER零假设与保持期望度的植入双块差异下，每组需 m≍t^{-3} 张图（信噪比 t<1），有符号三角形数可达该速率，对齐时仅需 m≍t^{-1}，错位代价约 t^{-2}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个图群体常需在顶点无对应关系（未对齐）的情况下比较，如不同社区的网络的对比，或评估图生成模型与留出图。论文研究此类未对齐两样本检验需要多少图，以及哪些图统计量能检测哪些差异。

Method: 针对 Erdős–Rényi 零假设与不改变任何期望度的植入双块差异，分析所需图数 m 与信噪比 t 的关系；使用有符号三角形计数、4-环、树基统计量等作为检验统计量；在 graphon 极限下分析度分布与消息传递 GNN 特征所属的统计量类别；并给出每组一或两张图的精确有效检验，以及非恒定零假设下的 motif 检验。

Result: 每组 m≍t^{-3} 张图是必要且充分的，有符号三角形计数达到该速率，下界对所有图规模成立；对齐顶点时 m≍t^{-1} 即足够，错位代价为 t^{-2} 量级。三角形信号抵消时速率为 t^{-4}，需用 4-环。树基统计量在两假设下期望完全相同，有限个此类检验渐近无功效；graphon 极限下该类包含度分布与消息传递 GNN 特征。非恒定零假设下一般差异在一阶可见，简单 motif 检验达到对齐样本量阶，表明错位代价主要针对低阶不可见差异。模拟中拟合指数接近预测值，度基与随机 GNN 评估指标停留在其水平，而有符号三角形约需 65 张图。

Conclusion: 未对齐会显著增加图两样本检验所需样本量（因子约 t^{-2}），只有能捕捉高阶结构（如有符号三角形、4-环）的统计量才具功效；树基统计量与常见 GNN 特征在该类差异下失效，错位代价主要出现在低阶不可见的差异上。

Abstract: Two populations of graphs often have to be compared without any correspondence between their vertices, for instance when networks come from different communities, or when a graph generative model is evaluated against held-out graphs. We study how many graphs such an unaligned two-sample test needs, and which graph statistics can detect which differences. For an Erdős--Rényi null and a planted two-block difference that leaves every expected degree unchanged, we show that $m\asymp t^{-3}$ graphs per group are necessary and sufficient when the per-graph signal-to-noise ratio is $t<1$. Signed triangle counts attain this rate, and the lower bound holds for every graph size. With aligned vertices $m\asymp t^{-1}$ graphs suffice, so misalignment costs a factor of order $t^{-2}$. When the triangle signal cancels, the rate becomes $t^{-4}$ and $4$-cycles are needed. Statistics built from trees have exactly the same expectation under both hypotheses, and tests based on finitely many of them have asymptotically no power. In the graphon limit, this class includes degree distributions and message-passing graph neural network features. For a non-constant null, a generic difference is visible at first order, and a simple motif test attains the aligned order of sample size, suggesting that misalignment is costly mainly for differences that are invisible at low orders. We also give an exactly valid test for one or two graphs per group, at a cost in power. In our simulations, the fitted exponents are close to the predicted ones, and degree-based and random-GNN evaluation metrics stay at their level in a setting where signed triangles need about $65$ graphs.

</details>


### [281] [Is $\sqrt{d}$ Separation Necessary for Gradient EM to Learn Gaussian Mixtures in High Dimensions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07551)
*Yiran Zhang,Mo Zhou,Weihang Xu,Maryam Fazel,Simon S. Du*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究高维过参数化设置下梯度EM学习高斯混合模型的全局收敛性，证明真实分量间分离度必须随维度d增长，给出了Ω(d^{0.5-ε})的近乎最优最坏情况下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明，在过参数化且真实分量分离度按Ω(√d)增长时，梯度EM可全局收敛。作者希望探究这一维度依赖是否本质且不可避免，即是否存在更小的分离度仍可保证收敛。

Method: 考虑一种混合EM算法：混合权重使用标准EM更新，分量均值使用梯度EM更新。针对总体梯度EM（population gradient EM）分析，在随机初始化下研究其在下界框架中的收敛行为，通过构造最坏情况实例给出所需分离度的下界。

Result: 对任意ε>0，当维度足够大时，最坏情况下分离度仅为Ω(d^{0.5-ε})不足以保证总体梯度EM在次指数时间内全局收敛，即使在过参数化设置下也是如此。该下界与已知上界Ω(√d)几乎匹配。

Conclusion: 高维下梯度EM学习高斯混合所需的真实分量分离度存在维度依赖的近乎最优最坏情况下界，表明Ω(√d)这一量级在高维中不可回避，验证了现有上界在阶数上的紧致性。

Abstract: Learning Gaussian mixture models (GMMs) using the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm and its gradient-based variants is a fundamental problem in machine learning. It is known that randomly initialized (gradient) EM fails to learn multi-component GMMs in the exact-parameterized setting, where the number of components matches that of the ground-truth GMM. Recently, global convergence of gradient EM has been established in the over-parameterized setting, where more components are used, provided that the ground-truth components are well separated. In particular, the minimum separation between ground-truth components is required to scale as $Ω(\sqrt{d})$, where $d$ is the dimension. In this paper, we show that this dimensional dependence is unavoidable in high-dimensional settings. Specifically, we consider a hybrid EM algorithm that uses standard EM updates for the mixing weights and gradient EM updates for the component means. For any $ε> 0$, we prove that when the dimension is sufficiently large, in the worst case a separation of order $Ω(d^{0.5-ε})$ is insufficient to guarantee global convergence of population gradient EM in sub-exponential time under random initialization, even in the over-parameterized regime. Our result establishes an almost optimal worst-case lower bound on the ground-truth separation required for learning Gaussian mixtures via gradient EM in high dimensions.

</details>


### [282] [Explicit Asymptotic Bounds for Sequential Calibration Beyond $T^{2/3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07623)
*Eric Dai,Maxwell Fishelson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究二值结果的序列校准预测问题。作者提出"带复用的保号博弈"的两阶段递归标记策略，得到 $O(n^\alpha t^\beta)$ 界，并将保号上界到校准问题的归约改进为仅需 $O(\log T)$ 个博弈实例，从而首次给出显式低于 $2/3$ 的指数 $O(T^{0.662942288})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列预测的 $\ell_1$-校准误差累积界长期停留在 Foster-Vohra 提出的 $O(T^{2/3})$。Dagan 等人虽将指数 $2/3$ 降低了一个未指明的常数，但未给出显式指数。本文旨在获得显式且低于 $2/3$ 的界。

Method: 1) 针对"带复用的保号（sign-preservation-with-reuse）"博弈，提出两阶段递归标记策略，得到对所有空间与时间选择的 $O(n^\alpha t^\beta)$ 界；2) 改进 Dagan 等人的归约，将校准上界问题对保号博弈的等价性改为仅使用 $O(\log T)$ 个博弈实例；3) 结合两项改进并选取显式可行参数。

Result: 得到了序列校准的显式上界 $O(T^{0.662942288})$，这是序列校准问题中首个显式低于 $2/3$ 的指数。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段递归标记策略与改进归约的结合，本文突破了沿用二十余年的 $O(T^{2/3})$ 界，给出了显式更优的校准误差界。

Abstract: Probability forecasts are calibrated when predicted probabilities match empirical outcome frequencies: among events assigned a probability $p$, we'd hope that the fraction of positive outcomes is close to $p$. We study the problem of sequential forecasting of binary outcomes. The classical $O(T^{2/3})$ bound on expected cumulative $\ell_1$-calibration error established by Foster and Vohra stood for over two decades until Dagan et al. reduced the exponent $2/3$ by an unspecified constant.
  We establish a new two-phase recursive labeling strategy for the sign-preservation-with-reuse game that yields the bound $O(n^αt^β)$ for all choices of space and time. We then sharpen the reduction from upper bounds on sign preservation to calibration by modifying the equivalence of Dagan et al. to use only $O(\log T)$ instances of the sign-preservation-with-reuse game. This lets us establish an explicit bound of $O(T^{0.662942288})$, the first explicit exponent below $2/3$ for sequential calibration, by combining both improvements and choosing explicit feasible parameters.

</details>


### [283] [Uniform Discrete Diffusion Models are Minimax Optimal for Estimating Distributions with Small Effective Support Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07655)
*Dongsun Yoon,Saptarshi Chakraborty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为均匀离散扩散模型建立统计泛化理论，证明误差由有效支撑集大小$s_n(P_0)$控制，TV界为$O(\sqrt{s_n(P_0)/n})$且极小极大最优，KL界为$O(\frac{1}{n}s_n(P_0)\log(eK^d/s_n(P_0))\log n)$且至多差$\log n$因子，说明该模型可避免维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在文本、生物序列等离散乘积空间生成建模中已获成功，但其统计泛化性质理解不足。现有界只随环境空间$[K]^d$规模增长，无法刻画数据实际集中在极小子集（由语义或物理约束导致）的结构，导致近乎空洞的误差界。本文旨在填补均匀离散扩散的理论空白。

Method: 针对均匀离散扩散（与掩码扩散并列的两大范式之一），以$[K]^d$上未知数据分布$P_0$的$n$个i.i.d.样本为设定，引入样本量相关的分布复杂度度量——有效支撑集大小$s_n(P_0)$。通过选取合适的网络规模与超参数，推导期望TV损失与期望KL散度的上界，并建立相应的极小极大下界以验证最优性。

Result: 期望TV损失满足$O(\sqrt{s_n(P_0)/n})$；期望KL散度满足$O(\frac{1}{n}s_n(P_0)\log(eK^d/s_n(P_0))\log n)$。TV速率被证明为极小极大最优，KL速率在$\log n$因子内达到极小极大最优。

Conclusion: 均匀离散扩散能成功避免维度灾难：对有效支撑集小的分布，TV误差率仅通过$s_n(P_0)$依赖环境状态空间大小，KL速率仅额外多一个关于环境空间规模的对数依赖。上下界共同刻画了其统计效率。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have emerged as a practically successful framework for generative modeling on discrete product spaces, yet their statistical generalization properties remain poorly understood. Discrete real-world data such as text or biological sequences often concentrate on a small fraction of the astronomically large ambient space because of semantic or physical constraints, but existing bounds fail to capture this distributional structure and instead scale with the size of the ambient space, giving rise to almost vacuous error bounds. We address this gap for uniform discrete diffusion, one of the two dominant discrete diffusion paradigms alongside masking diffusion, by deriving statistical guarantees governed by the effective support size $s_n(P_0)$, a sample-size-dependent measure of distributional complexity. Given $n$ independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) samples from an unknown data distribution $P_0$ on $[K]^d$, we show that, with appropriate choices of network size and hyperparameters, the expected total variation (TV) loss scales as $O(\sqrt{s_n(P_0)/n})$, while the expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence is bounded by $O(\frac{1}{n}s_n(P_0)\log(eK^d/s_n(P_0))\log n)$. Furthermore, we show that the TV rate is minimax optimal and that the KL rate is minimax optimal up to a factor of $\log n$. Together, these upper and lower bounds show that uniform discrete diffusion successfully avoids the curse of dimensionality for distributions with small effective support size: the TV error rate depends on the ambient state-space size only through $s_n(P_0)$, while the corresponding KL rate incurs only an additional logarithmic dependence on the ambient state-space size.

</details>


### [284] [Stability of Measure-to-Measure Transformers on Sub-Gaussian Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07717)
*Frank Cole,Nicholas H. Nelsen,Takashi Furuya*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立变换器所定义的"测度到测度"算子的数学理论：证明其对亚高斯输入保持亚高斯性、依1-Wasserstein距离具Hölder连续性，并给出误差传播、交叉注意力平均场分析及逼近保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在经验上取得巨大成功，但其理论基础相对薄弱，缺乏关于其稳定性与有限样本性质的严格数学刻画。

Method: 将Transformer形式化为测度到测度的算子进行数学分析：证明其将亚高斯输入映为亚高斯输出（保证softmax算子任意长度复合良定义）；在适当的亚高斯输入空间上给出1-Wasserstein距离下的Hölder连续性；据此估计沿Transformer在亚高斯输入与其经验近似之间的误差传播；并研究交叉注意力机制的"平均场"类比（从一对概率测度到单个概率测度的算子）。

Result: Transformer对亚高斯输入保持亚高斯性且依1-Wasserstein距离Hölder连续；得到误差传播估计；发现交叉注意力在其两个输入变量上表现出不同的Hölder正则性与样本复杂度；并由此推出测度到测度Transformer的逼近保证。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同为亚高斯数据上的Transformer提供了坚实的稳定性与有限样本理论。

Abstract: Transformers have exhibited impressive empirical success across various domains, but their theoretical foundations remain less developed. This work constitutes a mathematical study of the measure-to-measure operators defined by transformers. We show that transformers map sub-Gaussian inputs to sub-Gaussian outputs; this ensures that taking arbitrary-length compositions of the softmax operator is well-defined. We then show that transformers are Hölder continuous with respect to the 1-Wasserstein distance on appropriate spaces of sub-Gaussian inputs. This allows us to establish estimates on the error propagation along a transformer between a sub-Gaussian input and its empirical approximation. We also study a mean-field analog of the cross-attention mechanism, which is an operator from a pair of probability measures to a single probability measure. We show that cross-attention exhibits different Hölder regularity and sample-complexity in its two input arguments. Last, we apply our results to deduce approximation guarantees for measure-to-measure transformers. Together, these results provide a firm stability and finite-sample theory for transformers on sub-Gaussian data.

</details>


### [285] [Nash Social Welfare for Multi Armed Bandits: Trajectory-wise Expected and High Probability Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07737)
*Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在Nash Social Welfare目标下研究公平多臂老虎机，提出轨迹级Nash遗憾与高概率Nash遗憾两种更强指标，并给出RR-NCB算法达到最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Nash遗憾对每轮边际期望取几何平均，忽略了跨轮奖励的联合分布，未在轨迹层面体现NSW公平动机，因此需要更忠实的指标。

Method: 提出轨迹级Nash遗憾（先对完整样本路径取几何平均再求期望）与高概率Nash遗憾；设计两阶段算法RR-NCB，结合轮询探索与Nash置信界索引策略。

Result: 理论上证明轨迹级Nash遗憾上界为Õ(√(k log T/T))，并以概率1-δ给出高概率遗憾上界Õ(√(k log(kT/δ)/T))，均匹配最优Õ(√(k/T))速率；通过AM-GM与标准极小极大下界论证最优性，仿真验证理论。

Conclusion: 在更强的轨迹级与高概率Nash遗憾指标下，RR-NCB仍达到最优速率，说明更强的公平度量不牺牲学习效率。

Abstract: We study fair multi-armed bandits under the Nash Social Welfare (NSW) objective, which measures performance via the geometric mean of accumulated rewards. Existing work defines Nash regret as $\mathrm{NR}_T = μ^\star - (\prod_{t=1}^T \mathbb{E}μ_{I_t})^{1/T}$, where $μ_{I_t}$ is the mean reward of the recommended arm $I_t$ and $T$ is the horizon. Since it applies the geometric mean to per-round marginal expectations, it ignores the joint distribution of rewards across rounds, leaving the NSW fairness motivation unaddressed at the trajectory level. We propose \emph{trajectory-wise Nash regret} $\widetilde{\mathrm{NR}}_T = μ^\star - \mathbb{E}[(\prod_{t=1}^T μ_{I_t})^{1/T}]$, which computes the geometric mean over complete sample paths before taking expectations, capturing NSW fairness more faithfully. By Jensen's inequality, $\widetilde{\mathrm{NR}}_T \geq \mathrm{NR}_T$, making it a strictly stronger metric. We also introduce \emph{high probability Nash regret} $\widehat{\mathrm{NR}}_T = μ^\star - (\prod_t μ_{I_t})^{1/T}$, giving the first high probability regret bounds in fair bandits. Our two-phase algorithm, Round Robin Nash Confidence Bound (\texttt{RR-NCB}), combines round robin exploration with a Nash confidence bound index policy. We show $\widetilde{\mathrm{NR}}_T \leq \widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{k\log T/T})$ and, with probability $1-δ$, $\widehat{\mathrm{NR}}_T \leq \widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{k\log(kT/δ)/T})$, matching the optimal $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{k/T})$ rate despite the stronger metrics. Optimality follows from a lower bound via AM-GM and standard $k$-armed bandit minimax arguments. Simulations validate our theory.

</details>


### [286] [High-dimensional online calibration from harmonic weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07740)
*Maxwell Fishelson,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究任意凸集 $Y\subseteq\mathbb{R}^d$ 上多维预测相对于任意误差范数的在线校准问题，提出首个对固定精度关于维度 $d$ 多项式时间的算法，达到 $d^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$ 轮校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多维在线校准算法的轮数对维度 $d$ 的依赖过高（如 $d^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)}$），在同时预测 $d$ 个二元结果或多分类场景下效率不足，因此需要降低维度依赖。

Method: 提出一种简单算法：每轮输出对历史结果进行调和平滑后的调和加权分布；该算法对所有预测集与范数通用，其轮数为 $\exp(O(\gamma(Y,L)/\varepsilon))$，其中 $\gamma(Y,L)$ 是由矩阵差异问题定义的几何参数。调和权重源于离散 Hilbert 变换矩阵在每 $L$ 下同时达到最优差异（至多差一个通用常数）。

Result: 对 $Y=[0,1]^d$ 的 $d$ 个二元结果同时预测，首次实现固定精度下关于 $d$ 的多项式轮数，需 $d^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$ 轮，指数级改进此前维度依赖；对多分类预测 $Y=Δ_d$ 得到相同 $d^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$ 速率，优于 Peng 与 Fishelson 等人的 $d^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)}$ 界。

Conclusion: 所提算法以调和加权分布实现通用在线校准，显著降低维度依赖，并揭示离散 Hilbert 变换矩阵的最优差异性质，该最优性结果可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: We study the online calibration of multidimensional forecasts over an arbitrary convex set $Y\subseteq\mathbb{R}^d$ relative to an arbitrary error norm $\|\cdot\|_{L}$. For forecasting $d$ binary outcomes simultaneously ($Y=[0,1]^d$), we give the first algorithm that achieves $\varepsilon$-calibration in a number of rounds that is polynomial in $d$ for every fixed accuracy. It requires $d^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$ rounds, exponentially improving the dimension dependence of previous bounds. For multi-class forecasting ($Y=Δ_d$), we obtain the same $d^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$ rate, improving the $d^{\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)}$ bounds of Peng and Fishelson et al.
  Our algorithm is simple: on each round, it outputs a harmonically weighted distribution over harmonically smoothed past outcomes. The same algorithm works for every forecast set and norm.
