Method: 在单位柱内考虑满足 Robin 型边界条件 $u_r(1,t)=u(1,t)$ 的正径向图。对已有经典区间证明高度与梯度界，并给出速度界到 $H$、$u_r/r$、$u_{rr}$ 估计的显式转换；推导与有限斜率 translator 比较时正确的边界缺陷项。在独立的内部速度界与双边斜率困陷假设下，证明紧性并识别所有平移极限为 cup translator；同时指出建立斜率困陷仍需的径向零点比较。方法覆盖静态帽与幂律 Robin 律。

Result: 得到 (i) 经典区间上的高度和梯度界；(ii) 速度界到几何量 $H$、$u_r/r$、$u_{rr}$ 的显式估计转换；(iii) 与有限斜率 translator 比较所需的边界缺陷；(iv) 在内部速度界与双边斜率困陷下，平移序列的紧性及极限唯一性（cup translator）；(v) 静态帽和幂律 Robin 律作为特例被纳入。

Conclusion: 文章搭建了 $V=H^α+b$ 径向图收敛分析的两层估计框架，并给出将归一化平移极限识别为 cup translator 的条件性结论；最终证明斜率困陷仍依赖于一个径向零点比较结果，这是后续需要补足的关键步骤。

Abstract: We study a positive radial graph evolving by $V=H^α+b$, with $α>0$ and $b<0$, in the unit cylinder and with $u_r(1,t)=u(1,t)$. We separate finite-time continuation estimates from the time-uniform interior estimates needed for convergence of normalized translates. On existing classical intervals we prove height and gradient bounds and an explicit conversion of velocity bounds into estimates for $H$, $u_r/r$, and $u_{rr}$. We derive the correct boundary defect for comparisons with finite-slope translators. Under independent interior velocity bounds and two-sided slope trapping, we prove compactness and identify every translated limit with the cup translator. We identify the radial zero-number comparison still required to establish that trapping. Stationary caps and power-law Robin laws are included.

</details>


### [330] [Strong Convergence of Continuous Median Filter Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08484)
*Fabius Krämer,Tim Laux*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出中值滤波器的连续时间版本用于图像去噪，在滤波器模板尺寸趋于零时证明其演化收敛于水平集平均曲率流，并首次对中值型滤波器方案证明了能量的收敛性，进而得到界面的单位重数这一关键几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中值型滤波器方案与水平集平均曲率流之间的联系此前缺乏严格的收敛性证明，尤其是能量的收敛通常只能作为假设出现在相关方案的收敛性证明中，缺少可证明的理论依据。作者希望建立这种联系并提供更强的收敛结果。

Method: 构造中值滤波器的连续时间对应物，对图像进行渐进去噪；在模板尺寸（stencil size）趋于零的极限下分析其演化行为，并采用受Evans与Spruck工作启发的补偿紧致性（compensated compactness）论证来完成证明。

Result: 证明该演化在模板尺寸趋于零时收敛于水平集平均曲率流；首次对任意中值型滤波器方案证明了该极限下能量的收敛性；该强收敛结果进一步给出界面的单位重数（unit multiplicity）这一重要几何信息。

Conclusion: 中值滤波器的连续时间版本与水平集平均曲率流之间存在严格的渐近联系，且能量收敛可以被证明而非仅作假设，这为相关滤波方案的收敛性分析提供了更坚实的理论基础和几何刻画。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-time counterpart of the median filter that gradually denoises a given image. In the limit of vanishing stencil size, our results show that the evolution converges to level-set mean curvature flow. Surprisingly, and for the first time for any median-type filter scheme, we can prove the convergence of energies in this limit. Such strong convergence results are of major interest as they often have to be assumed in the literature to prove convergence of related schemes. Moreover, this result carries crucial geometric information, namely the unit multiplicity of interfaces. The proof relies on a compensated compactness argument inspired by the work of Evans and Spruck.

</details>


### [331] [Nonlinear stability of 3D Couette flow on $\mathbb T\times\mathbb R^2$ under critical transverse low-frequency control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08288)
*Yubo Chen,Guoxu Yang,Jianbo Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明了三维 Couette 流在不可压 Navier–Stokes 方程下的非线性稳定性，在 $\mathbb T\times\mathbb R^2$ 上建立了粘性一致、$O(\nu)$ 阈值的全局 $H^2$ 稳定性结果，尽管缺少展向谱隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Couette 流稳定性的经典结果多依赖谱隙或增强耗散机制；在三维 $\mathbb T\times\mathbb R^2$ 中，展向缺乏谱隙，非零流向模态与零模态的相互作用带来 lift-up 线性增长，因此如何在无谱隙条件下保持 $O(\nu)$ 稳定性阈值是一个非平凡问题。

Method: 引入端点空间 $\dot B^{-2}_{2,\infty}$ 控制横向低频，结合该端点范数的传播估计、Fourier 分裂方法，以及两级 roll-to-streak 表示；对非零模采用 Wei–Zhang (CPAM, 2021) 框架并作适配。

Result: 散度自由初值满足 $\|u_{\rm in}\|_{H^2}+\|u^\perp_{0,\rm in}\|_{\dot B^{-2}_{2,\infty}}\leq c_*\nu$ 时，生成唯一全局 $H^2$ 解并保持接近 Couette 流；非零流向模增强耗散，其壁法向分量满足粘性一致时间积分无粘阻尼估计，横向零模以 $(1+\nu t)^{-1}$ 速率 $L^2$ 衰减。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使缺少展向谱隙，三维 Couette 流仍可在 $O(\nu)$ 阈值下实现非线性稳定；端点空间 $\dot B^{-2}_{2,\infty}$ 适用于控制横向低频、容纳散度形式非线性强迫，并对壁法向零模的线性 lift-up 起关键作用。

Abstract: We establish nonlinear stability of three-dimensional Couette flow for the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations on $\mathbb T\times\mathbb R^2$. Divergence-free perturbations satisfying $\|u_{\rm in}\|_{H^2}+\|u^\perp_{0,\rm in}\|_{\dot B^{-2}_{2,\infty}}\leq c_*ν$ generate unique global $H^2$ solutions that remain close to Couette flow, where $u^\perp_{0,\rm in}$ denotes the streamwise average of the transverse velocity. This result retains the $O(ν)$ stability threshold despite the absence of a spanwise spectral gap. The nonzero streamwise modes exhibit enhanced dissipation, their wall-normal component satisfies a viscosity-uniform time-integrated inviscid-damping estimate, and the transverse zero mode decays in $L^2$ at rate $(1+νt)^{-1}$. The endpoint space $\dot B^{-2}_{2,\infty}$ controls the transverse low frequencies, accommodates divergence-form nonlinear forcing, and is critical for linear lift-up from the wall-normal zero mode. The proof combines propagation of this endpoint norm, Fourier splitting, and a two-level roll-to-streak representation with an adaptation of the nonzero-mode framework of Wei--Zhang (CPAM, 2021).

</details>


### [332] [Regularity of Free Boundary for Monge-Ampère Obstacle Problem up to the Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08351)
*Hanfei Han,Genggeng Huang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究Monge-Ampère障碍问题自由边界在边界附近的正则性，重点探讨边值在接触点处的增长阶如何影响正则性，并发现二次增长边值下高维自由边界正则性优于二维的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于Monge-Ampère障碍问题自由边界正则性的研究多集中于内部，对直到边界的正则性以及边值增长阶的影响尚不清楚，作者希望刻画边值在接触点附近的增长阶与自由边界正则性之间的关系。

Method: 以Monge-Ampère障碍问题为对象，考察自由边界直到边界的正则性；通过分析边值在接触点附近的增长阶对自由边界行为的影响，并对不同维数（特别是二维与高维）进行比较研究。

Result: 发现边值在接触点附近的增长阶显著影响自由边界的正则性；并揭示在二次增长边值条件下，高维自由边界正则性优于二维，构成一个有趣的现象。

Conclusion: 边值增长阶是决定Monge-Ampère障碍问题自由边界正则性的关键因素，且自由边界正则性的维数效应（高维优于二维）在二次增长边值下表现明显。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the regularity of free boundaries for Monge-Ampère obstacle problem up to the boundary. We mainly focus on how the growth order of boundary value near the contact point impact the regularity. We also find an interesting phenomena that under quadratic growth boundary value, the free boundary enjoys better regularity in high dimensions than in two dimension.

</details>


### [333] [$L^p$ estimates for Dunkl--Schrödinger operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08371)
*Agnieszka Hejna-Łyżwa,Suman Mukherjee*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究 Dunkl–Schrödinger 算子 $L=-\Delta_k+V$ 在反向 Hölder 类势下的预解核衰减估计，并在反射不变势情形得到 $L^p(dw)$ 有界性、Riesz 变换及分数次势估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典 Schrödinger 算子理论中，Riesz 变换 $\\nabla L^{-1/2}$ 与 $VL^{-1}$ 的 $L^p$ 有界性已有深入结果，但在 Dunkl 框架下，由于反射群作用、轨道距离与齐次维数 $\\mathbf N$ 的引入，临界半径与核衰减结构发生变化，相关 $L^p$ 理论尚不完善。

Method: 在 $\\mathbb R^N$（$N>2$）上考察 Dunkl–Schrödinger 算子 $L=-\\Delta_k+V$，设 $V\\ge0$ 属于反向 Hölder 类 ${\\rm RH}^q(dw)$（$q>\\mathbf N/2$）。以临界半径和轨道距离刻画预解核的衰减；结合弱解的局部估计、Dunkl Fefferman–Phong 不等式，以及适配欧氏距离与轨道距离的核估计进行证明。

Result: 建立了预解核基于临界半径与轨道距离的衰减估计；对反射不变势，证明了 $VL^{-1}$、$L^{-1}$ 的二阶 Dunkl 导数以及 Riesz 变换 $\\nabla_kL^{-1/2}$ 的 $L^p(dw)$ 有界性，并给出分数次势估计与混合型估计。

Conclusion: 在 Dunkl 设定下，利用轨道距离与齐次维数可统一处理势为反向 Hölder 类的 Schrödinger 算子，所得 Riesz 变换与势乘子有界性推广了经典 Euclid 情形，并为反射不变势提供完整的 $L^p$ 理论框架。

Abstract: We study the Dunkl--Schrödinger operator $L=-Δ_k+V$ on $\mathbb R^N$, $N>2$, where $V$ is a nonnegative potential in the reverse Hölder class ${\rm RH}^q(dw)$, $q>\mathbf N/2$, and $\mathbf N$ is the homogeneous dimension. We establish decay estimates for the resolvent kernel in terms of the critical radius and the orbit distance. For reflection-invariant potentials, we obtain $L^p(dw)$ bounds for $VL^{-1}$, second-order Dunkl derivatives of $L^{-1}$, and the Riesz transform $\nabla_kL^{-1/2}$, together with fractional potential and mixed estimates. The proofs combine local estimates for weak solutions, the Dunkl Fefferman--Phong inequality, and kernel estimates adapted to the Euclidean and orbit distances.

</details>


### [334] [Extremal parameters, multiplicity and nonexistence for a singular elliptic problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08389)
*J. C. de Albuquerque,M. L. M. Carvalho,Edcarlos D. Silva,M. L. Silva*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一类带奇异项的椭圆问题，证明其非平凡非负弱解存在当且仅当参数λ超过临界值λ̅，且λ̅严格介于两个Rayleigh型参数之间，并在λ*左侧邻域内获得至少两个非平凡非负弱解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的能量泛函在H_0^1(Ω)中不是C^1的，标准临界点理论不适用，因此需要新的方法来刻画解的存在性与重数。

Method: 针对能量泛函非C^1的困难，引入并识别临界值λ̅，将其定位在两个Rayleigh型参数λ_*与λ*之间，并结合Nehari流形上的极小化及正能量的第二解构造进行分析。

Result: 证明了非平凡非负弱解存在当且仅当λ>λ̅，得到尖锐的存在阈值；并证明在λ*左侧邻域内至少存在两个不同的非平凡非负弱解，其中第一个为Nehari流形上的极小元且能量在λ*处变号，第二个具有严格正能量。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了尖锐的存在阈值与新的重数现象：当λ<λ*且充分接近λ*时，两解均具正能量，这与以往同问题中两解能量异号的多重性结果形成对比。

Abstract: We consider the following class of singular elliptic problems \[ \begin{cases} -Δu =
-\dfrac{u}{|u|^{β+1}}χ_{\{|u|>0\}} +λ|u|^{p-1}u, & \text{in }Ω,\\[1mm] u=0, & \text{on }\partialΩ, \end{cases} \] where $Ω\subset\mathbb R^N$ is a bounded smooth domain, $N\geq3$, $0<β,p<1$, and $λ>0$. The energy functional is not of class $C^1$ in $H_0^1(Ω)$, and the standard critical point theory does not apply. We prove that a nontrivial nonnegative weak solution exists if and only if $λ$ exceeds a critical value $\barλ$, which we identify and locate strictly between two Rayleigh-type parameters $0<λ_*<\barλ<λ^*<+\infty$. This gives a sharp existence threshold. We also prove that for every $λ$ in a neighborhood on the left of $λ^*$, there exist at least two distinct nontrivial nonnegative weak solutions. The first is a minimizer on the Nehari manifold, with an energy changing sign at $λ^*$. The second has a strictly positive energy. For $λ<λ^*$ sufficiently close to $λ^*$, both solutions have positive energy. This contrasts with previous multiplicity results for the same problem, in which the two solutions obtained have energies of opposite signs.

</details>


### [335] [Shape optimisation of the maximum of the gradient of the torsion function on convex sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08408)
*Michiel van den Berg,Dorin Bucur,Mickael Nahon*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究凸区域上torsion函数梯度的边界行为，证明其L∞范数在边界点取得，并在体积或周长约束下给出了最大化问题的C^1光滑、旋转对称最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解凸区域内torsion函数w_Ω梯度的边界行为，并探究在几何或变分约束下最大化‖∇w_Ω‖∞的极值域的形状与正则性。

Method: 分析凸域上torsion函数梯度的边界行为，证明其L∞范数在边界点取得并具有凸扰动下的稳定性；随后对以体积或周长为约束G(Ω)=m的极大化问题构造并分析极值域，证明其光滑性与对称性；此外简要讨论直径、外接圆半径、torsion及第一Dirichlet特征值等约束。

Result: 证明了‖∇w_Ω‖∞在边界点取得且在一般凸扰动下稳定；对于体积或周长约束的极大化问题，证明存在C^1光滑、关于某轴旋转对称、并在边界中包含一个(d-1)维球（其大小可估计）的最大化区域；对其他几何与变分约束刻画了其特征行为。

Conclusion: 凸区域内torsion函数梯度的L∞范数具有边界极值点与稳定性，且相应极大化问题的最优凸域具有良好的正则性与旋转对称结构，揭示了约束类型对极值域形状的关键影响。

Abstract: We analyse the boundary behaviour of the gradient of the torsion function $w_\Om$ in convex domains $\Om\subset\mathbb{R}^d$. We prove that its $L^\infty$-norm is attained at a boundary point and establish the stability of this norm under general (convex) perturbations. For problems of the form $$\sup\{\|\nabla w_{\Om}\|_\infty: \Om \subset \R^d,\textup{open, bounded and convex}, G(\Om)=m\},$$ where $G$ is either volume or perimeter, we show the existence of maximisers which are $C^1$-smooth, rotationally symmetric about an axis and contain a $(d-1)$- dimensional ball (of estimated size) in their boundary. Other constraints, whether geometric (such as diameter or circumradius) or variational (such as torsion or first Dirichlet eigenvalue), are also briefly discussed, and some of their characteristic behaviours are highlighted.

</details>


### [336] [Instantaneous regularisation via Harnack-type methods for a degenerate non-local equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08434)
*Simon M. Schulz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类退化非局部演化方程（源于活性布朗粒子系统的多粒子极限）具有瞬时光滑效应。核心机制是为某个平均量建立Harnack型不等式，从而证明方程在正时刻变为强抛物型并自正则化。相比前人工作，本文完全去除了对初值的非退化假设，并借助Bombieri-Giusti型方法给出了该平均量的定量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所研究方程是退化的非局部演化方程，其正则性行为并不显然。此前工作虽然已得到部分光滑性结果，但需额外假设初值满足（弱）非退化条件，这限制了结果的适用范围。本文的动机在于彻底去除这些附加假设，在更一般的初值条件下建立瞬时光滑效应，并给出定量刻画。

Method: 方法上，针对一个平均量推导Harnack型不等式，据此证明方程在正时刻变为强抛物型，实现自正则化。同时采用à la Bombieri-Giusti的迭代/覆盖论证方法，在完成正则性bootstrap的基础上，给出该平均量的定量估计。

Result: 主要结果是证明了退化非局部演化方程的瞬时光滑效应：方程对任意正时刻立即变为强抛物型并具有自正则性。该结论不再需要任何初值非退化假设，是对此前工作的显著改进。此外，还给出了相关平均量的定量估计。

Conclusion: 本文在更一般（无初值非退化假设）的条件下建立并量化了该类退化非局部方程的正则化机制，推广并强化了已有结果，为研究活性物质多粒子极限方程的适定性与长时间行为提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We prove an instantaneous smoothing effect for a degenerate non-local evolution equation (originally derived as the many-particle limit of a system of active Brownian particles). The underlying mechanism is that, by deriving a Harnack-type inequality for an averaged quantity, we are able to show that the equation becomes strongly parabolic for positive times, and thus self-regularising. This paper is a significant improvement of a previous work, where additional (weak) non-degeneracy assumptions were imposed on the initial data. Herein, we completely remove these assumptions, and prove (in addition to the regularity bootstrap) quantitative estimates on the aforementioned averaged quantity by means of an approach à la Bombieri-Giusti.

</details>


### [337] [Hessian Estimates for Convex Solutions of Hessian Quotient Equations via Compactness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08442)
*Jianxiang Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文用紧性方法重新证明了凸解在两类 Hessian 商方程下的内部 Hessian 估计，复现了 Lu 与 Fung 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lu 与 Fung 曾给出相关内部 Hessian 估计，本文旨在通过紧性论证提供更简洁的重新证明。

Method: 采用紧性（compactness）证明，针对方程 σ_n(D^2u)/σ_{n-2}(D^2u)=1 及 σ_n(D^2u)/σ_{n-1}(D^2u)=f(x,u,Du)>0（后者要求 1/f 关于梯度变量凹）建立凸解的内部 Hessian 估计。

Result: 给出了上述两类 Hessian 商方程凸解的内部 Hessian 估计的紧性证明，得到与 Lu、Fung 一致的估计结果。

Conclusion: 紧性方法可有效处理此类 Hessian 商方程的内部正则性估计。

Abstract: We give a compactness proof of interior Hessian estimates for convex solutions of $σ_n(D^2u)/σ_{n-2}(D^2u)=1$ and $σ_n(D^2u)/σ_{n-1}(D^2u)=f(x,u,Du)>0$, with $1/f$ concave in the gradient variable in the latter case, revisiting results of Lu and Fung.

</details>


### [338] [Global minimality of lamellae for isotropic Ohta--Kawasaki and Yukawa energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08485)
*Dario Reggiani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在维度 $2\le d\le5$、$m=0$ 及周期/零通量 Neumann 边界条件下，为尖锐界面 Ohta–Kawasaki 能量及其 Yukawa 屏蔽对应物建立了尖锐比较原理，并在最优条纹宽度与区域相容时证明了周期层状结构的全局极小性并刻画了所有极小元，同时指出 $d\ge6$ 时该测度构造的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖锐界面 Ohta–Kawasaki 能量及相关非局部泛函的极小化问题中，周期层状（条纹）结构是否全局最优以及极小元的唯一性/分类长期缺乏在一般维度下的尖锐判据。作者希望建立一个统一的比较原理，在最优条纹宽度与区域相容的情形下给出全局极小性的完整刻画，并厘清维度对证明方法的限制。

Method: 证明结合了三个核心工具：(1) 一个正的径向比较测度（positive radial comparison measure）；(2) 一个尖锐的 Fourier 界（sharp Fourier bound）；(3) $BV$ 中一类方向平移不等式的刚性（rigidity for a directional translation inequality）。作者同时分析了该比较测度构造在更高维度的可行性。

Result: 在 $2\le d\le5$、$m=0$ 以及周期和零通量 Neumann 边界条件下，当有限的最优条纹宽度与区域相容时，证明了周期层状结构的全局极小性，并对所有极小元进行了完整刻画。此外，识别出在 $d\ge6$ 时上述比较测度构造存在障碍。

Conclusion: 该工作为尖锐界面 Ohta–Kawasaki 型能量提供了一个在 $d\le5$ 时有效的尖锐比较/极小性框架，确立了周期层状结构在相容区域中的全局最优地位并刻画了极小元；同时表明该测度方法在高维（$d\ge6$）失效，提示高维情形需要新的思路。

Abstract: We establish a sharp comparison principle for the sharp-interface Ohta--Kawasaki energy and its Yukawa-screened counterpart in dimensions $2\le d\le5$, for $m=0$, and under periodic and zero-flux Neumann boundary conditions. Whenever the finite optimal stripe width is compatible with the domain, we prove global minimality of periodic lamellae and characterize all minimizers. The proof combines a positive radial comparison measure, a sharp Fourier bound, and rigidity for a directional translation inequality in $BV$. We also identify an obstruction to this measure construction in dimensions $d\ge6$.

</details>


### [339] [Stable Rational Approximation of PDE Transfer Functions with $H_\infty$ Error Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08497)
*Aleksandr Talitckii,Matthew M. Peet*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将 Krylov 子空间模型降阶方法推广到由偏积分方程（PIE，PDE 的等价表示）描述的无穷维系统，通过状态变换构造可控性矩阵并将 PIE 状态投影到截断可控性矩阵张成的空间，得到有理逼近，并在指数稳定性与适定性条件下给出可构造的有限频率 $H_\infty$ 误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程的传递函数是无理函数且难以获得，针对无理传递函数的控制与分析方法很少；为进行鲁棒分析与控制，需要有理逼近，并最好具备可证明的 $H_\infty$ 误差界，因此需要发展适用于 PIE 表示的无穷维系统的模型降阶方法。

Method: 首先提出 PIE 系统的一种状态变换，使得可以计算可控性矩阵；然后将 PIE 状态投影到由截断可控性矩阵张成的向量空间以获得有理逼近；最后证明 PIE 系统指数稳定性与适定性的充分条件可保证降阶系统的指数稳定性，并给出可构造的有限频率 $H_\infty$ 误差界。

Result: 所提出的降阶系统在给定充分条件下保持指数稳定性，并获得可构造的有限频率 $H_\infty$ 误差界；通过四个数值算例验证了方法的性能。

Conclusion: 将 Krylov 子空间模型降阶扩展至 PIE 描述的无穷维系统是可行的，能够在保证指数稳定性并提供 $H_\infty$ 误差界的前提下实现有理逼近。

Abstract: Transfer functions of Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) are irrational and difficult to obtain. Unlike for rational transfer functions, only a few methods are available for the control and analysis of irrational transfer functions. Thus, for robust analysis and control of PDEs, we need to use a rational approximation of the transfer function, preferably with provable $H_\infty$ error bounds. In this paper, we extend Krylov subspace model reduction to infinite-dimensional systems modeled by Partial Integral Equations (PIEs) -- an equivalent representation of PDEs. First, we propose a state transformation of the PIE system that allows us to compute a controllability matrix. Then, to obtain a rational approximation, we project the PIE states onto the vector space spanned by the truncated controllability matrix. Next, we show that sufficient conditions for exponential stability and well-posedness of the PIE system guarantee the exponential stability of the reduced-order system and provide constructive finite-frequency $H_\infty$ error bounds. Finally, we provide four illustrative numerical examples to demonstrate the performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [340] [Sheeting property and rigidity for stable solutions to the Allen--Cahn equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08499)
*Yong Liu,Tianci Luo,Kelei Wang,Juncheng Wei,Yong Wei,Ke Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明 Allen–Cahn 方程稳定解的叶状结构定理（维数 2–10），并得到整解一维性、blowdown 重数为一等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Allen–Cahn 方程稳定解的正则性与分类问题，特别是能量增长与 Morse 指标约束下解的对称性/一维性。

Method: 利用测度 $\varepsilon|Du_\varepsilon|^2\,dX$ 弱收敛到具有整数重数的超平面这一条件，建立 $\varepsilon$-尺度下零点集的叶状结构（sheeting）定理。

Result: 弱收敛到整数重数超平面推出零点集为互不相交的光滑图；$\mathbb R^N$（$N\le7$）中具 $O(R^{N-1})$ 能量增长的稳定整解是一维的；当 $W''(-1)=W''(1)$ 时，$4\le N\le10$ 下有限 Morse 指标且该能量增长的整解之 blowdown 重数为1；偶位势情形下，维数3–7的闭流形上（bumpiness或正Ricci曲率条件下）有界能量与Morse指标的极限重数为1。

Conclusion: 稳定解的叶状结构为 Allen–Cahn 方程在中等维数下的分类提供了统一框架，蕴含一维性与重数为一的刚性结果。

Abstract: We prove a sheeting theorem for stable solutions to the Allen--Cahn equation in ambient dimensions $2$ through $10$. We show that weak convergence of the measures $\varepsilon|Du_\varepsilon|^2\,dX$ to a hyperplane with integer multiplicity implies that the zero sets are disjoint smooth graphs. As a consequence, stable entire solutions in $\mathbb R^N$, $N\le7$, with $O(R^{N-1})$ energy growth are one-dimensional. When $W''(-1)=W''(1)$, every blowdown of an entire solution with finite Morse index and this energy growth has multiplicity one for $4\le N\le10$. For even potentials, we also obtain multiplicity one for limits with bounded energy and Morse index on closed manifolds of dimensions $3$ through $7$, under bumpiness or positive Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [341] [Large-volume fractional isoperimetry on products of closed manifolds and Euclidean spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08556)
*Juneyoung Seo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了流形笛卡尔积上的分数阶周长，证明了当其半径与底流形第一特征值的平方根之积足够大时，直柱体是唯一的最小值集，并给出了稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶周长（如由热半群定义的非局部周长）在度量测度空间中广泛出现，但其临界点集的唯一性与稳定性理论尚不完备。作者希望将非局部周长中的最小化问题推广到流形M与欧氏空间R^k的乘积上，理解底流形的几何（尤其是其谱）如何影响最优形状，并寻求分数阶周长下类似的唯一性与稳定性结果。

Method: 在M×R^k上通过热半群定义分数阶周长P_s。证明策略结合了三方面：首先对M分量做谱分解，将问题约化到欧氏片上的有效泛函；其次对欧氏切片进行重排（对称化）以减少周长；最后对能量中的"相互作用增益"与"凹性损失"进行单变量比较分析。通过比较直柱体M×B_R与其他等体积集，得到使直柱体最小化的阈值条件。

Result: 证明若R√λ₁(M)至少为仅依赖于k和s的显式阈值，则直柱体M×B_R（B_R为R^k中的球）在所有等体积可测集中唯一最小化P_s，至多相差R^k中的平移与零测集。在更大的阈值下，周长的亏量同时控制了切片体积与均值的偏差以及相对直柱体的非对称性的平方。对于平坦环面，这给出周期分数阶周长大体积下直柱体的唯一性，包括任意维数中的单周期集。

Conclusion: 底流形M的谱间隙（λ₁(M)）与柱体半径的乘积决定了分数阶周长最小化集的形态：当该乘积超过阈值时，直柱体是唯一最优解，且几何偏差受周长亏量定量控制。该结果将分数阶周长稳定性理论从欧氏空间推广到乘积结构，并为平坦环面上的周期问题提供了新的唯一性结论。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a closed connected Riemannian manifold and let $\mathcal P_s$ be the fractional perimeter on $M\times\mathbb R^k$ defined through its heat semigroup, where $k\ge1$ and $0<s<1$. We prove that the straight cylinder $M\times B_R$, with $B_R\subset\mathbb R^k$ a ball of radius $R$, uniquely minimizes $\mathcal P_s$ among all measurable sets of the same volume, up to translations in $\mathbb R^k$ and null sets, whenever $R\sqrt{λ_1(M)}$ is at least an explicit threshold depending only on $k$ and $s$. Here $λ_1(M)$ is the first positive eigenvalue of the nonnegative Laplace--Beltrami operator $-Δ_M$ on $M$. Under a larger threshold, the perimeter deficit controls both the deviation of the slice volumes from their mean and the square of the asymmetry relative to straight cylinders. For flat tori this gives large-volume uniqueness of straight cylinders for the periodic fractional perimeter, including singly periodic sets in every ambient dimension. The proof combines spectral reduction on $M$ with rearrangement of the Euclidean slices and a one-variable comparison between an interaction gain and a concavity loss.

</details>


### [342] [Relative isoperimetry in the three-dimensional cube: classification and quantitative stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08644)
*Bin Deng,Jiahuan Li,Yilu Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 确定三维单位立方体完整相对等周轮廓，分类极小集（角八分球、棱四分之一圆柱、坐标平板），转变体积为 $4\pi/81$ 与 $1/\pi$；证明周长亏损控制到极小族的平方 $L^1$ 距离（最优指数 2），并解决 Milman 猜想与 Brezis 开问题三维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对等周问题中极小集的结构分类、稳定性与定量下界尚不完整，尤其三维立方体在转变体积附近的退化行为，以及由此关联的 Milman 猜想与 Brezis 开问题亟待解决。

Method: 通过确定三维单位立方体的完整相对等周轮廓，并对所有极小集进行完全分类（含两处转变体积的等号情形）；随后建立周长亏损与平方 $L^1$ 距离之间的最优指数定量估计，在转变附近给出统一估计并刻画最优常数的线性退化。

Result: 极小集（至立方体等距与补集）为角八分球、棱四分之一圆柱与坐标平板，转变体积为 $4\pi/81$ 与 $1/\pi$；周长亏损以最优指数 2 控制到极小族的平方 $L^1$ 距离；转变附近统一估计结合相邻相的距离与超额周长；据此解决 Milman 猜想（$0<\beta\le1$ 的长方体）及 Brezis 开问题 10.1 的三维情形。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了三维立方体的相对等周轮廓与极小集，建立了最优指数的稳定性不等式，并在转变处揭示最优常数的线性退化，从而一并解决 Milman 猜想与 Brezis 开问题三维情形。

Abstract: We determine the complete relative isoperimetric profile of the three-dimensional unit cube and classify all minimizing sets, including the equality cases at both transition volumes. Up to cube isometries and complementation, the minimizers are corner eighth-balls, edge quarter-cylinders, and coordinate slabs, with transitions at $4π/81$ and $1/π$. Building on this classification, we prove that the perimeter deficit at each fixed volume controls the squared $L^1$ distance to the full minimizing family, with optimal exponent two. Near either transition, a uniform estimate combines the distance to each adjacent phase with its excess perimeter and identifies the linear degeneration, at neighboring volumes, of the best constant for distance to the actual minimizers. As consequences, we resolve the isoperimetric conjecture in Milman's formulation (Conjecture~1.1) for all cuboids $(0,β)\times(0,1)^2$, $0<β\leq1$, and the three-dimensional case of Brezis's Open Problem~10.1.

</details>


### [343] [Global large-data solutions for the BGK equation with physical collision frequency in a slab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08658)
*Young-Pil Choi,Sihyun Song*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明了在平板区域中，碰撞频率由局部密度给出的BGK方程在一般大初值下的全局重整化解存在性，允许入流与Maxwell边界条件，仅要求有限质量、动能、熵及相应入流通量界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果多为微扰性或需要点态有界、正下界、高阶矩等额外假设。本文旨在建立非微扰的全局大初值存在性理论，覆盖更弱、更物理的初值假设，包括密度相关碰撞频率的真实BGK情形。

Method: 核心困难在于能量紧性与未重整化碰撞算子的可积性同时丧失：有限能量不能保证第二速度矩的一致可积性，而密度依赖碰撞频率阻碍增益与损失项的局部L¹独立控制。作者通过截断碰撞频率一致的补偿估计、由数据确定的de la Vallée Poussin权重以及两次利用熵产的逐步重整化来克服。

Result: 获得宏观能量、局部Maxwellian及非线性碰撞项的紧性，从而得到全局重整化解，且在整个论证中保持对碰撞频率截断的一致性。

Conclusion: 这是该类初边值问题在上述一般假设下的首个非微扰全局存在性结果，拓展了BGK方程的理论适用范围，并为处理密度依赖碰撞频率下的紧性缺失提供了可复用的技术框架。

Abstract: We establish a global large-data existence theory for the Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook equation in a slab with the physical collision frequency given by the local density. We consider both inflow and Maxwell boundary conditions and assume only finite mass, kinetic energy, and entropy of the initial data, together with the corresponding inflow flux bounds. The data need not be small or pointwise bounded, and we impose neither positive lower bounds on the density or temperature nor prescribed velocity moments above order two. To our knowledge, this is the first nonperturbative global existence result for this initial and boundary value problem under these assumptions. The central obstacle is the simultaneous loss of energy compactness and integrability of the unrenormalized collision operator. Finite energy alone does not give uniform integrability of the second velocity moment, while the density dependent collision frequency prevents separate local $L^1$ control of the gain and loss terms. We overcome these difficulties through a compensated estimate uniform in the truncation of the collision frequency, a de la Vallée Poussin weight determined by the data, and two successive renormalizations using entropy production. This yields the compactness of the macroscopic energy, local Maxwellians, and nonlinear collision term, and hence global renormalized solutions.

</details>


### [344] [A nonlinear rod linkage model: zero-energy chains and continuum consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08705)
*Dennis Kriventsov,Philippe G. LeFloch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究一维刚性杆-弹性弹簧离散拉格朗日链模型：刻画零能约束几何，区分spinner与flipper两种机制；在graph-type假设下证明重标度后的零能链收敛到某自治ODE轨迹；对物理链接给出交替选择准则，使spinner机制下链条形成唯一的单调异宿族；flipper机制下确定约束集拓扑与后继数目，但全局分支选择仍开放；推导单/双组分连续残差展开，区分相容性与收敛性；数值实验验证所选spinner递推及其渐近乘子，并在简化单组分光滑周期数据模型上分别恢复一阶与二阶误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机在于理解由刚性杆与弹性弹簧构成的离散链在零能约束下的渐近行为，并建立离散模型与连续自治ODE之间的联系，从而对spinner/flipper两类机制给出几何与动力学刻画，为物理链接中的分支选择问题提供判据。

Method: 方法包括：建立离散拉格朗日链模型；分析零能约束集的几何结构；引入graph-type假设并对链条重标度以证明其收敛到自治ODE轨迹；针对物理链接提出交替选择准则；推导单组分与双组分连续残差展开；开展可复现数值实验检验spinner递推与渐近乘子。

Result: 主要结果：在graph-type假设下，选取的零能链经重标度后收敛到自治ODE轨迹；spinner机制下交替选择准则使链条形成唯一单调异宿族；flipper机制下确定约束集拓扑及后继数量，但全局分支选择未解决；单/双组分残差展开区分了相容性与时间依赖解的收敛；数值实验验证spinner递推与渐近乘子，并在简化单组分模型中恢复一阶、二阶误差。

Conclusion: 结论：该工作系统刻画了该离散链模型的零能约束几何与两类机制，建立了离散到连续的收敛框架，并在spinner机制中给出唯一异宿分支选择；flipper机制的分支选择仍待研究，数值结果支持理论并验证残差阶数。

Abstract: We study a discrete Lagrangian model for a one-dimensional chain of rigid rods coupled by elastic springs. We characterize the geometry of its zero-energy constraint and distinguish the spinner and flipper regimes. Under graph-type hypotheses, selected zero-energy chains converge, after rescaling, to trajectories of an autonomous ordinary differential equation. For the physical linkage, we identify a simple alternating selection criterion under which chains form a unique monotone heteroclinic family in the spinner regime. In the flipper regime, we determine the topology of the constraint set and the number of successors, but leave global branch selection open. We also derive one- and two-component continuum residual expansions, distinguishing consistency from convergence of time-dependent solutions. Reproducible numerical experiments test the selected spinner recurrence and its asymptotic multiplier; for a simplified one-component model with smooth periodic data, they recover first- and second-order discrepancies for the leading and corrected continuum equations, respectively.

</details>


### [345] [Normalised Hamiltonian Elliptic Systems: a Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality for bilinear mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08774)
*Daniele Cassani,Giulio Romani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带给定双线性质量 ∫uv=a>0 的哈密顿椭圆系统，在缩放不变的份量有界类上建立交叉梯度配对与双线性质量形式的 Gagliardo–Nirenberg 不等式，刻画临界曲线与能量三分法；对幂次非线性得到正解的唯一性（相差公共平移）与非退化性，并用精确缩放分类正规范化解、确定唯一临界质量。二维情形建立双线性指数型 Gagliardo–Nirenberg 估计（含尖锐梯度阈值）与截断标量精细化（最优四次主系数），结合质量归一商、负纤维约化与集中阈值下的紧性，对纯指数非线性在每个低于三次极限质量的规定质量处得到最小能量正径向解，并给出一阶扰动分支与小频率指数分支的显式质量响应公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究哈密顿椭圆系统在给定双线性质量约束下的变分结构与解的分类问题；核心动机是在缩放不变的份量有界类上刻画最优嵌入（临界曲线与能量三分法），并借此解决正规范化解的存在性、唯一性与临界质量识别问题，同时把二维指数型非线性情形纳入统一框架。

Method: 在缩放不变的分量有界类上建立交叉梯度配对与双线性质量形式的 Gagliardo–Nirenberg 不等式，确定临界曲线 1/p+1/q=N/(N+2) 及受限能量三分法；对幂次非线性用精确缩放做分类并证明唯一性与非退化性；二维情形发展带尖锐梯度阈值的双线性指数估计与截断标量精细化；借鉴 Cassani–Tarsi 的变分思想，结合质量归一化商、沿全部负纤维的约化以及集中阈值以下的紧性论证。

Result: 得到临界曲线 1/p+1/q=N/(N+2) 与受限能量三分法；幂次非线性下正 profile 的唯一性（相差公共平移）与非退化性在整个 Sobolev 次临界双曲线上成立；通过精确缩放分类了正规范化解并识别唯一临界质量；二维情形获得带尖锐梯度阈值的双线性指数 Gagliardo–Nirenberg 估计与最优四次主系数的截断标量精细化；对纯指数非线性在每个低于三次极限质量的规定质量处存在最小能量正径向解，并给出局部扰动分支与小频率指数分支的显式质量响应公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为带双线性质量约束的哈密顿椭圆系统提供了统一的 Gagliardo–Nirenberg 框架与变分存在性理论，明确了临界曲线、能量三分法与唯一临界质量，并将结论从幂次非线性推广到二维指数非线性，给出最小能量正径向解及显式质量响应。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian elliptic systems with prescribed bilinear mass $\int_{\mathbb{R}^N}uv=a>0$. We establish a Gagliardo--Nirenberg inequality in the crossed gradient pairing and bilinear mass, on scaling-invariant component-bounded classes. It identifies the critical curve $1/p+1/q=N/(N+2)$ and the restricted energy trichotomy. For power nonlinearities, we establish uniqueness up to common translations and nondegeneracy of positive profiles throughout the Sobolev-subcritical hyperbola. Exact scaling then classifies positive normalised solutions and identifies the unique critical mass. In dimension two, we develop a bilinear exponential Gagliardo--Nirenberg estimate with a sharp gradient threshold and a truncated scalar refinement with the optimal quartic leading coefficient. Adapting variational ideas from [Cassani-Tarsi, Calc.Var.PDE (2015)] we combine a mass-normalising quotient, reduction over the full negative fibres and compactness below the concentration threshold to obtain least-energy positive radial solutions for pure exponential nonlinearities at every prescribed mass below the cubic limiting mass. Local perturbative branches and a small-frequency exponential branch yield explicit mass-response formulas.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [346] [A numerical and efficient model for thermo-field electron emission calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07013)
*Salvador Barranco Cárceles,Andreas Kyritsakis,Anthony Ayari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: GETELEC-3：开源代码，用一维定态薛定谔方程直接数值求解替代Fowler-Nordheim、Murphy-Good等解析式，实现高效、高精度的热场电子发射计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子源尺寸缩小、采用新材料或追求更高性能时，热场电子发射的解析公式（Fowler-Nordheim、Murphy-Good及其基于JWKB的扩展）常被用于其推导条件之外的范围，导致精度不足，因此需要更严格的数值求解方法。

Method: 开发开源代码GETELEC-3，直接数值求解一维定态薛定谔方程（1D-TISE）；实现多种求解算法以比较速度、鲁棒性和解的不确定度；采用Noumerov算法并配合并行推进所有电子能量的优化内核；进一步训练神经网络，对半经典参考解对数残差建模以加速复杂势垒计算。

Result: Noumerov算法最快，解的不确定度低于1%；优化内核使电流密度计算耗时降至几毫秒，与JWKB评估相当；神经网络方法在极低计算成本下重现电流密度误差优于0.4%；代码支持金属与半导体，接受任意势和态密度。

Conclusion: GETELEC-3通过直接数值求解替代传统解析公式，兼顾速度与精度，并提供友好界面和可执行文件，推动严格电子发射计算与数据分析的普及。

Abstract: As electron sources are reduced in size, fabricated in new materials, or pushed for performance, the analytical formulations for thermo-field electron emission, namely Fowler-Nordheim and Murphy-Good equations and their JWKB-based extensions, are increasingly applied out of the range of conditions for which they were derived. We present GETELEC-3, an open-source code that replaces them with a direct numerical solution of the one-dimensional time-independent Schrödinger equation (1D-TISE). Several methods to solve the 1D-TISE have been implemented to test for speed, robustness, and solution's uncertainty. The Noumerov algorithm is found to be quickest, with the solution uncertainty below 1%, and together with an optimised kernel that advances all electron energies in parallel, the cost of calculating the current density is reduced to a few milliseconds - comparable to the JWKB evaluation. To further boost the calculation speed, especially in complicated barriers, a neural network has been trained on the residual of the logarithm of the semiclassical reference and it reproduces the current density better than 0.4% at a fraction of the computational cost. The code is developed for metals and semiconductors, accepts arbitrary potentials and density of states, and comes with a user-friendly interface and compiled executable to democratise rigorous electron emission calculations and data analysis.

</details>


### [347] [Phase diagram and transition properties of the Blume-Capel model: A Monte Carlo study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07567)
*Tenglong Li,Zhigang Feng,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 采用Monte Carlo方法研究Jx-Jy Blume-Capel模型（含/不含外磁场），验证三临界点位置、考察重入行为并研究磁热性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对特殊设计的Jx-Jy Blume-Capel模型，需明确其三临界点位置、是否存在重入行为，并系统研究其磁热效应，以补充该模型相图与热力学性质的认知。

Method: 使用Monte Carlo模拟，分别在无磁场与有外磁场条件下研究该模型；结合平均场理论（借鉴spin-1旋量玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚框架）解释所观察到的混合态现象，并分析磁热性质。

Result: 无磁场时临界温度低于常规反铁磁正方格子的结果；加磁场后在混合态区观察到一维自发对称破缺相迹象，并伴随次级比热峰。确认重入行为仅在固定温度、改变磁场时出现，未发现温度依赖的重入行为。磁热分析显示所考察相变点区域内无一级相变证据，逆磁热效应与正磁热效应均存在且与混合态相关，但相关相变均为二级。

Conclusion: 该Jx-Jy Blume-Capel模型的相变在本研究范围内均为二级；重入行为仅表现为磁场驱动的形式；混合态中出现的反常一维自发对称破缺相可通过平均场理论予以解释。

Abstract: The Jx-Jy Blume-Capel model, with and without an external magnetic field, is investigated using the Monte Carlo method. Our aim is to verify the location of the tricritical point, examine the existence of reentrant behavior, and study the magnetocaloric properties of this specifically designed system. In the non-field case, the critical temperature given by the Monte Carlo simulations is lower compared to the conventional antiferromagnetic square lattice. When an external magnetic field is applied, we observed signs of a one-dimensional spontaneous symmetry-breaking phase in the mixed-states regime, which is counterintuitive with secondary specific-heat peak. We take the snapshot of this state and elucidated the underlying mechanisms using mean field theory in the field of spin-1 spinor Bose-Einstein condensate . Regarding reentrant behavior, we confirmed its occurrence as the magnetic field varies, but only when the temperature is kept constant. Temperature-dependent reentrant behavior could not be identified within this system. Finally, we examined the magnetocaloric properties and found no evidence of first-order phase transitions within the region of the considered phase-transition point. Although both inverse and direct magnetocaloric effects are present and related to the mixed state, we identified that the relevant phase transitions are all of the second order.

</details>


### [348] [Long-Range Nonequilibrium Correlations as a Thermodynamic Speedometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07964)
*Vinesh Vijayan,Rishma Thilakaraj,Gokila V,Yogeshvari V A*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究边界驱动的对称简单排他过程，将精确的非局域大偏差泛函与弱时变驱动下的有限时间热力学不可逆性相联系，发现长程关联可分解为流体力学模式，其中慢模态的滞后受各驱动频率下过剩熵产生率上界约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡稳态虽由纯局域微观动力学演化而来，却可出现长程关联。作者希望揭示长程关联、大偏差泛函的曲率与有限时间耗散之间的定量联系。

Method: 针对边界驱动的对称简单排他过程，将精确的非局域大偏差泛函与弱时变驱动下的有限时间热力学不可逆性关联；把长程关联分解为流体力学模式，在每一驱动频率下推导最慢模态滞后与过剩熵产生率的上界，并考察准静态极限及不同波长模态的行为。

Result: 最慢模态的滞后满足由各驱动频率下过剩熵产生率决定的上界；该上界在准静态极限下被逼近，而较短波长模态一般不满足相应上界。

Conclusion: 结果建立了大偏差泛函曲率、非平衡稳态长程关联与有限时间耗散之间的定量联系，表明在线性响应范围内，最长波长流体力学模式在慢弛豫与热力学不可逆性之间具有独特关系。

Abstract: Despite purely local microscopic dynamics, nonequilibrium steady states can develop long-range correlations. We investigate this phenomenon in the boundary-driven symmetric simple exclusion process by relating its exact nonlocal large-deviation functional to finite-time thermodynamic irreversibility under weak time-dependent driving. Decomposing the long-range correlations into hydrodynamic modes, we find that the lag associated with the slowest mode satisfies an upper bound determined by the excess entropy production rate at each driving frequency. This bound is approached in the quasistatic limit, whereas progressively shorter-wavelength modes do not generally satisfy the corresponding bound. These results provide a quantitative connection between the curvature of the large-deviation functional, long-range correlations in the nonequilibrium steady state, and finite-time dissipation. Within the linear-response regime considered here, they indicate that the longest-wavelength hydrodynamic mode has a distinct relation between slow relaxation and thermodynamic irreversibility.

</details>


### [349] [Heterogeneous diffusion with recruitment and mortality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08022)
*Esther D. Gutiérrez,Alexander López,R. K. Singh,Sébastien Fumeron,Malte Henkel,Trifce Sandev*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究含独立生成（recruitment）与消亡（mortality）的位置依赖扩散过程，该框架将随机重置下的异质扩散作为特例（生成率=消亡率）。长时系统趋于非平衡稳态，均方位移饱和值独立于生成率，且饱和极限通过作用于扩散系数的有效指数直接编码输运机制；同时刻画了向稳态的非平凡过渡。推导了到达目标的平均首达时间，发现固定生成-消亡比时存在最优消亡率最小化搜索时间（"消亡辅助搜索"效应，数值验证）。将该模型重新解释为传染病传播时，中等移除率反而加速感染到达新区域。框架适用于细菌生长、反应性流体中示踪扩散、拥挤环境中的细胞种群动力学等粒子生成-消亡共存的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质扩散（位置依赖扩散系数）与随机重置的联合已被研究，但真实系统常同时存在独立的粒子生成（recruitment）与消亡（mortality）过程，随机重置只是生成率等于消亡率这一特例。论文旨在建立一个更一般的含生成-消亡的异质扩散框架，并探索其对搜索效率及传播动力学的影响。

Method: 构建含位置依赖扩散系数且同时存在独立生成率与消亡率的扩散过程，推广随机重置下异质扩散的特例；分析长时非平衡稳态及均方位移饱和行为，引入作用于扩散系数的有效指数刻画输运机制与向稳态过渡；推导到达目标的平均首达时间（MFPT），并针对固定生成-消亡比寻找最优消亡率；进行数值评估验证；将生成/消亡重新解释为感染/移除过程应用于传染病空间传播。

Result: 长时达到非平衡稳态，均方位移饱和到一个与生成率无关的值；该饱和极限通过作用于扩散系数的有效指数直接编码输运机制，并刻画了非平凡过渡。MFPT推导表明固定生成-消亡比时存在最优消亡率使搜索时间最小。数值评估证实"消亡辅助搜索"效应。在传染病应用中，中等移除率可反直觉地加速而非延迟感染到达新空间区域。

Conclusion: 含独立生成与消亡的异质扩散呈现非平衡稳态与消亡辅助搜索效应，最优消亡率可最小化搜索时间；类似地，中等移除率可加速感染的空間到达。该框架广泛适用于粒子同时生成与消亡的系统，如细菌生长、反应性流体中的示踪扩散、拥挤环境中的细胞种群动力学。

Abstract: We study a heterogeneous diffusion process with a position-dependent diffusion coefficient in the presence of independent recruitment and mortality. This framework generalises heterogeneous diffusion under stochastic resetting, which is recovered as the special case of equal recruitment and mortality rates. At long times, the system approaches a non-equilibrium stationary state with a mean squared displacement that saturates to a value independent of the recruitment rate. Crucially, this saturation limit directly encodes the underlying transport regime via an effective exponent applied to the diffusion coefficient, and we characterise the non-trivial transition to this stationary regime. We further derive the mean first-passage time to a target and show that, for a fixed recruitment-to-mortality ratio, an optimal mortality rate minimises the search time. This "mortality-assisted search" effect is confirmed by numerical evaluation. Reinterpreting recruitment and mortality as infection and removal processes, we apply the model to epidemic spreading and show that a moderate removal rate can counter-intuitively accelerate, rather than delay, the arrival of infection at a new spatial region. Beyond epidemiology, our framework offers broad relevance to systems featuring concurrent particle creation and destruction, such as bacterial growth, tracer diffusion in reactive fluids, or cellular population dynamics in crowded environments.

</details>


### [350] [Spatial density, first passage times, and entropy of run and tumble particles in a bistable potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08029)
*R. K. Singh,Erez Aghion*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究热浴中双势阱势内run-and-tumble粒子（RTP）的动力学，求得稳态位置分布及其在Laplace空间的含时形式，揭示弛豫从中心向外的弹道式传播，并给出平均首达时的精确形式与首达时间分布的长时行为，进而将动力学粗粒化为二分马尔可夫过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTP是活性物质中描述自驱动粒子的基本模型，而双势阱势是研究双稳态与跃迁的典型框架。作者希望精确刻画RTP在双势阱中的稳态分布、弛豫动力学与首达统计，并探讨粗粒化描述能否保留RTP稳态下的熵产生等非平衡特征。

Method: 在热浴与双势阱势条件下建立RTP的动力学方程，解析求解稳态位置分布与其在Laplace空间的含时解；基于该解分析空间概率密度的弛豫性质；精确求解平均首达时并推导首达时间分布的长时渐近行为；最后将动力学粗粒化为二分马尔可夫过程并比较熵产生。

Result: 得到稳态位置分布的精确形式及Laplace空间的含时解；发现空间概率密度自中心向外收敛至稳态，弛豫前沿随时间弹道式移动，该弹道行为出现在密度尾部、即粒子距原点距离大于表征RTP稳态的长度尺度时；给出平均首达时的精确形式与首达时间分布的长时行为；粗粒化后的二分马尔可夫过程可再现这些行为。

Conclusion: RTP在双势阱中的弛豫具有弹道式传播前沿，尾部弛豫由稳态特征长度尺度决定；粗粒化虽可将动力学约化为二分马尔可夫过程这一双稳态范式，但会丢失信息——RTP即使在稳态也具有有限熵产生，而其粗粒化描述中熵产生恒为零。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of a run and tumble particle (RTP) in a bistable potential, in contact with a heat bath. We find the exact form of the steady state position distribution as well as its time-dependent form in Laplace space. We use this result to study relaxation properties of the spatial probability density and find that it converges to the steady state from the center outwards, with the relaxation front moving ballistically in time. Physically this ballistic behavior arises when observing relaxation properties at the tails of the density, where the distance of the particle from the origin is larger than the length-scale describing the steady state of the RTP. We also find the exact form of the mean first passage time, and the long-time behavior of the first passage time distribution. This allows us to coarse-grain the dynamics as a dichotomous Markov process, a paradigm for a wide class of bistable processes. Coarse-graining, however, comes at the cost of a loss of information: while the RTP possesses a finite entropy production even at steady state, it identically vanishes for its coarse-grained description.

</details>


### [351] [Closed analytical form of many-body free volume and thermodynamics of monodisperse hard disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08265)
*Victor M. Pergamenshchik,Taras Bryk,Andrij Trokhymchuk*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文给出了硬盘模型自由体积的闭式精确几何表示：任意构型下自由体积可由至多五个排斥盘的交叠面积解析表达，据此将N盘配分函数分解为条件自由体积之积，并导出低密度空腔控制与高密度私有胞控制两种渐近形式，从而在几乎整个密度范围内重现硬盘状态方程，并揭示中间密度区存在"笼中缺陷"混合流体区及五盘交叠面积作为六角序敏感局域标量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬盘模型是排除体积物理、液体结构与二维有序化的基础参考体系，但由于盘心可用自由体积具有高度非平凡的多体几何，其热力学长期缺乏解析表述，这是本文要解决的核心难题。

Method: 作者从粒子坐标出发，证明自由体积可用至多五个排斥盘的交叠面积精确表示，建立了从坐标到构型配分函数与熵的几何路径；进而证明N盘配分函数可分解为条件自由体积的乘积，并借助代表性平衡构型计算几何度量。

Result: 该理论在几乎整个密度范围内（直至密堆积）重现了已有的硬盘状态方程；识别出低密度（广延空腔控制）与高密度（强度私有胞控制）两种极限渐近形式；在中间密度发现局域化笼中缺陷提供额外构型熵的混合流体区，位于液晶-六角相共存之前；并表明五盘交叠面积是六角有序的敏感局域标量度量。

Conclusion: 硬盘自由体积具有封闭精确的五盘交叠几何表示，配分函数的条件自由体积分解为状态方程解析构建提供了直接途径，并凸显多体关联在二维有序化中的核心作用。

Abstract: The hard disk model is a fundamental reference system for excluded volume physics, liquid structure, and 2D ordering. Yet, despite its apparent simplicity, an analytical formulation of its thermodynamics has remained difficult because the free volume available to disks centers has a highly nontrivial many-body geometry. Here we show that this geometry admits a closed and exact representation: for any given hard disk configuration, the free volume can be analytically expressed through intersection areas of up to five exclusion disks. This provides a direct geometrical route from particle coordinates to the configurational partition function and entropy. We prove that the N disk partition function factorizes into a product of conditional free volumes and identify its two limiting asymptotic forms: the low density fluid regime controlled by extensive cavities and the high density fluid regime controlled by intensive private cells. Using the geometric measures computed from representative equilibrium particle configurations, the resulting theory reproduces the established hard disk equation of state over almost the entire density range up to close packing. At intermediate densities, the analysis reveals a mixed fluid regime in which localized caged defects provide an additional configurational entropy within the density interval preceding liquid hexatic coexistence. Finally, the five disk intersection area is shown to be a sensitive local scalar measure of hexagonal ordering, complementing the orientational order parameter and emphasizing the central role of many-body correlations.

</details>


### [352] [Hamiltonian curl-forces in systems coupled to multiple thermal reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08423)
*Omer Chor,Aljaž Godec,Oren Raz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将多热库系统通过正则变换映射为单热库+确定性卷力（哈密顿形式）的等价系统，揭示其非平衡本质，并对N维欠阻尼OU过程给出稳态分布与熵产生率的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多温度热库耦合系统通常维持非平凡环流，但其统计力学图像（尤其是环流的力学起源）尚不清晰，且哈密顿结构与非平衡弛豫之间的矛盾值得阐明。

Method: 采用正则变换，将多热库系统映射为耦合单一热库、并受确定性卷力（速度无关、可逆、非保守、旋度非零）驱动的粒子系统；该力可由具各向异性动能项的哈密顿量描述。以两个示例展示映射的力学解释，并对N维欠阻尼Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程进行精确求解。

Result: 映射给出了多温系统电流的力学诠释，且环流方向可由确定性力场定性预测；对N维欠阻尼OU过程得到稳态分布与熵产生率的显式表达式，表明力场的（广义）旋度驱动系统偏离热平衡；进一步证明非保守力可将任意多热库系统精确平衡化并给出相应平衡分布。

Conclusion: 尽管映射后的系统具有哈密顿结构，它并不弛豫到相应的玻尔兹曼分布，本质上是非平衡的；力场的广义旋度是环流与非平衡性的关键，且可通过非保守力实现多热库系统的精确平衡。

Abstract: We investigate the statistical mechanics of a system coupled to multiple heat reservoirs at different temperatures, a setting which typically sustains nontrivial circulation. By applying a canonical transformation, we map the system onto that of a particle coupled to a single reservoir yet driven by a deterministic curl-force---a reversible, velocity-independent non-conservative force with non-vanishing curl---that can be described by a Hamiltonian with an anisotropic kinetic energy term. Despite the Hamiltonian structure, the system does not relax to the corresponding Boltzmann distribution, and is genuinely out of equilibrium. The mapping provides a mechanical interpretation of the currents in multi-temperature systems, as we demonstrate by means of two examples. In particular, the direction of circulation is qualitatively predictable from the deterministic force field. For the exactly solvable case of an $N$-dimensional underdamped Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, we derive explicit expressions for the steady-state distribution and the entropy production rate, highlighting the role of the (generalized) curl of the force field in driving the system out of thermal equilibrium. Finally, we show how a non-conservative force can be used to exactly equilibrate any system coupled to multiple reservoirs, and obtain the corresponding equilibrium distribution.

</details>


### [353] [Replica Fragmentation and Glassy Dynamics in Parity Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08503)
*Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究独立训练的Transformer如何在从局部畴壁重建二元串的任务中表现出类似自旋玻璃的有限尺寸副本碎裂现象，并识别出记忆、退化与恢复三种学习区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解独立训练但共享数据与协议的神经网络为何会实现不同函数，以及这种副本间差异如何随训练集规模变化，是刻画泛化与记忆边界的关键问题。

Method: 将独立训练的运行视为副本，测量真值对齐m、自置信度q_self与跨副本一致性q_cross，并用q_self−q_cross间隙及扣除整体和位置依赖对齐后的残差相关性来区分学习机制。

Result: 小训练集下副本正确拟合全部训练样本但对未见输入仍高置信错误，表现为记忆；较大数据集下模型可泛化随后退化，学习-未学习边界向更短串退缩，之后许多样本又随边界推进而恢复。自-跨间隙清晰区分记忆、退化与恢复三区间；残差相关性也不同，记忆副本残差弱且均匀相关，退化期则有最大比例的反相关副本对。

Conclusion: 这些有限尺寸观测可将持续记忆与进行中的退化-恢复动力学区分开来；热力学极限、长时间极限及向其他学习任务的推广仍待研究。

Abstract: We study how independently trained Transformer neural networks reconstruct a binary string from its local domain walls. Runs sharing the data and training protocol can realize different functions. We treat them as replicas and measure truth alignment $m$, prediction confidence $q_{\mathrm{self}}$, and cross-replica agreement $q_{\mathrm{cross}}$. Confident disagreement defines the finite-size replica fragmentation that we call glass-like. With small training sets, replicas predict all training examples correctly but remain confident in incorrect predictions for unseen inputs, a regime we call memorization. With larger sets, runs can generalize and then retreat. Retreat occurs when outputs start to deviate from truth while confidence remains high. The frontier between learned and unlearned outputs recedes toward shorter strings. Many later recover as the frontier advances again. The self--cross gap $q_{\mathrm{self}}-q_{\mathrm{cross}}$ clearly distinguishes the three learning regimes of memorization, retreat, and recovery. With overall and position-dependent truth alignment subtracted, their residual correlations also differ: memorizing replicas have weakly and uniformly correlated residuals, while retreat has the largest fraction of replica pairs whose residuals are anti-correlated. That is, on the inputs where one replica of such a pair does better than its average, the other tends to do worse. These finite-size observations distinguish persistent memorization from ongoing retreat--recovery dynamics. The thermodynamic and long-time limits, and extensions to other learning tasks, remain open.

</details>


### [354] [Analytical results for the Shannon entropy of the critical transverse-field Ising chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08591)
*M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在 n=1 处直接处理临界横场 Ising 链的 Shannon 熵奇异性，通过将计算基 Born 分布表示为独立长程 Bernoulli 边的奇次边界并应用 Shannon 链式法则，分离出解析边反常与条件圈空间项，建立全阶解析层级并给出若干系数闭式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域基 Shannon 熵的次领头项含普适信息，但临界横场 Ising 链的 Shannon 点在常规 Rényi 方法（n→1 解析延拓）下是奇异的，无法直接处理，因此需要绕过 Rényi 框架在 n=1 直接求解。

Method: 将完整计算基 Born 分布表示为独立长程 Bernoulli 边的奇次边界；利用 Shannon 链式法则将熵拆分为显式独立边项与条件圈空间项。对周期链分离解析边反常，并用 linked vertex support 组织剩余常数，把每个非零 linked 系数表示为有限维积分以构造全阶层级；对区间情形将 exterior 约化为单个 ghost vertex 并利用平方对数相消，并对条件圈项做物理线与 ghost-linked 扇区的全支撑分解。

Result: 周期链方面：给出全阶解析层级，四顶点项闭式求值，前几个 linked 扇区已近乎饱和已知周期常数，并由精确有限尺寸分布独立重构该常数。区间情形：解析边贡献为 γ_E = log2/4 − 1/16，显式平方对数相消。条件圈项给出全阶重整化线与 monomer–dimer 边界周期层级；首个完整 augmented linked 系数闭式求值，其线与 ghost 边界层反常精确相消，并由精确有限尺寸概率独立重构得到 γ_1 = 0.060020(3)。

Conclusion: 该工作在不依赖 Rényi 延拓的 n=1 框架下为临界 Ising 链 Shannon 熵建立了全阶解析层级，解析边反常与 ghost 边界层反常精确相消，数值与已确立的周期常数及 γ_1 一致，验证了方法的自洽性；部分结果仍依赖于所述有限部分匹配、森林与边界森林减除闭合等假设。

Abstract: Local-basis Shannon entropies of critical wave functions contain universal subleading information, but the Shannon point of the critical transverse-field Ising chain is singular in the conventional Rényi approach. We treat it directly at $n=1$ by representing the complete computational-basis Born distribution as the odd-degree boundary of independent long-range Bernoulli edges. The Shannon chain rule separates the entropy into explicit independent-edge and conditional cycle-space terms. For the periodic chain this isolates the analytic edge anomaly and organizes the remaining constant by linked vertex support. Conditional on the stated finite-part matching and forest assumptions, every nonvanishing linked coefficient is represented by an explicit finite-dimensional integral, giving an all-orders analytic hierarchy; the four-vertex term is evaluated in closed form, and the first few linked sectors already nearly saturate the established periodic constant, which is also reconstructed independently from the exact finite-size distribution. For an interval, the exterior reduces exactly to one ghost vertex and the explicit squared logarithms cancel, leaving the analytic edge contribution $γ_E=\log2/4-1/16$. The conditional cycle term has an exact all-support decomposition into physical-line and ghost-linked sectors; conditional on closure of the boundary forest subtraction, these sectors define an all-orders hierarchy of renormalized line and monomer--dimer boundary periods for the remaining logarithmic coefficient. The first complete augmented linked coefficient is evaluated in closed form, with an exact cancellation between its line and ghost boundary-layer anomalies; an independent reconstruction from exact finite-size probabilities recovers the established coefficient $γ_1=0.060020(3)$.

</details>


### [355] [Fast free-energy estimation for complex systems with nonreversible simulated tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08598)
*Yoshihiko Nishikawa,Koji Hukushima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Wang--Landau型反馈的模拟退火自由能估计方法，通过引入温度空间中的非可逆动力学与每步多次温度更新来加速收敛，并在三维Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统的自由能估计通常收敛缓慢，尤其在大系统尺寸下计算代价高昂，因此需要一种能显著加速收敛并适用于大尺度自旋玻璃模型的算法。

Method: 在模拟退火（simulated tempering）框架下采用Wang--Landau型反馈机制进行自由能估计，并引入温度空间中的非可逆动力学以及每步多次温度更新。

Result: 该算法在三维Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃模型上可精确估计自由能，系统尺寸可达L≃64；利用估计所得自由能进行的平衡模拟退火可在覆盖自旋玻璃转变温度的宽温区内快速采样，且其收敛时间随系统尺寸的增长远慢于另一种在线自由能估计方法。

Conclusion: 温度空间中的非可逆动力学和每步多次温度更新能显著加速自由能估计的收敛，该算法在大尺度自旋玻璃系统中具有精确性与高效性，且在收敛速度上优于已有在线估计方法。

Abstract: We discuss free-energy estimation for complex systems using simulated tempering with Wang--Landau-type feedback. We show that introducing nonreversible dynamics in temperature space and multiple temperature updates per step significantly accelerates the convergence of the free-energy estimate. We then apply the algorithm to the three-dimensional Edwards-Anderson spin-glass model. Our results show that the algorithm can precisely estimate the free energy up to a large system size $L \simeq 64$, and that subsequent equilibrium simulated tempering simulations with the estimated free energy can rapidly sample from the Boltzmann distributions over a wide temperature range that covers the spin-glass transition temperature. The convergence time of our algorithm grows much more slowly with system size than that of another known algorithm for on-the-fly free-energy estimation.

</details>


### [356] [Algebraic Tensor Network Renormalization and Holographic duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08787)
*Chenqi Meng,Tian Lan,Zhengcheng Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出 R-TNR 方法，将融合范畴 R 的范畴约束嵌入局域张量空间，并在 Loop-TNR 中保持这些约束，从而在无需精细调节初始张量的情况下实现全息拓扑自举，高精度恢复红外共形场论及其按任意子扇区分解的共形数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涌现广义对称性与全息原理对理解量子相变至关重要。近年来，结合广义对称性的张量网络重整化及其不动点张量表述被提出，作为重构共形场论的离散时空框架。然而该框架尚缺乏完整的全息表述，且现有表述无法直接容纳一般具有模协变性的扇区配分函数。因此，作者希望构建一个能自然包含范畴对称性、并可直接处理模协变扇区分解的统一框架。

Method: 作者构建了 R-TNR：将融合范畴 R 的范畴约束（即融合规则、量子维度等范畴数据）编码进局域张量空间，并设计 R-Loop-TNR 算法使这些约束在重整化流下保持不变。对合适的 R 选择，初始张量在 R-Loop-TNR 下流向稳定的无能隙不动点。该方法按任意子扇区解析共形塔，提取标度维度、共形自旋以及扇区分解的结构常数幅度。

Result: 在 Ising、三临界 Ising 和三态 Potts 共形场论实例中，广泛的初始参数范围在每个实例下都流向同一无能隙不动点。方法按任意子扇区分辨共形塔，得到的标度维度、共形自旋以及扇区分解结构常数幅度，与以往方法相比精度极高。

Conclusion: 结果实现了基于稳定对称-拓扑序的拓扑自举：有限的范畴与几何输入，加上无需精细调节的初始张量，就足以恢复红外共形场论及其扇区分解的共形数据。这表明广义对称性、张量网络重整化与全息原理可在统一的离散框架下自洽结合。

Abstract: Emergent generalized symmetries and the holographic principle play very important roles in understanding quantum phase transitions. In recent years, tensor-network renormalization with generalized symmetry and the corresponding fixed-point tensor formulation have been proposed as a discrete spacetime framework for reformulating conformal field theory. Nevertheless, a complete holographic formulation within this framework is still lacking, and the current formulation does not directly accommodate sector partition functions that are modular covariant in general. In this work, we formulate $\mathcal{R}$-TNR, which builds the categorical constraints of a fusion category $\mathcal{R}$ into local tensor spaces and preserves these constraints under Loop-TNR. For suitable choices of $\mathcal{R}$, the initial tensors constructed here flow under $\mathcal{R}$-Loop-TNR to stable gapless fixed points. In the Ising, tricritical Ising, and three-state Potts conformal field theory examples, a broad range of initial parameters leads to the same gapless fixed point in each example. The method resolves conformal towers by anyon sector and yields scaling dimensions, conformal spins, and magnitudes of sector-resolved structure constants with extremely high accuracy compared to previous methods. These results realize a topological bootstrap based on stable symmetry-topological-order in which finite categorical and geometric input, together with an initial tensor that need not be fine-tuned, suffices to recover the infrared conformal field theory and its sector-resolved conformal data.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [357] [A toric Calabi-Yau counterexample to Schimpf's connected stable pairs pole conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06876)
*Reginald Anderson*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个环面反例，推翻了Schimpf关于稳定对极点猜想连通版本的预测：在连通下降系数中，非零p-极点仅出现在-p为m次单位根且1≤m≤divis(β)处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pandharipande的极点猜想及其Schimpf连通重构预测了稳定对连通下降系数中p-极点的限制规律。本文旨在检验该预测是否成立，寻找可能的反例。

Method: 取一个光滑拟射影环面Calabi–Yau三维簇，其紧致环面不变曲线构成链C1∪C2。对原始类β=2[C1]+[C2]，计算插入ch_z([v]_T)的连通级数中Q^β z^3系数（[v]_T为v=C1∩C2的等变类），并限制到在v处切权为(1,2,−3)的单参数子环面上进行Laurent展开。

Result: 该系数在限制后的Laurent展开为(9/4)·1/(p−1)+O(1)，即在p=1处出现非零极点。由于divis(β)=1，Schimpf条件仅允许非零极点p=−1，故p=1处的极点被禁止，从而构成反例。

Conclusion: 该环面反例表明Schimpf对Pandharipande极点猜想的连通重构预测不成立，其关于p-极点仅出现在特定单位根处的限制条件被违反。

Abstract: Schimpf's connected reformulation of Pandharipande's pole conjecture for stable pairs predicts the following restriction: in class \(β\), connected descendent coefficients have nonzero \(p\)-poles only where \(-p\) is an \(m\)-th root of unity with \(1\leq m\leq\divis(β)\). We give a toric counterexample. The threefold is a smooth quasi-projective toric Calabi--Yau threefold whose compact torus-invariant curves form a chain \(C_1\cup C_2\). For the primitive class \(β=2[C_1]+[C_2]\), the coefficient of \(Q^βz^3\) in the connected series for the insertion \(\ch_z([v]_T)\), where \([v]_T\) is the equivariant class of \(v=C_1\cap C_2\), has Laurent expansion \[
  {\frac{9}{4}}\frac{1}{p-1}+O(1) \] after restricting to the one-parameter subtorus with tangent weights \((1,2,-3)\) at \(v\). Since \(\divis(β)=1\), Schimpf's condition allows only the nonzero pole \(p=-1\). The pole at \(p=1\) is therefore forbidden.

</details>


### [358] [Deformations of toric pairs and the smoothing of Batyrev Calabi-Yau threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06884)
*Bernd Johannes Wuebben*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有本原边的自反四维多胞形所定义环面四fold的形变，用以光滑一般反规范Calabi-Yau超曲面在指定二维面上的奇点，并给出面局部判据及其在Kreuzer-Skarke分类中的统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反规范Calabi-Yau超曲面在自反多胞形的奇异面上产生奇点，如何通过环面四fold的形变实现光滑化是Calabi-Yau几何的核心问题；现有方法难以逐面刻画光滑化条件，作者希望给出可操作的局部判据并评估其覆盖范围。

Method: 选取自反四polytope Δ的面扇形定义环面四fold，对选定二维面的面锥可光滑化、且对偶边含内格点情形构造形变；在Cox坐标下给出三项式方程，实现该面上超曲面的光滑化。若仅该面锥奇异则得到超曲面的光滑化；若所有奇异面均满足条件，则在节点情形或半通用形变空间不可约时得到同时光滑化。

Result: 给出面式判据：面锥可光滑化且对偶边含内格点。构造七顶点例子，含一个普通二重点且不可光滑化，说明对偶边条件是最优的。扫描Kreuzer-Skarke分类发现，该判据解释了Batyrev-Kreuzer普查中30,241个光滑化里的3,774个，其余26,467个需要不同面的例外曲线类之间的关系。

Conclusion: 面局部形变可有效光滑反规范Calabi-Yau超曲面的面奇点，条件尖锐，但仅覆盖约12.5%的已知光滑化，大多数情形需借助跨面的例外曲线类关系。

Abstract: Let $Δ$ be a reflexive four-polytope with primitive edges. We construct deformations of the toric fourfold defined by its face fan that smooth the singularities of a generic anticanonical Calabi-Yau hypersurface over a chosen two-dimensional face. The construction applies when the face cone is smoothable and the dual edge has an interior lattice point; its equations are trinomials in Cox coordinates. It gives a smoothing of the hypersurface when this is the only face whose cone is singular. If every singular face satisfies these conditions, simultaneous smoothing follows in the nodal case and, in general, under irreducibility of the semiuniversal deformation space. A seven-vertex example with one ordinary double point and no smoothing shows that the dual-edge condition is sharp. A scan of the Kreuzer--Skarke classification shows that the facewise criterion accounts for 3,774 of the 30,241 smoothings in the Batyrev--Kreuzer census; the other 26,467 require relations among exceptional curve classes from different faces.

</details>


### [359] [Determinacy and Modality in Positive Characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07092)
*Yotam Svoray*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在正特征下通过"指定喷流横截性、Frobenius 形变方向、有限平坦临界覆盖"三种互补构造，研究矩阵芽与孤立超曲面奇点的有限决定性及模态，并给出确定性界、模族参数化及 F-跳跃数有限性等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正特征下有限决定性与模态理论尚不完善，需建立与特征无关或依赖特征的判定工具，并刻画 Milnor 数、确定性、本质余秩等不变量，同时统一处理 F-跳跃数与 F-纯阈值的有限性问题。

Method: 采用三种互补构造：(1) 指定喷流横截性，将有限左右决定性等价于切像的有限余维；(2) Frobenius 形变方向；(3) 有限平坦临界覆盖。对任意域上矩阵芽，利用对秩分层的指定喷流横截性；对正特征代数闭域上孤立超曲面奇点，引入其长度与 Loewy 结构控制 Milnor 数、决定性和本质余秩的 Frobenius 代数。

Result: 矩阵芽情形：有限左右决定性等价于切像有限余维；纯右等价、无限域上单边作用及有限域指定范围有类似结果；得到正维理想有限接触决定性刻画，并推出行列式理想的期望高度、Cohen-Macaulay性、既约性与正规性。超曲面情形：真模态与右模态一致；固定特征下有界右模态给出与外围维数无关的确定性界，且模非奇异二次悬浮可有限代数参数化；F-跳跃数一致有限，F-纯阈值在单一 Frobenius 层确定。

Conclusion: 正特征奇点理论中，喷流横截性与 Frobenius 方法相结合可统一处理决定性与模态问题，并揭示 Frobenius 代数对奇点不变量的控制作用及 F-奇异量的有限性规律。

Abstract: We study finite determinacy and modality in positive characteristic through three complementary constructions: prescribed-jet transversality, Frobenius deformation directions, and a finite flat critical cover. For matrix germs over an arbitrary field, prescribed-jet transversality to the rank stratification shows that finite left-right determinacy is equivalent to finite codimension of the tangent image. Analogous results hold for pure right equivalence, for one-sided actions over infinite fields, and in specified ranges over finite fields. These methods also yield a characterization of finite contact determinacy for positive-dimensional ideals and imply expected-height, Cohen-Macaulayness, reducedness, and normality results for determinantal ideals. For isolated hypersurface singularities over an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p>0$, we introduce a Frobenius algebra whose length and Loewy structure govern the Milnor number, determinacy, and essential corank. Using a finite flat critical cover, we prove that proper modality coincides with right modality. It follows that, in fixed characteristic, bounded right modality gives an ambient-dimension-independent bound on determinacy and, up to nonsingular quadratic suspension, a finite algebraic parametrization by modular families. The same Frobenius methods yield uniform finiteness results for $F$-jumping numbers and show that the $F$-pure threshold is determined at a single Frobenius level.

</details>


### [360] [Schubert compactifications of matrix pencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07164)
*Vincenzo Galgano,Fulvio Gesmundo,Hanieh Keneshlou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文完整研究矩阵束（dim L=2）的Schubert紧化，构造出作为P²爆碎的奇异有理曲面类比——Schubert曲面，并将其几何不变量与矩阵束的Kronecker不变量建立可逆对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性矩阵空间L⊆Mat_{n×m}的Schubert紧化将L的代数不变量转化为Grassmannian中相应簇的射影与交理论不变量，可统一经典Schubert簇与拟阵Schubert簇。作者希望在dim L=2的最简单非平凡情形（矩阵束）中彻底发展这套几何，理解其奇异性与不变量字典。

Method: 在Gr(n,n+m)中构造矩阵束的Schubert紧化，得到Schubert曲面；分析其正规性与商奇点，计算次数与上同调环，并用矩阵束的Kronecker不变量描述其特殊有理曲线及相交配对；进一步证明该字典可反向读取束的不变量，并研究Plücker嵌入下定义理想的生成次数与Green性质N_p。

Result: Schubert曲面是P²爆碎所得有理曲面的奇异类比；证明其为具有商奇点的正规曲面，计算了次数、上同调环，并以Kronecker不变量刻画特殊有理曲线及其相交配对；证明该字典可逆，Schubert曲面可唯一确定束的正则分量（在GL_2×GL_n×GL_m作用下）；并证明Plücker嵌入下矩阵束Schubert曲面的理想由二次生成，且在一定范围内满足Green性质N_p。

Conclusion: 矩阵束的Schubert紧化为研究线性矩阵空间提供了强有力的射影与交理论工具，其几何与Kronecker不变量之间存在双向对应，为更高维情形的推广奠定基础。

Abstract: The Schubert compactification of a linear space of matrices $L \subseteq \textrm{Mat}_{n\times m}$ is a natural compactification of $L$ in the Grassmannian $\textrm{Gr}(n, n+m)$. This construction generalises classical Schubert varieties and matroid Schubert varieties, and it turns invariants of linear matrix spaces into projective and intersection-theoretic invariants of the associated variety. We develop this geometry completely in the case of matrix pencils, that is $\dim L=2$. The resulting Schubert surfaces are singular analogues of rational surfaces obtained as blow-ups of $\mathbb{P}^2$. We show that they are normal with quotient singularities, compute their degree, cohomology ring, and describe the distinguished rational curves and their intersection pairing in terms of the Kronecker invariants of the matrix pencil. Remarkably, this dictionary can be reversed: the invariants of the pencil are read off from intersection numbers and other invariants of the variety. In particular, we prove that the Schubert surface determines the regular component of the pencil, up to the natural action of $\mathrm{GL}_2 \times \mathrm{GL}_n \times\mathrm{GL}_m$. Finally, we prove that the ideal of Schubert surfaces of matrix pencils in the Plücker embedding is generated in degree $2$ and satisfies Green's property $N_p$ in a certain range.

</details>


### [361] [Algebraic ellipticity of quartic double spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07260)
*Alexander Dvorsky,Shulim Kaliman,Mikhail Zaidenberg*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了特征零代数闭域上光滑四次双n重（n≥2）均为Gromov意义下的代数椭圆流形，特别地光滑四次双立体是代数椭圆的，从而回答了Zaidenberg提出的问题4.28；并证明了除去余维至少2的闭子集保持光滑四次双空间与光滑三次超曲面的代数椭圆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov代数椭圆性是复代数几何与双曲性研究中的重要概念，与Oka流形、可缩性及有理连通性密切相关。光滑四次双立体等具体簇是否为代数椭圆长期是公开问题（Zaidenberg问题4.28），本文旨在解决该问题并推广至更高维情形。

Method: 通过构造覆盖四次双n重的代数族C-纤维化（即支配性代数喷流/C-纤维化映射），结合典范除子与分支轨迹的几何分析，验证Gromov意义下的代数椭圆性定义；对去除余维≥2闭子集的情形，利用代数椭圆性在去除小余维闭子集下的稳定性进行论证。

Result: 证明特征零代数闭域上任意光滑四次双n重（n≥2）是代数椭圆的；由此光滑四次双立体为代数椭圆，肯定回答了Zaidenberg问题4.28；并证明对光滑四次双空间与维数≥2的光滑三次超曲面，移除任意余维至少2的闭子集后仍保持代数椭圆性。

Conclusion: 光滑四次双n重及其去余维≥2闭子集的版本均为Gromov代数椭圆流形，扩展了代数椭圆性的已知例子类，并验证了Zaidenberg调查中的公开问题。

Abstract: We prove that every smooth quartic double $n$-fold, $n\ge2$, over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero is algebraically elliptic in Gromov's sense. In particular, every smooth quartic double solid is algebraically elliptic, which answers Question~4.28 in [M. Zaidenberg, Algebraic Gromov ellipticity: a brief survey, Taiwanese J. Math. 29 (2025), no. 6, 1681-1705]. We also prove that removing any closed subset of codimension at least two preserves algebraic ellipticity for smooth quartic double spaces and smooth cubic hypersurfaces of dimension at least two.

</details>


### [362] [Potential density of rational points on sextic double solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07479)
*Ivan Cheltsov,Yuri Tschinkel*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了光滑六次双有理体（sextic double solid）的例子，其在任意特征为零的域上具有潜在稠密的有理点集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有理点稠密性是算术代数几何的核心问题之一。光滑六次双有理体作为Calabi-Yau三维簇的一类，其潜在稠密有理点集的存在性尚缺乏一般性构造，本文旨在提供此类实例。

Method: 通过对光滑六次双有理体进行具体构造，在任意特征零的域上给出具有潜在稠密有理点集的例子。摘要未披露具体技术细节（如所选取的域、构造策略等）。

Result: 给出了光滑六次双有理体的例子，其有理点集在任意特征零的域上均为潜在稠密（potentially dense），即在某个有限扩张上稠密。

Conclusion: 光滑六次双有理体可以具有潜在稠密的有理点集，为相关潜在稠密性问题的研究提供了新的具体证据。

Abstract: We provide examples of smooth sextic double solids with potentially dense sets of rational points, over any field of characteristic zero.

</details>


### [363] [The finite-degree profile of essential dimension I: general theory and characteristic prime to $|G|$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07508)
*Abhishek Shukla*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文引入了有限度轮廓（finite-degree profile）$\edd d_k(\tau)$ 及其跳跃度（jump degrees）$d_j(\tau)$，用以刻画一个torsor需要多大域扩张才能降到给定参数数目。作者给出了若干结构性定理，包括跳跃度的Sylow整除定理、赋值论意义的rank–index定理，并据此计算了若干群（如$(\Z/2)^n$、$\Z/p$、$S_5$、$S_n$）的跳跃度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本质维数虽刻画定义torsor所需的参数数目，但它在域扩张下可能急剧下降，且未说明需要多大扩张才能实现这种下降。为弥补这一缺陷，论文引入有限度轮廓与跳跃度，量化"在有限度域扩张下torsor所需的最少参数数目"。

Method: 1) 定义有限度轮廓 $\edd d_k(\tau)=\min\{\ed_k(\tau\otimes_FF'):[F':F]\le d\}$ 及跳跃度 $d_j(\tau)$；2) 给出几何刻画：$\edd d_k(\tau)\le j$ 当且仅当某个维数 $\le j$ 的泛自由 $G$-簇在其twist的自由轨迹上有次数 $\le d$ 的闭点；3) 证明跳跃度的Sylow整除定理与赋值论rank–index不等式；4) 对含足够单位根的域上的交换群、$\Z/p$（特征0）、$S_5$、$S_n$ 分别进行显式计算，并使用Abel–Jacobi刚性作为下界工具。

Result: 1) 对特征不整除 $|A|$ 的交换群 $A$，证明 $\edd d_k(\tgen)\ge\min\{\rk B:B\le A,\ [A:B]\le d\}$，并在含足够单位根域上完全确定轮廓，例如 $(\Z/2)^n$ 的情形为 $n-\lfloor\log_2 d\rfloor$；2) 特征0下对 $\Z/p$ 得 $\lceil m/2\rceil\le d_1\le[k(\zeta_p+\zeta_p^{-1}):k]$，$m=[k(\zeta_p):k]$，且 $m$ 为偶数时取等号；3) 特征0下 $S_5$ 的 $d_1=4$，说明Klein对一般五次式的约化为最优；4) $n\ge6$ 时 $6\mid d_1(S_n)$。

Conclusion: 有限度轮廓与跳跃度为研究torsor在域扩张下参数数目的下降提供了精细的不变量，能统一刻画交换群情形并由赋值论与Sylow方法给出整除性约束。特别地，$S_5$ 最优约化的下界来自Abel–Jacobi刚性而非上同调不变量，表明这类下界需要新的非上同调工具。

Abstract: Essential dimension counts the parameters needed to define a torsor, but it can drop sharply over a field extension, and it does not say how large an extension is needed. For a $G$-torsor $τ$ over a field $F\supseteq k$ we study the \emph{finite-degree profile} $\edd d_k(τ)=\min\{\ed_k(τ\otimes_FF'):[F':F]\le d\}$, a variant of invariants of Farb--Kisin--Wolfson and Farb--Wolfson, and its jump degrees $d_j(τ)$, the least degree of an extension over which $τ$ needs only $j$ parameters. The profile is geometric over every field: $\edd d_k(τ)\le j$ iff some generically free $G$-variety of dimension $\le j$ has a closed point of degree $\le d$ in the free locus of its twist by $τ$. We prove a Sylow divisibility theorem for jump degrees and a valuation-theoretic rank--index theorem, $\edd d_k(\tgen)\ge\min\{\rk B:B\le A,\ [A:B]\le d\}$ for abelian $A$ of order prime to $\operatorname{char}k$, which determines the profile of every abelian group over a field with enough roots of unity, for instance $n-\lfloor\log_2d\rfloor$ for $(\Z/2)^n$. For $\Z/p$ in characteristic $0$ we show $\lceil m/2\rceil\le d_1\le[k(ζ_p+ζ_p^{-1}):k]$, $m=[k(ζ_p):k]$, with equality for $m$ even. For $S_5$ in characteristic $0$ we prove $d_1=4$, so Klein's reduction of the generic quintic is optimal; the lower bound comes from Abel--Jacobi rigidity, not from cohomological invariants. For $n\ge6$, $6\mid d_1(S_n)$.

</details>


### [364] [Indexed Logarithmic Cartier Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07543)
*Sami Fersi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 将Ogus–Vologodsky的Cartier变换推广到对数情形：通过对构造的topoi与crystal赋予指标结构，并利用对数微分算子代数的Azumaya性质，证明相应指标crystal范畴的等价，从而完成Cartier变换的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前文已构造对数晶体-like site与函子C'→underline{C}并证明其全忠实，但由于对数光滑情形下Frobenius一般不平坦，本质满性不明确，故需新方法解决。

Method: 在Spf W上取平凡对数结构，设S为局部有限型且log flat的fs log p-adic形式概型的特殊纤维，对log smooth的X→S，细化既有topoi与crystal，赋予指标结构（受Lorenzon的Cartier下降指标扩张启发），并利用对数微分算子指标代数的Azumaya性质。

Result: 得到相应指标crystal范畴之间的等价。

Conclusion: 推广了Cartier变换，绕过了Frobenius不平坦的障碍。

Abstract: This article is the third of a series of three articles whose goal is to generalize the Cartier transform of Ogus and Vologodsky to the logarithmic setting. In the second one, we generalized a topos-theoretic version of this transform, due to Oyama. Let $W$ be the ring of Witt vectors of a perfect field of positive characteristic $p$ and equip $\operatorname{Spf}W$ with the trivial log structure. Let $\mathfrak{S}$ be an fs log $p$-adic formal scheme, locally of finite type and log flat over $\operatorname{Spf}W$ and denote its special fiber by $S.$ For a log smooth morphism of logarithmic schemes $X \rightarrow S,$ we constructed crystalline-like ringed topoi $\mathcal{E}'$ and $\underline{\mathcal{E}}$ and subcategories of crystals of quasi-coherent modules $\mathcal{C}'$ and $\underline{\mathcal{C}},$ equivalent respectively, under some lifting assumption, to modules with Higgs fields and integrable connections, both satisfying certain nilpotence conditions, and a morphism of topoi $\underline{\mathcal{E}} \rightarrow \mathcal{E}'.$ We proved that the pullback functor of this morphism of topoi preserves quasi-coherent crystals and induces a fully faithful functor $\mathcal{C}' \rightarrow \underline{\mathcal{C}}.$ Since the Frobenius morphism is not, in general, flat in the log smooth setting, it is not clear that this functor is essentially surjective. To address this issue, we refine the topoi and crystals mentioned above by endowing them with an indexed structure, inspired by Lorenzon's indexed extension of Cartier descent to smooth logarithmic schemes. Using the Azumaya property of the indexed algebra of logarithmic differential operators, we then obtain an equivalence between the corresponding categories of indexed crystals, thereby generalizing the Cartier transform.

</details>


### [365] [Semisimplicity, purity and Mumford-Tate conjecture for hyper-Kähler varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07890)
*Kazuhiro Ito,Haitao Zou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了有限生成域上超Kähler簇各次数的Mumford-Tate猜想及p进域上的权重-单值猜想，并验证了Weil-Deligne表示的强相容性与积分细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mumford-Tate猜想与权重-单值猜想是算术代数几何中关于Galois表示与代数闭链的核心问题。超Kähler簇作为特殊的一类代数簇，其ℓ进上同调的半单性与单值群结构尚需系统刻画，尤其在一般有限生成域与p进情形缺乏统一处理。

Method: 通过消去全上同调代数单值群（algebraic monodromy group）的单幂根（unipotent radical），建立ℓ进上同调的半单性，从而证明各次数的Mumford-Tate猜想；同时结合p进Hodge理论处理权重-单值猜想。

Result: 对有限生成于ℚ的域上超Kähler簇，任意次数Mumford-Tate猜想成立；对p进域上超Kähler簇，权重-单值猜想成立；对b2≥4的数域上超Kähler簇，经适当有限扩张后，取值于Mumford-Tate群的Weil-Deligne表示具有强相容性，并给出远离开有限多素数的积分细化。

Conclusion: 该工作系统解决了超Kähler簇的Mumford-Tate猜想与权重-单值猜想，并强化了Weil-Deligne表示的相容性理论，为超Kähler簇的算术性质提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We prove the Mumford-Tate conjecture in every degree for hyper-Kähler varieties over fields finitely generated over $\mathbb{Q}$. The proof establishes semisimplicity of $\ell$-adic cohomology by eliminating the unipotent radical of the algebraic monodromy group of total cohomology. We also prove the weight-monodromy conjecture for hyper-Kähler varieties over $p$-adic fields. For hyper-Kähler varieties over number fields with $b_2\geq 4$, we establish, after suitable finite extensions, strong compatibility of the associated Weil-Deligne representations valued in Mumford-Tate groups and its integral refinement away from finitely many primes.

</details>


### [366] [Derived supergeometry and perfect obstruction theories on algebraic superstacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08608)
*Ugo Bruzzo,Daniel Hernández Ruipérez,Emanuele Pavia,Andrea T. Ricolfi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在导出代数几何框架下发展代数超叠的形变理论，构造稳定超映射模空间的完美障碍理论，并计算其虚拟维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为稳定超映射模空间建立形变理论基础，构造完美障碍理论并给出此前被猜测的虚拟维数公式的证明。

Method: 将导出代数几何工具推广到超几何情形，引入导出超叠、余切超复形与代数超叠的完美障碍理论；对稳定超映射的Deligne–Mumford超叠构造自然导出增强并计算其余切超复形。

Result: 证明代数超叠的拟光滑导出增强总给出完美障碍理论；证明诱导的障碍理论是完美的；通过超Grothendieck–Riemann–Roch计算虚拟维数，得到与先前猜测一致的公式，并退化为经典稳定映射模叠的虚拟维数。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典形变理论原理推广至超几何情形，为稳定超映射模空间提供形变论证明并统一了经典情形。

Abstract: We develop a deformation-theoretic framework for algebraic superstacks and apply it to the construction of perfect obstruction theories on moduli spaces of stable supermaps. In order to achieve this we employ the tools and concepts from derived algebraic geometry, extending them to the supergeometric setting. Our main technical contributions are (i) the notions of derived superstacks, (ii) their cotangent supercomplexes and (iii) perfect obstruction theories for algebraic superstacks. We prove that a quasi-smooth derived enhancement of an algebraic superstack always yields a perfect obstruction theory: this generalizes a well-known principle from classical deformation theory. We then turn to the case of the Deligne--Mumford superstack $\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathsf{st-}\mathrm{SUSY}}_{g,\mathfrak{n}}(\boldsymbol{Y})$ of stable supermaps of fixed genus, homology class and numbers of Neveu--Schwarz and Ramond--Ramond punctures, with target a smooth projective superscheme $\boldsymbol{Y}$. We exhibit a natural derived enhancement $\mathbb{R} \boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathsf{st-}\mathrm{SUSY}}_{g,\mathfrak{n}}(\boldsymbol{Y})$ and compute its cotangent supercomplex; we prove that the induced obstruction theory on $\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}^{\mathsf{st-}\mathrm{SUSY}}_{g,\mathfrak{n}}(\boldsymbol{Y})$ is perfect. Finally, we compute its virtual dimension by super Grothendieck--Riemann--Roch. The resulting formula gives, in particular, a deformation-theoretic proof of the virtual dimension formula previously conjectured for stable supermaps and specializes to the usual virtual dimension of the classical moduli stack of stable maps.

</details>


### [367] [Semiample perturbations over arbitrary fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07908)
*Dae-Won Lee*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在假设射影对数消解成立的前提下，构造保持所有对数典范位的半丰沛扰动，并给出有效除子刻画与广义三维极小模型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意域上（尤其正特征情形）处理对数典范奇点与半丰沛性时，缺乏统一的扰动工具，且需刻画除子类何时可表示为lc/klt对的有效部分。

Method: 假设射影对数消解（projective log resolutions），构造任意域上的半丰沛扰动（semiample perturbations），并保持所有对数典范位（log canonical places）；随后利用渐近消没阶（asymptotic orders of vanishing）进行刻画。

Result: 1) 构造出保持所有lcs的半丰沛扰动；2) 对截面环有限生成的除子类，用渐近消没阶刻画了能给出lc或klt对的有效除子存在性；3) 在特征p>5的F-有限域上得到广义三维的极小模型与半丰沛性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作以对数消解假设为骨架，提供统一的半丰沛扰动技术，并将有效除子存在性问题归结为渐近消没阶，进而推广极小模型纲领到F-有限域上特征p>5的广义三维情形。

Abstract: Assuming projective log resolutions, we construct semiample perturbations over arbitrary fields that preserve all log canonical places. As an application, for divisor classes with finitely generated section rings, we characterize the existence of effective divisors yielding lc or klt pairs in terms of asymptotic orders of vanishing. We also derive minimal model and semiampleness results for generalized threefolds over $F$-finite fields of characteristic $p>5$.

</details>


### [368] [Hodge--Tjurina Numbers, Tjurina Spectra, and Thom--Sebastiani Formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08290)
*Shijie Zheng,Huaiqing Zuo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究孤立超曲面奇点的 Hodge–Tjurina 数与 Tjurina 子谱，将其与 Hodge 理想、微局部 V-滤过联系，考察 Thom–Sebastiani 和下的行为，并给出分离和下 Tjurina 子谱 Thom–Sebastiani 性质的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hodge–Tjurina 数与 Tjurina 子谱是孤立超曲面奇点的重要不变量，但其与 Hodge 理想、微局部 V-滤过的联系，以及在 Thom–Sebastiani 和下的表现尚不清晰。特别是 Almirón 提出的 Tjurina 子谱 Thom–Sebastiani 性质问题尚未解决。

Method: 利用 Hodge 理想与微局部 V-滤过建立这些不变量之间的联系，分析其在 Thom–Sebastiani 和下的行为，并对分离和推导滤过亏格公式；进一步讨论有理采样、谱重构与对称性界。

Result: 对分离和得到滤过亏格公式，并证明 Tjurina 子谱的 Thom–Sebastiani 性质成立当且仅当 Tjurina 数可乘，从而肯定回答 Almirón 的问题；同时给出说明整数 Hodge–Tjurina 数据所能捕捉与丢失信息的例子。

Conclusion: Tjurina 子谱的 Thom–Sebastiani 性质等价于 Tjurina 数的可乘性；整数 Hodge–Tjurina 数据仅部分刻画奇点信息，需结合有理采样与谱重构等工具。

Abstract: We study Hodge--Tjurina numbers and the Tjurina subspectrum of isolated hypersurface singularities. We relate these invariants to Hodge ideals and microlocal $V$-filtrations and investigate their behavior under Thom--Sebastiani sums. For separated sums, we obtain a filtered defect formula and, in particular, give an affirmative answer to the question raised by Almirón concerning the Thom--Sebastiani property of the Tjurina subspectrum: it holds if and only if the Tjurina number is multiplicative. We also discuss rational sampling, spectral reconstruction, symmetry bounds, and examples illustrating the information captured and lost by integer Hodge--Tjurina data.

</details>


### [369] [On constructibility of klt type varieties and simple $D$-module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08334)
*Donghyeon Kim*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：稠密 klt 型与 $\mathcal{D}$-单性（$\mathcal{D}$-simplicity）相结合可推出 klt 型性质，从而在代数微分算子理论与双有理几何之间建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: klt 型（klt type）是双有理几何中刻画奇点的重要概念，而 $\mathcal{D}$-单性属于代数微分算子理论的研究范畴。两者此前分属不同领域，缺少直接的沟通桥梁；本文旨在通过研究二者的相互作用，将代数微分算子理论引入双有理几何的奇点分析。

Method: 采用纯理论证明的方式：在同时满足稠密 klt 型与 $\mathcal{D}$-单性两个条件的假设下，通过逻辑推证建立该组合与 klt 型性质之间的蕴含关系。

Result: 证明了"稠密 klt 型 + $\mathcal{D}$-单性 ⟹ klt 型"这一命题。

Conclusion: 该结果为代数微分算子理论与双有理几何之间搭建了桥梁，表明微分算子（如 $\mathcal{D}$-模）性质可作为判定或推出 klt 型奇点性质的有效工具，为后续跨领域研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that the combination of dense klt type and $\mathcal{D}$-simplicity property establishes the klt type property. The result paves the way to connect the theory of algebraic differential operators with the area of birational geometry.

</details>


### [370] [Special Loci in the Moduli of Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08382)
*David Urbanik,Ziquan Yang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入研究模空间中混合特征特殊轨迹的新技术，并以曲线模空间 $\mathsf{M}_g$ 为重点给出若干应用，包括分裂雅可比簇的 Zariski 稠密性、超奇异轨迹的非稠密性以及代数闭链的提升结果，方法可推广至映到 Hodge 型 Shimura 簇的模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模空间中的混合特征特殊轨迹是算术代数几何的核心问题之一，Oort 与 de Jong 曾提出相关问题，亟需新的技术手段来刻画这些特殊轨迹的几何性质。

Method: 引入研究模空间混合特征特殊轨迹的新技术，并推广到更一般的情形，即映到 Hodge 型 Shimura 簇（甚至超出 Shimura 框架）的模空间。

Result: 在适当的数值条件下，得到分裂雅可比簇的 Zariski 稠密性结果、超奇异轨迹的非稠密性结果，以及代数闭链的提升结果。

Conclusion: 所提方法具有良好的普适性，可应用于曲线模空间之外的模空间，并与 Oort 和 de Jong 提出的问题相关联。

Abstract: We introduce new techniques to study mixed-characteristic special loci in moduli spaces and give several applications, focusing in particular on the moduli space $\mathsf{M}_g$ of curves. These applications include Zariski density results for split Jacobians, non-density results for supersingular loci, and lifting results for algebraic cycles, all under appropriate numerical conditions. Our methods generalize to more-or-less arbitrary moduli spaces mapping to Hodge-type Shimura varieties (and even some settings beyond the Shimura case), and relate to questions raised by Oort and de Jong.

</details>


### [371] [On plane Cremona maps of degree 4 and length 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08416)
*Alberto Calabri,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Giao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文将四次平面Cremona映射按长度分为两类：de Jonquières型（长度1，含三重基点）与非de Jonquières型（长度2）。作者在射影平面自同构的左右作用下，对长度2的四次平面Cremona映射进行分类，并计算其普通二次长度，证明该上界为7，同时分类达到该上界的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四次平面Cremona映射可依homaloidal type分为de Jonquières型与非de Jonquières型，且依Blanc-Furter定义的"长度"分别为1和2。长度2（非de Jonquières型）一类尚缺乏在自同构群作用下的完整分类，作者希望填补这一空白，并进一步计算其普通二次长度以刻画复杂度上界。
