Method: 以homaloidal type区分四次平面Cremona映射的两类；对长度2（非de Jonquières型）映射，在射影平面自同构于左右两侧作用（即双有理等价意义）下进行等价分类；进而利用该分类计算所有此类映射的普通二次长度，并确定其最大值及取得该上界的映射。

Result: 完成了四次平面Cremona映射中长度2（非de Jonquières）类型在射影平面自同构左右作用下的分类；计算了所有此类映射的普通二次长度，证明其普通二次长度至多为7，并对达到上界7的映射进行了分类。

Conclusion: 长度2的四次平面Cremona映射可在自同构左右作用下完全分类，其普通二次长度有上界7，且取得该最大值的映射可被明确刻画，从而加深了对四次Cremona映射复杂度的理解。

Abstract: Plane Cremona maps of degree four are divided into two different classes according to their homaloidal type: either they are de Jonquières, that is, they have a base point of multiplicity 3, or they are not de Jonquières. The maps of the former class have length one, while the maps of the latter class have length two, according to the definition of length of a plane Cremona map given by Blanc and Furter.
  In this paper, we classify plane Cremona maps of degree four and length two, up to the action of automorphisms of the projective plane on the left-hand side and on the right-hand side of the map. This classification also allows us to compute the ordinary quadratic length of all such maps. In particular, we show that this ordinary quadratic length is at most 7 and we classify those maps which attain this upper bound.

</details>


### [372] [Geometry of a semistable degeneration of moduli of vector bundles on curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08589)
*Sourav Das*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了含一个节点的不可约曲线 $X_0$ 上稳定秩2、次数1向量丛的 Gieseker 模空间 $\mathcal{M}_{X_0}$，给出其正规化为广义抛物丛模空间的两次爆破并通过边界对合粘合恢复原空间，进而计算其 Picard 群与虚拟 Poincaré 多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇点曲线上的向量丛模空间（尤其是一个节点情形）的几何与上同调结构尚需明晰，本文旨在揭示该模空间与其正规化、规范化模空间之间的构造关系，并计算其基本不变量。

Method: 利用正规化 $\tilde X_0$ 上的广义抛物丛模空间，构造 $\mathcal{M}_{X_0}$ 的正规化为两次爆破，并通过边界上的自然对合粘合恢复原空间；采用 Harder–Narasimhan 分层计算虚拟 Poincaré 多项式；借助正规化态射是小态射的性质联系交叉上同调与普通上同调。

Result: 计算得到 $\operatorname{Pic}(\mathcal{M}_{X_0})$ 以及通过 HN 分层得到的虚拟 Poincaré 多项式；由于正规化态射为小态射，交叉上同调等于光滑正规化的普通上同调，从而给出以算术亏格表示的闭公式。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{M}_{X_0}$ 可由 $\tilde X_0$ 上广义抛物丛模空间的两次爆破经边界对合粘合得到，其 Picard 群与（交叉/普通）上同调均获显式描述，闭公式依赖于算术亏格。

Abstract: We study the Gieseker moduli space $\mathcal{M}_{X_0}$ of stable rank-$2$, degree-$1$ vector bundles on an irreducible curve $X_0$ with one node. Its normalization is a two-step blow-up of the moduli space of generalized parabolic bundles on the normalization $\tilde X_0$, and $\mathcal{M}_{X_0}$ is recovered by gluing along a natural involution on the boundary. From this we compute $\operatorname{Pic}(\mathcal{M}_{X_0})$ and, via a Harder--Narasimhan stratification, the virtual Poincaré polynomial of $\mathcal{M}_{X_0}$. Since the normalization morphism is small, the intersection cohomology of $\mathcal{M}_{X_0}$ equals the ordinary cohomology of the smooth normalization, which gives a closed formula in terms of the arithmetic genus.

</details>


### [373] [Xiao's Degree-Four Fibration and the Polizzi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08655)
*Anar Akhmedov,Sümeyra Sakallı*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文从Polizzi的分支模型与Xiao的十三类奇异纤维分类出发，系统研究四次族曲面$S_E\subset E(3)$的局部球面辫与Picard–Lefschetz提升，并给出$\pi_1$、Albanese核及模群cusp对应等结果，同时用三次单值群构造奇异$\mathbb CP^2\#7\overline{\mathbb CP}^{\,2}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Xiao四次族中的曲面$S_E$被Polizzi识别为$E(3)$中的光滑除子，但其局部单值结构（球面辫、Picard–Lefschetz变换）、双截线的归一化与二重覆盖结构、基本群与Albanese核、以及单值变换与模群cusp的联系尚未被完整刻画；此外，三次单值群在奇异四维拓扑构造上的应用也值得挖掘。

Method: 以Polizzi分支模型和Xiao十三类奇异纤维分类为起点，推导局部球面辫及其Picard–Lefschetz提升（含七个可约纤维处的染色$3+3$分划）；将每条双截线的归一化识别为$E/\{\pm1\}$，并把其二重ruling映射等同于由非零2-挠点平移所诱导对合的商；计算基本群与Albanese核，分析六类非分离Picard–Lefschetz变换与$\Gamma(4)$六cusp的对应；通过环面手术考察椭圆截面附近自然乘积环面的同调性质。

Result: 得到局部球面辫与Picard–Lefschetz提升的显式描述；双截线归一化为$E/\{\pm1\}$且其二重ruling为2-挠平移对合的商；证明$\pi_1(S_E)\cong\mathbb Z^2$；计算正则亏格二纤维的初等除子$4$的Albanese核；证明六个非分离Picard–Lefschetz变换对应$\Gamma(4)$的六个cusp；证明椭圆截面附近自然乘积环面零同调，且环面手术不能将$b_1$降至$2$以下；作为三次单值群的独立应用，构造出奇异$\mathbb CP^2\#7\overline{\mathbb CP}^{\,2}$。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了Xiao四次族曲面$S_E$的局部与整体单值结构，揭示其与$\Gamma(4)$模几何的精确对应，并给出环面手术的拓扑限制；同时展示了三次单值群在构造奇异四维流形中的独立价值。

Abstract: Let $S_E$ be the surface in Xiao's degree-four family identified by Polizzi as a smooth divisor in $E(3)$. Starting from Polizzi's branch model and Xiao's classification of the thirteen singular fibers, we derive the local spherical braids and their Picard--Lefschetz lifts, including the colored $3+3$ partitions at the seven reducible fibers. We identify the normalization of each bisection with $E/\{\pm1\}$ and its degree-two ruling map with the quotient by the involution induced by translation by a nonzero two-torsion point. We prove $π_1(S_E)\cong\mathbb Z^2$, compute the elementary-divisor-$4$ Albanese kernel of a regular genus-two fiber, and show that the six nonseparating Picard--Lefschetz transformations represent the six cusps of $Γ(4)$. We also prove that the natural product tori near an elliptic section are nullhomologous and cannot lower $b_1$ below $2$ by torus surgery. As a separate application of the degree-three monodromy, we give a twisted-double construction of an exotic $\mathbb CP^2\#7\overline{\mathbb CP}^{\,2}$.

</details>


### [374] [The unbearable projectivity of being a good moduli space of Gorenstein curves of genus one with non-colliding markings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08742)
*Luca Battistella,Andrea Di Lorenzo,Michele Pernice*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 分类了 $\mathcal{G}_{1,n}$ 中所有容许 proper good moduli space 的开子叠，并给出其好模空间投影性的数值判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画 log-canonically polarized 亏格1 Gorenstein 曲线叠 $\mathcal{G}_{1,n}$ 中具有 proper good moduli space 的开子叠结构，并判断对应好模空间何时投影。

Method: 通过构造性对应，证明此类开子叠恰为 Bozlee–Kuo–Neff 定义的 $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{1,n}(c)$；对 $c$ 为 level $m$ 的 pure 情形，建立好模空间投影性的数值判据。

Result: 所讨论的好模空间在 $n\to\infty$ 时渐近几乎从不投影。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{G}_{1,n}$ 中容许 proper good moduli space 的开子叠被完全分类为 $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{1,n}(c)$；其好模空间的投影性极为罕见，随 $n$ 增大渐近几乎不成立。

Abstract: We classify all open substacks admitting a proper good moduli space inside the stack $\mathcal{G}_{1,n}$ of log-canonically polarized Gorenstein curves of genus one with $n$ distinct marked points: they are exactly the stacks $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{1,n}(c)$ defined by Bozlee--Kuo--Neff. Restricting to the case where $c$ is pure of level $m$, we then give a numerical criterion for the projectivity of the good moduli spaces: these turn out to be asymptotically almost never projective as $n$ tends to infinity.

</details>


### [375] [The Kuranishi space of joint deformations of Calabi--Yau manifolds and holomorphic vector bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08776)
*Runze Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究复流形与全纯向量丛配对$(X,E)$的联合形变，证明在Fujiki流形与挠典范丛及$H^2(X,\mathrm{End}^0E)=0$条件下形变无阻碍，并构造非Kähler例子及有阻碍的配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Li--Pan与Iacono--Manetti关于流形形变无阻碍的定理推广到非Kähler情形下的配对$(X,E)$联合形变，并回答Felten提出的两个问题。

Method: 在$X$为Fujiki流形、典范丛挠且$H^2(X,\mathrm{End}^0E)=0$（$\mathrm{End}^0E$为无迹自同态丛）时证明配对形变无阻碍；典范丛平凡时用三个弱$\partial\bar\partial$条件替代Fujiki假设；并构造Frölicher谱序列在$E_1$不退化、满足条件的例子。

Result: 得到配对形变无阻碍的充分条件；当去掉消没假设时，既有无阻碍也有有阻碍的配对。在Thomas例子中证明配对无阻碍，而固定流形时丛的形变有阻碍；证明任意维数至少三的严格射影Calabi--Yau流形上都存在具有阻碍联合形变的简单丛；在复环面上构造Hermitian平坦丛的有阻碍配对。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Li--Pan与Iacono--Manetti定理的非Kähler推广，并回答了Felten的两个问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study joint deformations of pairs $(X,E)$, where $X$ is a compact complex manifold and $E\rightarrow X$ is a holomorphic vector bundle. We prove that $(X,E)$ has unobstructed deformations if $X$ is a Fujiki manifold with torsion canonical bundle and $H^2(X,\textrm{End}^0E)=0$, where $\textrm{End}^0 E$ denotes the trace-free endomorphism bundle. When the canonical bundle is trivial, the Fujiki assumption can be replaced by three weak $\partial\bar\partial$-conditions. We construct examples satisfying these conditions whose Frölicher spectral sequences do not degenerate at $E_1$. These results provide non-Kähler extensions of the theorems of Li--Pan and Iacono--Manetti.
  Without this vanishing assumption, we obtain both unobstructed and obstructed pairs. In Thomas's example, we prove unobstructedness of the pair, although the bundle has obstructed deformations with the manifold fixed. On the other hand, we prove that every strict projective Calabi--Yau manifold of dimension at least three admits a simple bundle with obstructed joint deformations. We also construct obstructed pairs with Hermitian flat bundles on complex tori. These results answer two questions raised by Felten.

</details>
